### [1] [MechReason: Benchmarking Multi-Image Multi-Hop Reasoning in Mechanical Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16012)
*Tengyue Wang,Kang An,Chenxu Du,Zhongyu Yang,Yuanchi Zhu,Xinqi Yang,Hebao Zhu,Ziliang Wang,FaQiang Qian,Yunli Yang,Qibing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MechReason基准，基于真实机械工程论文构建12k问答对与21k多模态素材，覆盖四类推理维度与八种任务类型，用于评估多模态大模型在机械工程复杂推理上的能力，实验显示最优模型准确率仅62.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态评测基准多聚焦图纸识别、CAD解读或单图表查询等基础任务，无法评估模型在面对真实复杂机械工程问题时，整合多图像、文本条件、物理原理与工程约束进行多步推理的能力。

Method: 构建MechReason基准：数据源自真实机械工程论文，包含12k带显式推理链标注的问答对与21k视觉素材（涵盖统计图表、参数表、工程图纸、显微图像、仿真图像等九种证据类型）；采用四阶段构建流程——提取核心工程论断并将支撑证据分解为前提、推理过程、结论与佐证；通过遮蔽后验验证信息生成防捷径问题；最后进行多模态质量校验以保证任务质量与多跳性。任务覆盖解释、预测、设计、诊断四个推理维度下的八类任务。

Result: 大量实验表明MechReason极具挑战性，即使最先进的模型也仅达到62.89%的准确率。

Conclusion: MechReason填补了机械工程领域复杂多模态推理评测的空白，其高难度特性可为后续多模态大模型在工程推理能力上的提升提供有效的评测支撑。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in general visual question answering and cross-modal understanding, multimodal large language models still face a pronounced gap in evaluation for complex reasoning within the mechanical engineering domain. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on rudimentary tasks such as drawing recognition, CAD interpretation, or single-chart querying, falling short of assessing whether models can integrate multiple images, textual conditions, physical principles, and engineering constraints to perform multi-step reasoning when confronted with authentic, intricate mechanical problems. To address this, we introduce MechReason, a benchmark derived from real mechanical engineering papers, comprising 12k question-answer pairs with explicit reasoning-chain annotations and 21k visual materials spanning nine evidence types, including statistical charts, parameter tables, engineering drawings, microscopic images, simulation images, system architectures, real mechanical scene photos, CAD model images and manufacturing flowcharts. MechReason covers eight task types across four reasoning dimensions: explanation, prediction, design, and diagnosis. We devise a four-stage construction pipeline: we first extract core engineering claims and decompose their supporting evidence into premises, reasoning processes, conclusions, and corroborative evidence; we then generate shortcut-preventing questions by masking posterior verification information; finally, we apply multimodal quality validation to ensure task quality and multi-hop nature. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that MechReason is highly challenging, with even the most advanced models achieving only 62.89\% accuracy.

</details>


### [2] [DenseFace: Bias Mitigation in Face Recognition via Density-Aware Probabilistic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16149)
*Mansur Bultygov,Vadim Seliutin,Dmitry Nekhaev,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DenseFace方法，通过von Mises-Fisher分布建模人脸嵌入，在不牺牲识别精度和无需重训练的前提下减少预训练人脸识别模型中的种族偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人脸识别模型存在明显的群体偏见，而现有去偏方法往往对训练过程施加限制，并导致识别精度下降。

Method: 对每个人的面部嵌入用von Mises-Fisher (MF) 分布建模，观察到人口统计属性与MF分布密度之间的依赖关系，进而提出DenseFace——一种考虑MF分布差异的概率人脸匹配流程。

Result: 大量实验表明，DenseFace在不同网络架构、训练数据集和损失函数的强人脸识别模型上均能持续减少种族偏见，同时保持识别精度，且无需重训练底层模型。

Conclusion: DenseFace可在不重训练、不损失精度的条件下有效缓解种族偏见，并对此前采用的偏见度量方法进行了探讨并给出建议。

Abstract: Despite steady progress in face recognition, current face recognition models still suffer from significant demographic biases. While approaches for bias mitigation have been proposed, existing methods often impose constraints on the training procedure and result in the degradation of recognition accuracy. To address this issue, we here introduce a method that reduces racial bias in pre-trained face recognition models without compromising their accuracy. To this end, we model face embeddings of each person by von Mises-Fisher (MF) distribution. We next observe the dependency between demographic attributes and the density of MF distributions, and propose DenseFace, a probabilistic face matching procedure that accounts for differences in MF distributions. Our extensive experiments demonstrate DenseFace to consistently reduce racial bias in strong face recognition models varying in network architectures, training datasets and loss functions. Notably, DenseFace preserves recognition accuracy and requires no retraining of the underlying face recognition model. Our work also investigates previously adopted bias measures and makes suggestions.

</details>


### [3] [Hyperbolic Contrastive Learning with Entailment for Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16207)
*Daniela Vega,Paula Cárdenas,Hannah Ceballos,Leonardo Manrique,Pablo Arbelaéz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyCLoST，一种基于双曲几何的对比学习模型，通过基因-图像蕴含损失捕捉空间转录组数据中的层次结构，从而提升从病理图像预测基因表达的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组（ST）能实现组织切片上基因表达的空间映射，但成本高、设备专业要求高、对实验噪声敏感，限制了其可扩展性。近年计算机视觉方法尝试直接从病理图像预测基因表达，但现有方法存在基因表达过度平滑、组织区域预测过于均匀的问题。作者认为进一步突破需要学习能够反映基因调控和组织形态层次性与非对称结构的表征。

Method: 提出HyCLoST（Hyperbolic Contrastive Learning with Entailment for Spatial Transcriptomics），一种双曲对比学习模型：利用双曲几何建模ST数据内在的层次关系，并引入基因到图像的蕴含损失（gene-to-image entailment loss），学习结构化且具有生物学依据的表征。

Result: 在26个ST数据集上，HyCLoST相比此前方法实现了MSE降低6%、PCC提升8%的效果，改善了基因表达预测精度。

Conclusion: 双曲几何结合蕴含式对比学习能够有效缓解基因表达过度平滑问题，学习反映层级结构的表征，提升从病理图像预测空间基因表达的精度。源代码已公开于 https://github.com/BCV-Uniandes/HyCLoST。

Abstract: Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) has transformed biomedical research by enabling the spatial mapping of gene expression across tissue sections. However, high operational costs, specialized equipment requirements, and sensitivity to experimental noise limit the accessibility and scalability of ST. Recent computer vision approaches aim to overcome these limitations by predicting spatial gene expression directly from histopathology images. While effective, current approaches often suffer from gene expression over-smoothing and overly uniform predictions across tissue regions, suggesting that further progress depends on learning representations that reflect the hierarchical and asymmetric structure of gene regulation and tissue morphology. To address these issues, we propose Hyperbolic Contrastive Learning with Entailment for Spatial Transcriptomics (HyCLoST), a hyperbolic contrastive learning model that captures the intrinsic hierarchical relationships within ST data. By leveraging hyperbolic geometry and a gene-to-image entailment loss, HyCLoST learns structured, biologically grounded representations that improve gene expression prediction accuracy, achieving a 6% reduction in MSE and an 8% increase in PCC across 26 ST datasets, over previous methods. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/BCV-Uniandes/HyCLoST

</details>


### [4] [SceneBench: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Vision-Language Understanding of 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16233)
*Anubhav Khanal,Prabigya Acharya,Roshni Poudel,Sujan Kapali,Bigyan Bhatta,Pramish Paudel,Francois Rameau,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SceneBench，一个基于高斯溅射重建的966个逼真3D场景基准，具有跨场景、房间、功能区、物体组和个体对象的层级语义标注，并定义了三类空间推理评估任务，揭示现有模型在层级与组合推理上的明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在2D图像理解上表现出色，但3D空间推理能力有限。现有3D基准存在三点不足：依赖点云而丢失纹理、文本、材质等视觉特征；标注孤立处理对象，忽略真实世界的层级组织；评估任务偏重基础识别，缺乏多步空间推理。

Method: 构建SceneBench基准，包含966个用高斯溅射重建的逼真3D场景，并通过人机协同流程（视觉语言模型结合约1500小时人工迭代精修与验证）进行密集层级语义标注，覆盖场景、房间、功能区、物体组和个体对象，产出超过183K个带文本描述和3D边界框的标注节点。基于该表示定义三类任务：存在性问答、空间智能问答（计数、尺寸比较、距离、方向关系）以及需要跨语义层级多步推理的Grounded QRA三元组。

Result: 对最先进视觉语言模型的实验表明，模型在基础识别任务上表现良好（如检测准确率最高约85%），但在层级和组合推理上性能大幅下降（如计数降至约60%），暴露出既有基准未能捕捉的局限。

Conclusion: SceneBench为在逼真3D环境中开发与评估具备细粒度空间推理能力的模型提供了现实的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel at 2D image understanding but remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning. Progress is hindered by limitations in current benchmarks. First, 3D datasets often rely on point clouds that capture geometry but discard rich visual features like texture, text, and materials. Second, annotations treat objects in isolation while ignoring real-world hierarchical organization (scenes, rooms, functional areas, object groups). Third, evaluation tasks focus narrowly on basic recognition rather than multi-step spatial reasoning.
  In this context, we introduce SceneBench, a benchmark of 966 photorealistic 3D scenes reconstructed with Gaussian Splatting and densely annotated with hierarchical semantics spanning scenes, rooms, functional areas, object groups, and individual objects. These annotations are produced through a human-in-the-loop pipeline combining vision-language models with roughly 1,500 human-hours of iterative refinement and verification, producing over 183K annotated nodes with textual descriptions and 3D bounding boxes. Building on this representation, we define three evaluation tasks: Existence-Based Questions probing object attributes, Spatial Intelligence Questions covering counting, size comparison, distance, and directional relations, and Grounded Question-Reasoning-Answer (QRA) triplets requiring multi-step reasoning across semantic levels. Experiments with state-of-the-art vision-language models show that while models perform well on basic recognition tasks (e.g., up to 85% accuracy for detection), performance drops substantially on hierarchical and compositional reasoning (e.g., down to 60% for counting), revealing limitations not captured by existing benchmarks. SceneBench provides a realistic testbed for developing and evaluating models capable of fine-grained spatial reasoning in photorealistic 3D environments.

</details>


### [5] [ProtoLIP: From Sentence-Level to Object-Level Evidence Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16284)
*Yan Zhu,Yongbo Chen,Zhengming Ding,Rebecca Faust*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProtoLIP，一种轻量级原型中介证据层，通过文本衍生的语义家族与查询相关的家族路由来约束证据来源，从而改善 VLM 的证据定位与分离能力，并实现匹配分数的可解释分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询条件下的视觉-语言模型虽能揭示视觉证据如何随文本查询变化，但基于完整描述得到的证据未必能解析为特定对象的证据，且暴露的证据图也未必对应构成模型预测的证据。作者发现对象级查询常保留共现对象与共享上下文的证据，因此需要一种机制来约束证据来源。

Method: 提出 ProtoLIP：一个轻量级原型中介证据层，将可复用的视觉原型组织为文本衍生的语义家族，并采用查询相关的家族路由来限制哪些原型可以提供证据。方法无需空间标注，也无需重新训练骨干网络，仅使用文本衍生的弱监督。

Result: 在多种 VLM 架构与独立基准上，ProtoLIP 在跨查询粒度上提升了证据定位与分离能力；定位增益可迁移到具有良好对齐的 patch–text 表示的独立预训练 VLM。尽管仅用文本弱监督，仍可与空间监督的 grounding 模型竞争，并保持较强的匹配与图像-文本检索性能。

Conclusion: ProtoLIP 直接从局部化原型证据构建匹配分数，使该分数可精确分解为语义家族与原型贡献，从而在提升证据定位与分离的同时增强了模型预测的可解释性。

Abstract: Query-conditioned vision--language models enable fine-grained interpretation by revealing how visual evidence changes with textual queries. However, evidence conditioned on complete descriptions does not necessarily resolve into object-specific evidence, nor does an exposed evidence map necessarily identify the evidence that constitutes the model's prediction. Across multiple VLM architectures and independent benchmarks, we find that object-level queries often retain evidence from co-occurring objects and shared context. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{ProtoLIP}, a lightweight prototype-mediated evidence layer that organizes reusable visual prototypes into text-derived semantic families and uses query-dependent family routing to constrain which prototypes may provide evidence. Without spatial annotations or backbone retraining, ProtoLIP improves evidence localization and separation across query granularities, with localization gains transferring to independently pretrained VLMs with well-aligned patch--text representations. Despite using only text-derived weak supervision, ProtoLIP remains competitive with a spatially supervised grounding model while maintaining strong matching and competitive image--text retrieval. Crucially, ProtoLIP constructs its matching score directly from localized prototype evidence, enabling the score to be exactly decomposed into semantic-family and prototype contributions.

</details>


### [6] [Sequence Recognition in Bharatnatyam dance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16306)
*Himadri Bhuyan,Rohit Dhaipule,Partha Pratim Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种识别Bharatanatyam舞蹈中Adavu动作序列的方法，先用CNN识别关键姿态、SVM识别动作，再用编辑距离算法与真值比对匹配序列，取得高准确率，并具备数字化传承与舞蹈教学等应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bharatanatyam作为最古老的印度古典舞，其Adavu包含15种动作及58种变体，每种变体由特定顺序的动作与姿态构成，学生不仅需学习舞步还需掌握其出现顺序。然而以往研究多仅基于关键姿态识别，未充分考虑动作与序列信息，且仅使用一两个变体，缺乏对序列识别与可扩展性的系统研究。

Method: 作者首先使用卷积神经网络（CNN）识别Adavu中的关键姿态（KPs），使用支持向量机（SVM）识别动作（motions）；随后通过编辑距离（Edit Distance）算法将识别出的关键姿态与动作序列与真值序列进行比对，以找到最佳匹配。

Result: CNN对关键姿态的识别准确率达到99%，SVM对动作的识别准确率为84%；编辑距离算法进行序列匹配的准确率达98%。此外，论文还通过分析每条序列的预测时间评估性能，并与处理相同问题的先前工作进行对比，同时通过纳入所有Adavu变体测试方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该研究在数字遗产保护和舞蹈教学系统等方面对现有技术作出重要贡献，具有三项创新：（a）基于关键姿态与动作的联合序列识别，而非仅依赖关键姿态；（b）以每条序列预测时间衡量性能并与先前工作对比；（c）涵盖所有Adavu变体以验证可扩展性，克服了以往仅使用一两个变体的局限。

Abstract: Bharatanatyam is the oldest Indian Classical Dance (ICD) which is learned and practiced across India and the world. Adavu is the core of this dance form. There exist 15 Adavus and 58 variations. Each Adavu variation comprises a well-defined set of motions and postures (called dance steps) that occur in a particular order. So, while learning Adavus, students not only learn the dance steps but also take care of its sequence of occurrences. This paper proposed a method to recognize these sequences. In this work, firstly, we recognize the involved Key Postures (KPs) and motions in the Adavu using Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and Support Vector Machine (SVM), respectively. In this, CNN achieves 99% and SVM's recognition accuracy becomes 84%. Next, we compare these KP and motion sequences with the ground truth to find the best match using the Edit Distance algorithm with an accuracy of 98%. The paper contributes hugely to the state-of-the-art in the form of digital heritage, dance tutoring system, and many more. The paper addresses three novelties; (a) Recognizing the sequences based on the KPs and motions rather than only KPs as reported in the earlier works. (b) The performance of the proposed work is measured by analyzing the prediction time per sequence. We also compare our proposed approach with the previous works that deal with the same problem statement. (c) It tests the scalability of the proposed approach by including all the Adavu variations, unlike the earlier literature, which uses only one/two variations.

</details>


### [7] [Racing in Volume with Flow Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16310)
*Saswat Subhajyoti Mallick,Riu Cherdchusakulchai,Marc Ruiz Olle,Albert Mosella-Montoro,Jose Ribeiro-Gomes,Francisco Vicente Carrasco,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FastFlowGS流式4D高斯泼溅方法与Monaco4D高速户外重建基准，实现稀疏固定外置相机对高速运动目标的实时4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式4D重建仅限于室内密集相机阵列拍摄常速运动主体；户外4D重建或依赖车载相机，或用覆盖有限的阵列离线处理准静态主体。观众真正关心的"快速运动主体+稀疏环状环绕相机+流式"场景尚无方法和基准。

Method: FastFlowGS融合稀疏匹配、半稠密轨迹与稠密光流三种信号，将每种信号以几何不确定性提升到3D，并通过Kalman式时序更新进行组合。同时构建Monaco4D基准，基于Unreal Engine 5，提供F1赛道多光照条件下的赛道边、车载、无人机视角序列及稠密真值。

Result: 在CMU-Panoptic上，FastFlowGS相比最强基线VMAF提升12.6%，效率提升35%。在Monaco4D上，现有流式方法严重退化，而FastFlowGS动态区域PSNR最高提升18.6%，逐帧优化时间降低28.3%。

Conclusion: FastFlowGS与Monaco4D填补了稀疏固定相机下高速户外流式4D重建的方法与基准空白，在质量与效率上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Streaming 4D reconstruction has been demonstrated only indoors, on dense camera rigs surrounding subjects that move at human pace. Outdoor 4D reconstruction exists but relies either on cameras mounted on the moving vehicle itself, or on limited-coverage arrays observing quasi-static subjects offline. The case that actually matters for spectators is a fast-moving subject, watched from a sparse ring of allocentric cameras, streaming. No method targets this, and no benchmark exists to evaluate one. To this end, we introduce FastFlowGS, a streaming 4D Gaussian Splatting method for reconstructing fast-moving subjects from a small set of fixed external cameras, and Monaco4D, a photorealistic Unreal Engine 5 benchmark for high-speed outdoor reconstruction. FastFlowGS fuses sparse matches, semi-dense tracks, and dense optical flow by lifting each signal to 3D with geometric uncertainty and combining them through a Kalman-style temporal update. Monaco4D provides Formula 1 sequences under varied illumination from trackside, onboard, and drone viewpoints with dense ground truth. On CMU-Panoptic, FastFlowGS exceeds the strongest baseline by 12.6% VMAF at 35% greater efficiency. On Monaco4D, where existing streaming methods degrade severely, it improves dynamic-region PSNR by up to 18.6% with 28.3% lower per-frame optimization time. Dataset and additional details can be found at https://humansensinglab.github.io/monaco4d/.

</details>


### [8] [Reasoning with Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16409)
*Nishad Singhi,Hector Garcia Rodriguez,Aditya Arora,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReImaGin，用图像生成模型作为多模态大模型的灵活视觉推理机制，在六项视觉推理任务上超越纯文本推理和专用视觉工具基线，最高提升 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理虽提升了大语言模型的问题分解能力，但局限于文本域，难以直接操作视觉表征。现有做法给多模态大模型外接深度估计、目标检测等视觉专家工具，但这些工具依赖狭窄、固定的操作，无法灵活生成或变换视觉内容。

Method: 将图像生成模型作为多模态大模型的视觉推理机制：不同于固定功能工具，它能接受自然语言指令，执行开放式视觉操作，例如移除遮挡物，或从房间的多个不相交视角生成平面图。

Result: 在包括多视角空间推理、碰撞预测在内的六项多样化视觉推理任务上，ReImaGin 一致优于纯文本推理和专用视觉工具基线，性能提升最高达 25%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了灵活、生成式视觉推理的优势，为多模态大模型提供了一种更具通用性的视觉推理范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has revolutionized natural language processing by enabling large language models (LLMs) to decompose problems into intermediate steps before answering. Yet confining reasoning to the textual domain presents limitations for tasks requiring direct manipulation of visual representations. Recent efforts augment multimodal LLMs with external visual expert tools such as depth estimation or object detection modules, but these remain fundamentally limited by their reliance on narrow, rigid operations that cannot flexibly generate or transform visual content. We propose ReImaGin, which leverages image generation models as a flexible visual reasoning mechanism for multimodal LLMs: unlike fixed-function tools, they accept natural language commands and can perform open-ended visual operations, like removing an occlusion or generating a floorplan from multiple disjoint views of a room. Across six diverse visual reasoning tasks including multi-view spatial reasoning and collision prediction, ReImaGin consistently outperforms both text-only reasoning and specialist vision-tool baselines, with gains of up to 25\%, demonstrating the advantage of flexible, generative visual reasoning.

</details>


### [9] [Decentralized Gossip Learning and Federated Averaging for Histopathology Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16448)
*Yusuf Ozturk,Enes Goltekin,Bengisu Atli,Akin Ozturk,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了FedAvg、全去中心化gossip learning及Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg在乳腺浸润性导管癌(IDC)图像块分类上的表现，发现三者总体性能接近，FedAvg最稳定可靠，gossip为可行的去中心化替代方案，Hybrid则在点对点扩散与周期性全局协调之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺组织病理分析中，跨机构直接汇总数据常受隐私、治理和通信限制，因此分布式学习日益重要，有必要系统比较中心化联邦学习与去中心化gossip方案的性能与鲁棒性。

Method: 使用277,524张彩色图像块，按患者不重叠划分训练/验证/测试集，并以工作负载均衡、Dirichlet引导的方式分配到六个节点；比较基于服务器的FedAvg、全去中心化gossip learning和Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg，评估环形、随机度3和全连接gossip拓扑，并开展统计异质性、混合系数、学习率、模型漂移、预测分歧、校准、临床操作点、通信负载及患者级IDC负担等敏感性分析。

Result: 在alpha=0.3的主实验中，Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg测试ROC-AUC为0.8811，FedAvg为0.8801，全连接gossip为0.8751。三次独立患者级重复中，FedAvg与Hybrid平均ROC-AUC均为0.9082，标准差分别为0.0037和0.0043；Hybrid平均PR-AUC最高(0.8240)，FedAvg平均Brier分数最低(0.1335)。更密集的gossip图提升判别力但增加理论模型负载，环形gossip对学习率和混合强度较敏感。

Conclusion: FedAvg提供了最一致可靠的基于服务器的基线，拓扑感知的gossip是可用的去中心化替代方案，而Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg在点对点扩散与周期性全局协调之间实现了均衡折中。

Abstract: Breast histopathology analysis increasingly relies on distributed learning because direct data pooling across institutions is often restricted by privacy, governance, and communication constraints. This study compares server-based Federated Averaging (FedAvg), fully decentralized gossip learning, and Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg for invasive ductal carcinoma (IDC) patch classification. Experiments used 277,524 color image patches with patient-disjoint training, validation, and test partitions and a workload-balanced, Dirichlet-guided allocation across six nodes. Ring, random degree-3, and fully connected gossip topologies were evaluated together with sensitivity analyses for statistical heterogeneity, mixing coefficient, learning rate, model drift, prediction disagreement, calibration, clinically motivated operating points, communication payload, and patient-level IDC burden, together with auxiliary backbone robustness analyses. In the principal alpha=0.3 experiment, Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg achieved a test area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.8811, closely followed by FedAvg at 0.8801 and fully connected gossip at 0.8751. Across three independent patient-level repetitions, FedAvg and Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg obtained the same mean ROC-AUC of 0.9082, with standard deviations of 0.0037 and 0.0043, respectively. Hybrid achieved the highest mean area under the precision-recall curve of 0.8240, whereas FedAvg produced the lowest mean Brier score of 0.1335. Denser gossip graphs improved discrimination but increased theoretical model payload, while ring gossip remained sensitive to learning rate and mixing strength. Overall, FedAvg provided the most consistently reliable server-based baseline, topology-aware gossip offered a viable decentralized alternative, and Hybrid Gossip-FedAvg provided a balanced compromise between peer-to-peer diffusion and periodic global coordination.

</details>


### [10] [A multimodal large language model for evidence-based autism spectrum disorder screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16464)
*Jun Chen,Qi Zhao,Yunliang Jiang,Shuqin Cao,Yunqiang Lin,Chenglong Jia,Qiang Guo,Guang Dai,Xiongtao Zhang,Mengmeng Wang,Xiaoyue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASDchat：面向孤独症谱系障碍（ASD）循证筛查的多模态大语言模型，以视频、音频与对话为输入，采用"决策分支+证据分支"双分支架构，在1,035名中国被试上验证，ASD vs TD 的 AUC 达 0.953±0.021，9个外部站点平均 AUC 0.932，并可将 ASD 聚类为六个亚型并给出干预建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD 临床管理的瓶颈在于早期筛查：受过训练的专业人员稀缺，且传统评估工具带有主观性，难以支撑大规模、标准化的早期筛查需求。

Method: 提出 ASDchat 多模态大语言模型，输入为视频、音频与对话。采用双分支架构：决策分支输出筛查概率，证据分支生成可追溯、带时间戳的行为证据，且与标准化临床标准（ADOS-2）对齐。训练与评估使用来自中国27个站点的1,035名被试数据，涵盖典型发育（TD）儿童、ASD 儿童及其他障碍儿童；并在9个未参与训练的留出站点上做外部验证。此外对行为维度进行无监督聚类以划分亚型。

Result: ASD vs TD 场景下 AUC 为 0.953±0.021；在9个未用于训练的留出站点上平均 AUC 为 0.932。无监督聚类将 ASD 病例划分为六个表型特征各异的亚型，并针对每个亚型给出干预建议。

Conclusion: ASDchat 为临床实践中大规模、循证化的 ASD 早期筛查提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The clinical management of autism spectrum disorder (ASD) faces a bottleneck in early screening, mainly because trained specialists are scarce and conventional assessment tools are subjective. Here, we introduce ASDchat, a multimodal large language model designed for evidence-based ASD screening, which takes video, audio, and dialogue as input. ASDchat adopts a dual-branch architecture, where the decision branch generates screening probabilities and the evidence branch generates traceable, timestamped behavioral evidence aligned with standardized clinical criteria (ADOS-2). The model was trained and evaluated on a dataset of 1,035 participants from 27 sites in China, which covered typically developing (TD) children, children with ASD, and children with other disorders. For ASD versus TD, ASDchat reached an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.953 $\pm$ 0.021. On 9 held-out sites that were not used for training, the mean AUC was 0.932. Furthermore, unsupervised clustering of the behavioral dimensions split the ASD cases into six subtypes with different phenotypic profiles, and ASDchat suggests an intervention for each subtype. ASDchat provides a feasible path for large-scale, evidence-based early ASD screening in clinical practice.

</details>


### [11] [MDN-Control: Mask-Depth-Noise Guided Region Control for Multi-Subject Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16475)
*Jiayi Yu,Xi Ye,Lina Wang,Yunkun Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 MDN-Control，通过掩码定位、深度感知遮挡控制与噪声潜变量提示，实现多主体视频编辑的一致生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体视频编辑需在修改指定主体的同时保留非目标内容，但面临跨主体属性泄漏与遮挡边界模糊问题。现有方法依赖掩码，难以区分重叠主体，也难以保证生成一致性。

Method: 提出免训练框架 MDN-Control，联合控制目标定位、遮挡几何与外观初始化：掩码引导的定位提供一致的目标定位；深度感知遮挡控制解决重叠主体间边界模糊；并引入噪声潜变量提示，从噪声库中检索高斯初始化以获取与提示相关的先验。

Result: 在 MSVBench 上，MDN-Control 取得最低的 CM-Err 与最高的 Q-Edit，同时保持具竞争力的文本对齐与时序一致性。

Conclusion: 将空间、几何与潜变量先验相结合，对多主体视频编辑是有效的。

Abstract: Multi subject video editing modifies designated subjects while preserving non target content, but faces cross subject attribute leakage, and occlusion ambiguity. Existing approaches rely on masks and struggle to distinguish overlapping subjects or ensure consistent generation. To address these limitations, we propose MDN-Control, a training free framework jointly controlling target localization, occlusion geometry, and appearance initialization. Specifically, mask-guided localization provides consistent target localization, while depth-aware occlusion control resolves ambiguous boundaries between overlapping subjects. We further introduce noise latent prompting, which retrieves Gaussian initializations from a noise library for prompt relevant priors. Experiments on MSVBench show that MDN-Control achieves the lowest CM-Err and the highest Q-Edit, while maintaining competitive text alignment and temporal consistency, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining spatial, geometric, and latent priors for multi subject video editing.

</details>


### [12] [VPRef: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16486)
*Quanwei Liu,Tao Huang,Jiaqi Yang,Wei Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨域遥感指代图像分割（RRSIS）中视觉域漂移与文本逻辑漂移的双重难题，构建了首个跨域基准VPRef，并提出基于SAM3+LoRA的参数高效域适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS模型在实际部署中面临耦合的双重漂移：一是跨空间分辨率不匹配与光谱差异导致的视觉域漂移，二是用户输入粒度不受约束且多变导致的文本逻辑漂移，造成性能严重下降。同时领域缺乏跨域RRSIS基准。

Method: 构建首个跨域RRSIS基准数据集VPRef，包含46,972个语言-图像-标注三元组，组织为三层语言层级。在此基础上，以SAM3为底座、通过LoRA构建参数高效域适应基线：用伪标签驱动的自训练缓解视觉分布差异，用随机多粒度文本提示混合应对文本逻辑漂移。

Result: 消融实验的指标分布表明跨模态语义鲁棒化与视觉域对齐可能存在解耦：语言方差驱动细粒度语义不变性，伪标签传播主导宏观尺度空间网格对齐。框架在仅修改基础参数1.08%的情况下取得了更优的跨域分割边界。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来多模态遥感域适应研究建立了稳健基线，数据集与代码将开源于 https://github.com/quanweiliu/VPRef。

Abstract: Rapid advancements in vision-language models have propelled Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) to the forefront of Earth observation. However, practical deployments suffer severe performance degradation under a coupled dual-drift paradigm: visual domain drift from cross-spatial-resolution mismatches and spectral variations, alongside textual logic drift from unconstrained, variable user-input granularities. To mitigate these bottlenecks, this paper establishes the first cross-domain RRSIS benchmark, designated as the Vaihingen-Potsdam Referring (VPRef) dataset, comprising 46,972 language-image-annotation triplets organized into a three-tier linguistic hierarchy. Building upon this benchmark, we develop a tailored parameter-efficient domain adaptation baseline anchored on the Segment Anything Model (SAM3) via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our framework counteracts visual distribution discrepancies through pseudo-label-driven self-training and addresses textual logic drift via random multi-granularity text prompt mixing. Crucially, the distribution of empirical metrics across ablative variants suggests a potential decoupling between cross-modal semantic robustification and visual domain alignment, demonstrating that linguistic variance drives fine-grained semantic invariance while pseudo-label propagation governs macro-scale spatial grid alignment. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate the proposed framework achieves superior cross-domain segmentation boundaries while modifying merely 1.08\% of the foundational parameter footprint, establishing a robust baseline for future multi-modal remote sensing domain adaptation research. The dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/quanweiliu/VPRef.

</details>


### [13] [Which Pretext Task Transfers? Self-Supervised Pretraining Objectives for Lung Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16551)
*Moein Heidari,Junbo Rao,Jai Choraria,Wenjin Chen,David J. Foran,Ilker Hacihaliloglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在相同骨干、预训练语料、优化流程与冻结评估协议下，系统比较了对比学习、掩码重建与JEPA三类自监督目标在肺部超声中的表现，发现POCUS上的探针准确率排名与跨数据集迁移排名出现反转，说明单一数据集评估不足以判断目标函数的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学标注数据稀缺，自监督学习可减少对标注医学图像的依赖，但肺部超声（LUS）中何种预训练目标最优尚不明确。对比学习、掩码重建与JEPA在目标定义空间上不同，然而既有超声研究在语料、骨干网络与评估协议上不一致，难以公平比较。

Method: 在统一编码器骨干、预训练语料（COVID-BLUeS LUS视频）、优化调度与冻结评估协议下，比较三类自监督目标。采用线性、kNN与注意力探针，在5%、10%、50%、100%标签预算下评估。在POCUS上使用患者级五折交叉验证，并在独立采集且完全排除于预训练与探针拟合之外的Mendeley-Uganda数据集上测试。

Result: 在100%标签预算、线性探针下，POCUS上VideoMAE与V-JEPA的平衡准确率分别为66.5±13.1与65.4±11.7，MoCo仅为42.1±1.2。而在Mendeley-Uganda上排名反转：MoCo最佳为62.7±1.0，VideoMAE为53.8±2.8，V-JEPA接近随机水平的35.1±4.9。

Conclusion: 仅凭POCUS上的探针准确率无法识别跨数据集迁移最优的自监督目标。作者计划进一步开展表征层面分析以解释该反转现象。代码已公开于指定GitHub仓库。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) can reduce the need for labelled medical images, but the choice of pretext objective remains unclear for lung ultrasound (LUS). Contrastive learning, masked reconstruction, and joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA) differ in the space in which their targets are defined, yet existing ultrasound studies compare them under different corpora, backbones, and evaluation protocols. We compare these three objective families using the same encoder backbone, pretraining corpus, optimisation schedule, and frozen-evaluation protocol. Encoders are pretrained on COVID-BLUeS LUS videos and evaluated with linear, $k$NN, and attentive probes at 5\%, 10\%, 50\%, and 100\% label budgets. Evaluation is performed on POCUS using patient-level five-fold cross-validation and on the independently acquired Mendeley-Uganda dataset, which is excluded from both pretraining and probe fitting. At the full label budget under linear probing, VideoMAE and V-JEPA achieve $66.5 \pm 13.1$ and $65.4 \pm 11.7$ balanced accuracy on POCUS, while MoCo achieves $42.1 \pm 1.2$. On Mendeley-Uganda, the ranking reverses: MoCo performs best at $62.7 \pm 1.0$, followed by VideoMAE at $53.8 \pm 2.8$, while V-JEPA falls near chance at $35.1 \pm 4.9$. These results show that POCUS probe accuracy alone does not identify the objective that transfers best across datasets. We also outline planned representation-level analyses to examine this reversal. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/moeinheidari7829/LUSVideoSSL.

</details>


### [14] [Vision And Text Transformer For Predicting Answerability On Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16565)
*Tung Le,Huy Tien Nguyen,Le Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VT-Transformer，将视觉问答中的可回答性（Answerability）建模为回归任务，利用Transformer融合视觉与文本特征进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将视觉问答系统的输出二值映射为可回答性，这种二分类方式无法反映问题本质，即图像与问题之间应存在连续的可回答程度。

Method: 将Answerability视为回归任务，提出VT-Transformer，采用Transformer架构同时提取并融合图像的视觉特征与问题的文本特征。

Result: 在VizWiz 2020数据集上进行实验，与多个具有竞争力的基线方法相比，VT-Transformer在Answerability预测上表现出有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将可回答性作为回归任务并借助Transformer进行多模态特征融合，是一种可行且有效的方案，能够更贴合视觉问答可回答性问题的本质。

Abstract: Answerability on Visual Question Answering is a novel and attractive task to predict answerable scores between images and questions in multi-modal data. Existing works often utilize a binary mapping from visual question answering systems into Answerability. It does not reflect the essence of this problem. Together with our consideration of Answerability in a regression task, we propose VT-Transformer, which exploits visual and textual features through Transformer architecture. Experimental results on VizWiz 2020 dataset show the effectiveness and robustness of VT-Transformer for Answerability on Visual Question Answering when comparing with competitive baselines.

</details>


### [15] [Counterfactual Reasoning for Robust Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16567)
*Truong-Binh Duong,Thanh-Ngan Tran,Ngoc-Thao Nguyen,Bac Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强反事实对比学习的VQA训练框架，通过三阶段课程、改进的批对比损失及两种新正则项（答案对比损失与梯度差异损失），在VQA-CP v2上取得61.64%准确率，同时VQA v2保持62.80%，泛化差距仅1.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VQA模型常利用训练数据中的虚假相关性，因语言偏差导致分布外（OOD）泛化能力差。已有反事实学习方法虽有前景，但在引导注意力关注因果证据、增强特征判别力方面仍可改进。

Method: 提出增强反事实对比学习的训练框架，含三项贡献：(1)用于稳定多目标优化的三阶段课程；(2)增强的批对比损失以学习更具判别力的特征；(3)两种新正则项——答案对比（AC）损失精炼预测空间，梯度差异（GD）损失强化因果视觉 grounding。

Result: 在偏差敏感的VQA-CP v2基准上达到61.64%的准确率，在标准VQA v2数据集上保持62.80%，泛化差距仅1.16%。

Conclusion: 该框架在分布外鲁棒性与分布内性能之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Modern Visual Question Answering (VQA) models often exploit spurious correlations in training data, leading to poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization due to language bias. Although counterfactual learning has shown promise, existing methods can be improved to better guide attention toward causal evidence and strengthen feature discrimination. To address this, we propose a novel training framework that enhances counterfactual contrastive learning for VQA. Our framework introduces three key contributions: (1) a three-stage curriculum for stable multi-objective optimization, (2) an enhanced Batch-Contrastive loss for more discriminative feature learning, and (3) two novel regularizers, Answer-Contrastive (AC) loss to refine the prediction space and Gradient-Discrepancy (GD) loss to enforce causal visual grounding. Our model achieves a competitive accuracy of 61.64% on the bias-sensitive VQA-CP v2 benchmark while maintaining 62.80% on the standard VQA v2 dataset, yielding a small generalization gap of 1.16%. This demonstrates a strong balance between OOD robustness and in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [16] [Efficient Text-to-Image Generation: An Adaptive Step Schedule Controller for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16572)
*Kuluhan Binici,Cihan Acar,Shivam Aggarwal,Siying Liu,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应扩散控制器，无需额外训练即可根据文本提示复杂度动态调整去噪步数，在COCO和DiffusionDB上减少推理时间并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型通常使用固定去噪步数来平衡时间成本与图像质量，但最优步数实际上取决于输入文本提示的复杂度，固定步数导致效率与质量难以兼顾。

Method: 提出自适应扩散控制器，无需额外模型训练。方法利用包含不同步长的混合步数调度方案，并在每个时间步评估误差项差异，据此在不同调度之间切换以优化性能。

Result: 在COCO和DiffusionDB数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持视觉保真度的同时减少了推理时间，为文本到图像扩散模型提供了更高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过自适应调整去噪步数，可在不牺牲图像质量的前提下提升文本到图像扩散模型的推理效率，且无需额外训练，具有良好的实用性。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models often use a fixed number of denoising steps, balancing time costs and image quality. However, the optimal number of steps depends on the complexity of the input text prompt. We propose an adaptive diffusion controller that dynamically adjusts the number of steps to generate high-quality images efficiently, without additional model training. By leveraging a mixture of step schedules with varying step sizes and evaluating the error term discrepancy at each timestep, our method transitions between schedules to optimize performance. Experiments on COCO and DiffusionDB show that our approach reduces inference time while maintaining visual fidelity, offering a more efficient alternative for text-to-image diffusion models.

</details>


### [17] [GraLoD: Graphics-Inspired Continuous Level-of-Detail Learning for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16578)
*Hu Gao,Lizhuang Ma,Yulong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用框架GraLoD，将图像复原的表示尺度建模为空间可变、随重建阶段变化的连续变量，通过LOD表示空间与阶段条件LOD场实现自适应尺度查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像复原所需的空间支撑范围随退化类型、图像区域和重建阶段而变化，但现有方法多依赖预定义的多尺度层级，通过固定融合或注意力聚合特征，使表示尺度基本由网络架构决定。当任务特定骨干被扩展到all-in-one复原中的异构退化时，这一局限更加明显。

Method: 受计算机图形学中细节层次（LOD）渲染启发，GraLoD复用编码器原生层级，将其多尺度特征对齐到共享LOD表示空间，并在每个解码阶段预测阶段条件LOD场；每个空间位置连续地仅查询两个相邻表示层级。此外引入最小充分足迹校准（MSFC）与结构感知正则化（SAR），避免退化或任意的尺度选择，鼓励复原有效且空间连贯的LOD分配。该框架可直接集成到现有复原骨干中，无需重新设计基本特征处理模块。

Result: 在任务特定复原和all-in-one复原中均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将复原尺度视为空间可变、阶段相关的连续变量是有效的，GraLoD作为即插即用框架可提升现有骨干的复原表现。

Abstract: The spatial support required for image restoration varies across degradation types, image regions, and reconstruction stages. However, most existing methods rely on predefined multi-scale hierarchies and aggregate features through fixed fusion or attention, leaving the representation scale itself largely determined by the network architecture. This limitation becomes more pronounced when a task-specific backbone is extended to heterogeneous degradations in all-in-one restoration. Inspired by level-of-detail (LOD) rendering in computer graphics, we propose GraLoD, a plug-and-play framework that treats restoration scale as a spatially varying and stage-dependent continuous variable. GraLoD reuses the native encoder hierarchy, aligns its multi-scale features into a shared LOD representation space, and predicts a stage-conditioned LOD field at each decoder stage. Each spatial location then continuously queries only two neighboring representation levels, enabling the effective restoration scale to adapt to both local image content and reconstruction progress. To prevent degenerate or arbitrary scale selection, we further introduce minimal-sufficient footprint calibration (MSFC) together with structure-aware regularization (SAR) to encourage restoration-effective and spatially coherent LOD assignments. GraLoD can be directly integrated into existing restoration backbones without redesigning their fundamental feature-processing blocks. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in task-specific and all-in-one restoration.

</details>


### [18] [FLAT: Resampling Image and Text into 1D Flexible-Length Aligned Transmodal Tokens for Retrieval and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16591)
*Guangyu Sun,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Wentao Bao,Robert Zhenheng Yang,Xiao Wang,Xiyuan Wang,Yujunrong Ma,Chen Yuan,Max Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao,Jianpeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLAT将多模态表示学习与生成统一在一个预训练框架中，产生可直接供生成解码器使用的线性可插值嵌入，在检索与生成任务上均达到或接近SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态表示学习与生成分为两阶段，先训练对比或自监督视觉编码器，再训练独立的下游生成模型，导致生成性能受制于冻结的嵌入表示。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，通过联合优化表示与生成获得可被生成解码器直接消费的嵌入。

Method: 提出FLAT（Flexible-Length Aligned Transmodal representations）预训练框架：联合优化共享多模态编码器与下游T2I、I2T解码器；结合对比对齐与双向跨模态生成目标，使表示同时作为判别性语义描述符和生成条件；架构上将视觉与文本输入映射到统一的连续一维序列空间，并在前缀K个token上施加嵌套dropout以支持动态输出长度。

Result: 单阶段预训练即可在不同前缀K下完成跨模态检索与生成，T2I GenEval达71.1；经任务微调后：T2I生成GenEval 83.1；MS-COCO图像描述BLEU-4 40.5、CIDEr 138.6；MS-COCO Recall@5为86.8(I2T)/75.8(T2I)，Flickr30K为98.3(I2T)/93.6(T2I)。定性评估显示表示原生支持线性插值、潜在空间算术与零样本组合检索。

Conclusion: FLAT通过联合优化表示学习与生成，使统一嵌入兼具判别与生成能力，并在检索、生成及零样本组合任务上取得优异表现，验证了表示与生成联合建模的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Traditional multimodal representation learning and generation are two stages: a contrastive or self-supervised visual encoder is trained first, followed by a separate downstream generative model. This setup bottlenecks generative performance behind frozen embeddings. To bridge this gap, we revisit joint multimodal representation learning and generation to produce linearly interpolatable embeddings that are directly consumable by generative decoders. We present FLAT (Flexible-Length Aligned Transmodal representations), a representation pre-training framework that jointly optimizes a shared multimodal encoder alongside downstream text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) decoders. By combining contrastive alignment with bidirectional cross-modal generative objectives, FLAT ensures its representations function as both discriminative semantic descriptors and generative conditions. Architecturally, FLAT maps visual and textual inputs into a unified continuous 1D sequence space, applying nested dropout over prefix-K tokens to enable dynamic output lengths. A single pre-training stage allows FLAT to perform cross-modal retrieval and generation across variable prefix K, achieving a T2I GenEval score of 71.1. Task-specific fine-tuning aligns model performance with state-of-the-art baselines: 83.1 GenEval on T2I generation; 40.5 BLEU-4 and 138.6 CIDEr on MS-COCO image captioning; and Recall@5 scores of 86.8 (I2T) / 75.8 (T2I) on MS-COCO alongside 98.3 (I2T) / 93.6 (T2I) on Flickr30K. Finally, qualitative evaluations demonstrate that FLAT representations natively support linear interpolation, latent space arithmetic, and zero-shot composed retrieval.

</details>


### [19] [FRPSS: Feature Rearrangement in Pre-Shape Space for Single-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16594)
*Yuexing Han,Haoxuan Zhang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FRPSS方法，通过预形状空间的特征重排缓解单图生成中的结构错位问题，并在三个数据集上取得最优SIFID分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图生成方法多依赖随机噪声驱动生成且缺乏显式全局结构约束，当结构发生变化时生成结果易出现空间结构错位，难以兼顾全局结构完整性与局部多样性。

Method: 核心为MSR-FAGS模块，用重排后的预形状特征替换低尺度生成器的随机初始化特征，并在后续尺度引导图像生成；同时设计SSPE策略与带SSPE的方向性CLIP监督模块（CLIP-SSPE）以支持风格化等下游任务。

Result: 在三个数据集上均取得最优SIFID分数，同时保持有竞争力的LPIPS；定性实验验证了FRPSS结合CLIP-SSPE在多个下游任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: FRPSS通过预形状空间特征重排有效降低结构错位风险，兼顾全局结构与局部多样性，并可扩展至多种下游任务。

Abstract: Generative models trained on a single image often struggle to balance global structural integrity and local diversity. Existing single-image generation methods commonly rely on random noise to drive the generation process and lack explicit global structural constraints, making the generated results prone to spatial structural misalignment when structural variations occur. To address the issue, Feature Rearrangement in Pre-Shape Space for Single-Image Generation (FRPSS) is proposed in this paper. The core of FRPSS is the Manifold Structural Rearrangement with Feature Augmentation on Geodesic Surface (MSR-FAGS) module. MSR-FAGS replaces the randomly initialized features of the low-scale generator with rearranged Pre-Shape features and uses the features to guide image generation at subsequent scales, thereby reducing the risk of structural misalignment. To support downstream tasks such as stylization, a Scale-adaptive Sliding-window Patch Extraction (SSPE) strategy is further designed, and a directional Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training supervision module with SSPE (CLIP-SSPE) is constructed. Qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that FRPSS achieves the best Single Image Fréchet Inception Distance (SIFID) scores on all three datasets while maintaining competitive Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS). Further qualitative experiments verify the effectiveness of FRPSS across multiple downstream tasks with the CLIP-SSPE module.

</details>


### [20] [A Vision-Language Foundation Model for Precise and Comprehensive Brain Tumor Diagnosis from Preoperative Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16597)
*Yinong Wang,Jianwen Chen,Zhou Chen,Shuwen Kuang,Haoning Jiang,Yanzhao Shi,Huichun Yuan,Yan-ran,Wang,Bing Wang,Lei Wu,Bin Tang,Li Meng,Baihua Luo,Bin Zhou,Wei Ding,Weiming Zhong,Wei Hou,Yuanbing Chen,Zhiping Wan,Wei Wang,Zhenkun Xiao,Wenwu Wan,Allen He,Yuyin Zhou,Longbo Zhang,Feifei Wang,Zhixiong Liu,Michael Iv,Xuan Gong,Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究构建了BrainVLM，一个基于MRI的AI模型，可对全部12种WHO 2021脑肿瘤类型进行分类，并整合不确定性量化与影像报告生成功能，经多中心大规模验证及前瞻性临床研究证实其临床效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤类型的无创术前诊断面临挑战：不同肿瘤类型影像特征重叠、观察者间差异大、专业知识培训成本高。研究旨在开发自动、可靠的脑肿瘤分类AI模型，并提供诊断不确定性量化与影像报告生成能力。

Method: BrainVLM整合了不确定性量化策略（指示预测可靠性）和影像报告生成模块（阐释临床依据）。模型基于40,043人的多模态数据（MRI扫描、人口统计学信息、影像报告）训练，在5,211例病理确诊患者上验证，包括主医院3,877例留出患者和11家独立医院的1,334例患者。开展两项概念验证研究验证临床效用：1）盲法多读者研究，12名不同经验水平的神经放射科医生对248例回顾性病例在有/无AI辅助下判读；2）真实世界前瞻性研究，1,009例患者术前由BrainVLM与放射科医生独立盲法评估。此外在632例多中心成人型弥漫性胶质瘤队列中展示其术前分子亚组预测价值。

Result: 模型覆盖全部12种WHO 2021脑肿瘤类型；在主医院3,877例及11家独立医院1,334例病理确诊患者上完成验证；通过12名神经放射科医生对248例的盲法多读者研究及1,009例真实世界前瞻性研究验证了临床效用；并在632例成人型弥漫性胶质瘤中展示术前分子亚组预测能力。

Conclusion: BrainVLM可实现对全部12种WHO 2021脑肿瘤类型的自动、可靠分类，具备诊断不确定性量化与影像报告生成能力，并经多中心验证、多读者盲法研究及真实世界前瞻性研究证实其在AI-临床医生工作流程中的临床效用，同时可支持成人型弥漫性胶质瘤的术前分子亚组预测。

Abstract: Background Non-invasive presurgical diagnosis of brain tumor types from Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is essential but challenging due to overlapping imaging features across tumor types, inter-observer variability, and the extensive training required for expertise. We aimed to develop an MRI-based Artificial Intelligence (AI) model for automatic and reliable brain tumor classification with diagnostic uncertainty quantification and radiology reports generation.
  Methods We developed BrainVLM to classify all 12 World Health Organization (WHO) 2021 brain tumor types. BrainVLM integrates an uncertainty quantification strategy to indicate prediction reliability and a module for generating radiology reports to elucidate the clinical rationale. BrainVLM was trained on multi-modal data (MRI scans, demographics, and radiology reports) from 40,043 individuals. It was validated on 5,211 patients with pathologically confirmed brain tumors, including 3,877 held-out patients from the primary hospital and 1,334 patients from 11 independent hospitals. We further conducted two proof-of-concept studies to validate its clinical utility in AI-clinician workflows: 1) a blinded multi-reader study where 12 neuroradiologists across varying experience levels interpreted 248 retrospective cases with or without AI assistance, and 2) a real-world prospective study in which 1,009 patients were independently and blindly assessed by BrainVLM and radiologists before surgery. Additionally, we demonstrated BrainVLM's utility in preoperative molecular subgroup prediction for adult-type diffuse gliomas, using a multi-center cohort of 632 patients.

</details>


### [21] [SAVOR: Self-Aware Visual Grounding via Confidence-Calibrated Reinforcement Learning for Multimodal Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16601)
*Zixiu Ding,Zilin Zhao,Yingjie He,Xinlang Kang,Guansu Wang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Savor：通过可校准自评估与GRPO训练，减少多模态大模型幻觉并保留通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在VQA和图像描述中进步明显，但仍会生成与图像不符的流畅表述。现有方法要么在测试时改解码增加延迟，要么用DPO类偏好微调只教"哪个答案更好"而不教"模型何时自己的答案不可靠"，缺少校准自评估信号。

Method: 提出Savor训练框架：(i)在输出模式中加入token级与答案级置信度；(ii)用GRPO目标优化策略，惩罚校准误差与不当弃答；(iii)推理时用学到的置信度仅在不确定时回看视觉证据。

Result: 在POPE、HallusionBench、AMBER、MMHal-Bench上，基于InternVL3-8B与Qwen3-VL-8B两个骨干，Savor降低幻觉，同时在MME和MMBench上保持通用能力，并比DPO和解码基线有更低的期望校准误差。

Conclusion: 校准的自评估是缺失的关键信号，Savor在减少幻觉、保持通用性能与降低校准误差上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made strong progress on visual question answering and image captioning, yet they still produce fluent claims about objects, attributes, or relations that are not grounded in the image. Many remedies either modify decoding at test time, which adds latency, or fine tune with preferences such as DPO variants, which teach which answer is preferred but not when the model's own answer is unreliable. We argue that calibrated self assessment is the missing signal. We introduce Savor, a training framework that (i) augments the output schema with token and answer confidence, (ii) optimises the policy with a Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) objective that penalises calibration error and poor abstention decisions, and (iii) uses the learned confidence at inference time to revisit visual evidence only when the model is uncertain. Experiments on POPE, HallusionBench, AMBER and MMHal-Bench across two recent backbones (InternVL3-8B and Qwen3-VL-8B) show that Savor reduces hallucination while preserving general capability on MME and MMBench, with lower Expected Calibration Error than DPO and decoding baselines.

</details>


### [22] [G3AR: Graph-Guided Neural Visual Geometry for Scalable Multi-Sequence Aerial Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16603)
*Jeng Wen Joshua Lean,Ting-Yu Yen,Wei-Fang Sun,Simon See,Hung-Kuo Chu,Shih-Hsuan Hung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G3AR是一个图引导的可扩展密集神经几何框架，用于多序列航空影像配准，通过构建几何验证的图像邻近图来引导有界重叠分块并定义对齐拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全上下文神经视觉几何在数千张影像上不可行，而基于序列的分块方法难以捕捉多序列航空影像中不规则的非局部重叠。

Method: 局部推理前构建几何验证的图像邻近图，引导有界重叠分块并诱导出块图，其最大生成树定义对齐拓扑；兼容骨干网络独立处理各分块，随后利用共享影像预测估计三维相似（Sim(3)）变换，将局部相机和几何配准到统一坐标系。

Result: 在四个真实航空场景中，与匹配的VGGT和Pi3骨干对比，G3AR在姿态误差和运行时间上均有改善；其DA3变体在所评估的神经几何方法中取得最低姿态误差。

Conclusion: G3AR为多序列航空影像提供了一种可扩展的密集神经几何配准方案，兼具效率与精度。

Abstract: Full-context neural visual geometry is impractical for thousands of images, while sequence-based chunking poorly captures irregular non-local overlap in multi-sequence aerial collections. We present Graph-Guided Neural Visual Geometry for Aerial Registration (G3AR), a graph-guided framework for scalable dense neural geometry. Before local inference, G3AR builds a geometrically verified image-proximity graph that guides bounded overlapping chunks and induces a chunk graph whose maximum spanning tree defines alignment topology. Compatible backbones process chunks independently; shared-image predictions then estimate three-dimensional similarity (Sim(3)) transforms that register local cameras and geometry in a common frame. Across four real aerial scenes, G3AR improves pose error and runtime in matched VGGT- and Pi3-backed comparisons, while its DA3 variant achieves the lowest pose error among evaluated neural-geometry methods.

</details>


### [23] [EgoPathBench: Evaluating Zero-Shot Egocentric Waypoint Decision-Making in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16610)
*Yang Zhao,Zhuo Chen,Xubo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EgoPathBench，一个面向第一人称路点决策的数据集与五任务基准，用于评估 VLM 的零样本空间导航能力，并发布训练资源以提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能基准多评估孤立的关系、方向或目标判断，无法直接衡量整合目标识别、动作后果评估、距离估计与路径规划于一体的导航能力，因此存在评估空白。

Method: 每个问题给出第一人称 RGB 图像、自然语言目标与带编号可见路点，模型需返回可行候选或有序路线；从候选可行性、相邻边合法性与目标到达三方面评估，并区分点代理与具身几何。数据集含 31,852 训练、1,345 验证与 1,111 基准问题，每个路线问题均保留至少一条几何验证的参考路线。

Result: 九个 VLM 中最高 EgoPath Score 仅 28.3；最佳模型在 Point Path 成功率 35.9%，但在 Embodied Path 与 Intent Path 仅 2.9% 和 4.0%。在发布训练集上微调 Qwen 3.5 4B，EgoPath Score 从 3.9 提升至 38.9，并在三个外部空间基准的四项评估上提升 1.4–9.6 分。

Conclusion: 当前模型在具身约束下仍难以形成完整且目标一致的路线；EgoPathBench 填补了整合导航能力评估的空白，所发布训练资源可显著提升模型的空间导航表现。

Abstract: Zero-shot waypoint navigation requires vision-language models to select, from the current first-person observation, a sequence of spatial actions that is feasible for the agent and reaches the goal, placing joint demands on the integrated spatial intelligence of today's foundation VLMs. Existing spatial-intelligence benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated judgments of relations, directions, or targets and therefore do not directly measure the integrated navigation ability required to combine target recognition, action-consequence assessment, distance estimation, and path planning. To fill this evaluation gap, we introduce EgoPathBench, a dataset and five-task benchmark for first-person waypoint decision-making. Each question presents an egocentric RGB image, a natural-language goal, and numbered visible waypoints; a model returns traversable candidates or an ordered route. Predictions are evaluated for candidate feasibility, adjacent-edge legality, and goal arrival under point-agent or embodied geometry. EgoPathBench contains 31,852 training, 1,345 validation, and 1,111 benchmark questions and retains at least one geometrically verified reference route for every route question. Across nine VLMs, the highest EgoPath Score is only 28.3. The top-ranked model reaches 35.9% success on Point Path, but only 2.9% and 4.0% on Embodied Path and Intent Path, respectively, showing that current models remain limited in forming complete, goal-consistent routes under embodiment constraints. Beyond the evaluation data, we release the corresponding training resource. Fine-tuning Qwen 3.5 4B on the released training split raises its EgoPath Score from 3.9 to 38.9 and improves all four reported evaluations across three external spatial benchmarks, with gains of 1.4--9.6 points.

</details>


### [24] [JewelTry: Mask-Free Scale Aware Jewelry Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16626)
*Xinlei Niu,Peixia Li,Jun Wang,Chenchen Xu,Jiayu Yang,Jing Zhang,Pulak Purkait,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出JVTO-Bench基准与JewelTry框架，实现无掩码、尺度感知的珠宝虚拟试戴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 珠宝虚拟试戴（VTON）因珠宝体积小、结构刚硬且对细节敏感而研究不足；现有方法多依赖掩码引导，无掩码方法又缺乏对产品尺度的显式建模，难以实现外观与尺度、位置的双重保真。

Method: 构建JVTO-Bench基准数据集，提供源-目标参考三元组及真实产品尺度标注，覆盖四大珠宝类别；提出JewelTry，一种无掩码扩散框架，包含将产品尺寸编码为尺度token的scale adapter，实现上下文中的珠宝-人体解剖尺度关系学习；并引入单向条件注意力机制与注意力细化损失以保持参考珠宝的粗粒度几何与细粒度结构细节。

Result: 大量实验表明，JewelTry在视觉保真度、背景保持、物体一致性与尺度准确性之间取得了平衡，为无掩码、尺度感知的珠宝虚拟试戴建立了强基线。

Conclusion: 该工作通过基准数据集与尺度感知扩散框架，填补了珠宝虚拟试戴中尺度建模的空白，为后续研究提供了强基线。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTON) enables customers to visualize how fashion products appear when worn and has become an important technology for online shopping. While recent advances have substantially improved garment VTON, jewelry remains a challenging and underexplored category due to its small size, rigid structure, and sensitivity to fine-grained visual details. Realistic jewelry VTON requires not only faithful appearance transfer but also accurate scale and placement relative to the wearer. Existing jewelry VTON methods typically rely on mask guidance, whereas mask-free approaches lack explicit guidance for modeling the product scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce JVTO-Bench, a benchmark dataset for scale-faithful jewelry VTON, providing reference source target triplets with real-world product-scale annotations across four major jewelry categories. Building upon this benchmark, we propose JewelTry, a mask-free diffusion framework for scale-aware jewelry VTON. JewelTry incorporates a scale adapter that encodes product dimensions into a scale token, enabling the model to learn scale relationships between jewelry items and surrounding human anatomy in-context. To further improve jewelry consistency, we introduce a single-directional condition attention mechanism and an attention refinement loss that preserve both coarse geometry and fine-grained structural details of the reference jewelry. Extensive experiments show that JewelTry achieves a balance among visual fidelity, background preservation, object consistency and scale accuracy, establishing a strong baseline for mask-free, scale-aware jewelry virtual try-on.

</details>


### [25] [Can Knowledge Transfer Parameters Be Learned? LePoKet for Efficient Robotic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16637)
*Yanick C. Tchenko,Felix Mohr,Hicham Hadj-Abdelkader,Hedi Tabia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LePoKet，一种将知识传递嵌入前向计算的结构化迁移框架，通过可学习遗传注意力联合优化交互参数，在CIFAR分类与RAFT光流任务上均超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在计算、内存与延迟受限下需要高效感知，而现有从大模型向紧凑网络的知识迁移方法多依赖固定蒸馏目标或人工设计的交互机制，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 基于Hereditary Knowledge Transfer (HKT)，提出LePoKet结构迁移框架，将知识继承嵌入前向计算；引入块级Extract-Transform-Mix接口，并用Learnable Genetic Attention (LGA)算子与子网络联合优化交互参数，无需辅助蒸馏损失或温度缩放。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上使用ResNet父子网络，相对误差分别降低24.57%和25.1%；在仅用FlyingChairs与FlyingThings3D训练的紧凑RAFT光流模型上，Sintel Clean EPE由2.21降至1.92，Sintel Final由3.35降至3.01，KITTI由7.51降至6.39；与HKT相比，CIFAR-10准确率由92.40%提升至93.40%，并在Sintel Final和KITTI上取得最佳误差。

Conclusion: 可学习的结构化迁移可跨识别与运动感知任务泛化，为高效机器人视觉提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Efficient perception is central to robotic systems operating under constrained computation, memory, and latency budgets. Knowledge transfer from larger pretrained models offers a practical route to stronger compact perception networks, but existing approaches commonly rely on fixed distillation objectives or manually designed interaction mechanisms. Building on Hereditary Knowledge Transfer (HKT), we propose LePoKet (Learnable Parameter Optimization for Knowledge Transfer), a structural transfer framework that embeds knowledge inheritance directly into the forward computation. LePoKet introduces a block-wise Extract-Transform-Mix interface whose interaction parameters are optimized jointly with the child network through a Learnable Genetic Attention (LGA) operator, without auxiliary distillation losses or temperature scaling. We first characterize the mechanism on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 using ResNet parent-child pairs, obtaining relative error reductions of 24.57% and 25.1%, respectively, over standard child training. We then evaluate LePoKet for dense motion estimation by integrating it into a compact RAFT-based optical-flow model trained only on FlyingChairs and FlyingThings3D. LePoKet improves the compact RAFT baseline from 2.21 to 1.92 EPE on Sintel Clean, from 3.35 to 3.01 on Sintel Final, and from 7.51 to 6.39 on KITTI. A direct comparison with HKT further shows that LePoKet improves CIFAR-10 accuracy from 92.40% to 93.40% while achieving the best Sintel Final and KITTI errors among the evaluated compact transfer variants, with comparable performance on Sintel Clean. These results demonstrate that learnable structural transfer generalizes across recognition and motion perception tasks and provides a promising approach for efficient robotic vision.

</details>


### [26] [What Do Hallucinations Reveal About Multimodal Reasoning? Diagnosing Visual Grounding Failures via Contrastive Decoding Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16646)
*Zhipeng Zhao,Wenxu Wang,Peishun Liu,Ruichun Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练解码框架SAFE，通过对比视觉接地与视觉消融生成路径，生成token级对比接地分数，用于检测与抑制LVLM视觉幻觉，并以此把LVLM当作研究自身失败动态的实验仪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强大多模态模型普及后，仅靠基准分数不足以推动进展，需要把模型当作理解行为的科学仪器。作者据此提出：能否用LVLM作为实验仪器来研究其自身的失败动态，特别是视觉幻觉。

Method: SAFE是免训练的解码框架，对同一生成过程分别运行视觉接地路径与视觉消融路径并进行对比，得到token级对比接地分数，用于识别模型何时偏向语言先验而非视觉证据；该信号既可作为视觉无接地token的检测代理，也可作为解码时惩罚项进行干预。

Result: 分析得到三点经验发现：视觉依赖随生成过程衰减、幻觉以时间簇形式共同出现、早期干预可减少成簇且不明显损害流畅性。在MMHalBench上SAFE显著优于所有对比基线，但在其他数据集上结果较为混杂。

Conclusion: 设计对比式探针体现了更广泛的研究使命：把模型作为科学理解的仪器。SAFE既是实用的幻觉检测与解码干预方法，也是研究模型失败动态的实验范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: When strong multimodal models are widely available, progress requires new scientific methodologies beyond benchmark scores---using models as instruments for understanding behavior. We address this by asking: can we use large vision-language models (LVLMs) as experimental instruments for studying their own failure dynamics? Focusing on visual hallucination, we introduce SAFE, a training-free decoding framework that contrasts visually-grounded and vision-ablated generation paths to produce a token-level contrastive grounding score that identifies when the model favors linguistic priors over visual evidence. This signal serves dual roles: as a practical proxy for detecting visually-ungrounded tokens, and as the basis for decoding-time penalties. Our analysis yields three empirical observations: visual dependency decays over generation, hallucinations co-occur in temporal clusters, and early intervention reduces clustering without substantially degrading fluency. On MMHalBench, SAFE substantially outperforms all compared baselines; results elsewhere are more mixed. We argue that designing contrastive probes exemplifies a broader mission: using models as instruments for scientific understanding. Code: https://github.com/zhaozhipeng1997/SAFE_public.

</details>


### [27] [ViD: Vision-Dominant Gender Bias Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16647)
*Zhipeng Zhao,Zhaoqiang Wei,Peishun Liu,Youwei Zhao,Ruichun Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViD：一种因果启发框架，通过分析注意力机制并采用双机制（后门调整＋精细token选择）在无需额外训练的情况下有效缓解LVLM中的性别偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）存在性别偏见，损害其公平性与可靠性，影响输出可信度。现有缓解方法依赖训练阶段调整或事后校准，难以实现动态视觉偏见缓解，具体表现为：无法捕捉实时的视觉-文本不一致、依赖预定义的性别偏见分类体系，以及在新兴偏见模式下跨模态对齐退化。

Method: 提出ViD，一个因果启发的框架，分析五种不同模式的注意力机制，揭示强语言先验带来的混淆效应。ViD包含双重机制：后门调整（backdoor adjustment）用于对抗强语言先验，解码层中的精细token选择用于优化处理；利用视觉到语言的交叉注意力抑制偏见，同时保持通用推理能力和文本生成质量。

Result: 在多个基准上验证：在单属性评测FACET上减少性别偏见14.7%；在MS COCO图像描述任务上显著提升，LLaVA的性别偏见得分从0.6708提升至0.9978；提升了视觉接地与输出公平性，且无需额外训练开销。

Conclusion: ViD能显著缓解LVLM在多维社会属性上的性别偏见，提升视觉接地和输出公平性，且因无需额外训练开销，是一种可扩展、实用的偏见缓解方案。

Abstract: Gender bias in large vision-language models (LVLMs) undermines their fairness and reliability, compromising output trustworthiness. Current mitigation methods rely on training-phase adjustments or post-hoc calibration, but face limitations in dynamic visual bias mitigation. These include inability to capture real-time visual-textual incongruence, dependence on predefined gender bias taxonomies, and degraded cross-modal alignment with emergent bias patterns. To address these challenges, we propose ViD, a causally-inspired framework that analyzes attention mechanisms across five distinct patterns, revealing confounding effects from strong language priors. ViD demonstrates that visual-to-language cross-attention effectively suppresses bias while preserving general reasoning capabilities and text generation quality. ViD incorporates dual mechanisms: backdoor adjustment counters strong language priors, while refined token selection in decoding layers optimizes processing. This enhances model robustness and inference efficiency. Our integrated approach significantly mitigates gender bias across multidimensional social attributes in LVLMs, improving visual grounding and output fairness. Cross-benchmark validation shows ViD reduces gender bias by 14.7\% on single-attribute evaluations (FACET) and achieves significant improvements on image captioning tasks (MS COCO), with gender bias score improving from 0.6708 to 0.9978 for LLaVA. Crucially, these improvements require no additional training overhead, making ViD a scalable and practical solution for bias mitigation in LVLMs.

</details>


### [28] [Channel-Wise and Token-Aware Post-Training Quantization for Visual State Space Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16656)
*Jonghyeon Lim,Changhoon Yim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究VSSD视觉状态空间模型的低比特训练后量化行为，发现激活量化是主要瓶颈，提出CTOAC方法学习逐通道裁剪边界，在保持精度的同时实现最高1.42倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VSSD等状态空间模型作为注意力架构的高效替代已扩展到视觉领域，但其低比特训练后量化（PTQ）行为尚缺乏充分理解，尤其是量化瓶颈的具体来源和激活分布特性未被系统分析。

Method: 在VSSD-Tiny上进行权重-激活分离分析，定位激活量化为主要瓶颈；观察到部分VSSD主干线性层输入存在强通道级幅度变化和token局部极值。据此提出CTOAC（通道级Token平衡输出感知裁剪）方法，通过最小化对应线性输出的token平衡重建损失，学习逐输入通道的裁剪边界。仅量化选定线性层及其输入激活，其余主干操作保持原精度。

Result: 在VSSD-Tiny、VSSD-Small和VSSD-Base上保持ImageNet-1K精度，在更激进精度设置下显著优于所评估基线；在COCO和ADE20K上保持强目标检测、实例分割和语义分割性能；优化后的RTX 4090部署配置相较FP32实现最高1.42倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 激活量化是VSSD低比特PTQ的主要瓶颈，所提CTOAC方法能有效保留多任务精度并支持实际部署加速，为视觉状态空间模型的高效量化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, have emerged as efficient alternatives to attention-based architectures and have been extended to vision through ViM, VMamba, and Visual State Space Duality (VSSD). Yet the low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) behavior of VSSD remains insufficiently understood. A weight-activation split on VSSD-Tiny identifies activation quantization as the dominant low-bit bottleneck, while representative inputs to selected VSSD-backbone linear layers exhibit strong channel-wise magnitude variation and token-localized extremes. We propose the Channel-wise Token-balanced Output-Aware Clipping (CTOAC) method, which learns per-input-channel clipping bounds by minimizing a token-balanced reconstruction loss on the corresponding linear outputs. Only the selected linear layers and their input activations are quantized; other backbone operations retain their original precision. Across VSSD-Tiny, VSSD-Small, and VSSD-Base, the proposed CTOAC method retains ImageNet-1K accuracy and remains substantially more robust than the evaluated baselines at more aggressive precision settings. Applying the same quantization scope to VSSD backbones on COCO and ADE20K preserves strong object detection, instance segmentation, and semantic segmentation performance. An optimized RTX 4090 deployment configuration achieves up to 1.42x end-to-end speedup over FP32.

</details>


### [29] [SAVTrack: Selective Vote Aggregation for Reliability-Aware Point Cloud Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16662)
*Sifan Zhou,Linyue Tan,Qiwei Wang,Ziyu Zhao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAVTrack框架，通过选择性投票聚合（SAV）评估候选投票可靠性并剔除低置信度假设，提升稀疏点云下3D单目标跟踪精度，在KITTI与nuScenes上达到竞争性性能且保持82 FPS实时速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云稀疏且不完整，不同目标点对物体中心的约束能力差异大，部分点到中心的投票可靠性低。现有基于点的跟踪器直接聚合这些假设而未显式建模其可靠性，导致不准确的投票污染提案聚类，降低定位精度。

Method: 提出SAVTrack运动感知跟踪框架，核心为Selective Vote Aggregation（SAV）。SAV结合局部种子特征与帧间运动上下文估计每个候选投票的可靠性，在提案聚类之前滤除低置信度假设，形成预聚合门控机制，防止不可靠假设影响聚类结构，且计算开销较小。

Result: 在KITTI上达到68.4/87.4（Success/Precision），在nuScenes上达到58.44/69.82，运行速度82 FPS；仅保留密集聚合所用候选投票的不到六分之一，在稀疏目标观测下尤为有效。

Conclusion: 显式建模并门控投票可靠性可有效缓解稀疏点云下的错误聚合问题，SAVTrack在保持高实时性的同时实现了竞争性跟踪性能，并显著减少冗余候选投票。

Abstract: 3D single object tracking (SOT) in LiDAR point clouds is essential for autonomous systems, but remains challenging under sparse and incomplete observations. In such cases, different target points provide highly uneven constraints on the object center, causing some point-to-center votes to be substantially less reliable than others. Existing point-based trackers typically aggregate these hypotheses without explicitly modeling their reliability, allowing inaccurate votes to contaminate proposal clustering and degrade localization accuracy. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{SAVTrack}, a motion-aware tracking framework with \textbf{Selective Vote Aggregation (SAV)}. SAVTrack estimates the reliability of each candidate vote from both local seed features and inter-frame motion context, and removes low-confidence hypotheses before proposal clustering. This pre-aggregation gating prevents unreliable hypotheses from affecting cluster formation while introducing only modest computational overhead. SAVTrack achieves competitive performance on KITTI and nuScenes, reaching 68.4/87.4 and 58.44/69.82 Success/Precision, respectively, while running at 82 FPS. It retains fewer than one-sixth of the candidate votes used by dense aggregation and remains particularly effective under sparse target observations.

</details>


### [30] [Bridging the Perceptual Gap: Residual-Enhanced Downscaling and Manifold-Aware Perception Alignment Adaptation for NR-IQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16664)
*Yu Li,Zhengran Shen,Yachun Mi,Puchao Zhou,Shaohui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模态感知对齐适配器CMPA，通过流形感知框架从CLIP的语义密集特征中分离感知失真信号，并配合残差增强感知降采样策略，在多个NR-IQA基准上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等大型视觉语言模型虽刷新了无参考图像质量评估（NR-IQA）的基准，但其对比预训练本质上面向语义不变性，往往压制细微的感知信号（即"感知淹没"现象）；同时，常规预处理（裁剪、插值）进一步加剧了关键高频质量线索的丢失。因此需要一种能把感知失真从主导语义中解耦出来的方法。

Method: 提出CMPA（Cross-modal Perception Alignment Adapter），一个流形感知框架，包含三部分：(1) 感知敏感特征提取器（PFE），将CLIP特征投影到紧致低维子空间，显式放大失真引起的偏离流形偏差；(2) 跨模态感知对齐注入器（PAI），将特征与质量感知的文本锚点对齐并重注入骨干网络；(3) 残差增强感知降采样策略，基于恰可察觉差异（JND）引导的频率再注入，自适应补偿分辨率导致的信息损失以保证输入保真度。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量评估表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，有效恢复了淹没在语义密集表征中的感知信号。

Conclusion: CMPA通过流形感知的跨模态对齐与频率域补偿，成功从CLIP的语义主导表征中解耦并恢复感知失真信号，为基于大型视觉语言模型的NR-IQA提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Leveraging Large Vision-Language Models like CLIP has recently set new benchmarks for No-Reference Image Quality Assessment (NR-IQA). However, the contrastive pretraining of CLIP inherently prioritizes semantic invariance, which often suppresses subtle perceptual signals, a phenomenon we term perceptual submergence. Furthermore, standard preprocessing techniques (e.g., cropping and interpolation) further exacerbate the loss of critical high-frequency quality cues. In this paper, we propose the Cross-modal Perception Alignment Adapter (CMPA), a manifold-aware framework designed to disentangle perceptual distortions from dominant semantics. CMPA introduces a Perception-Sensitive Feature Extractor (PFE) that projects CLIP features into a compact, low-dimensional subspace, explicitly magnifying distortion-induced off-manifold deviations. Subsequently, a Cross-Modal Perception Alignment Injector (PAI) aligns these features with quality-aware text anchors and re-injects them into the backbone. To ensure input fidelity, we also devise a Residual-enhanced Perceptual Downscaling strategy that adaptively compensates for resolution-induced information loss using Just Noticeable Difference (JND) guided frequency re-injection. Extensive evaluations on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, effectively recovering the perceptual signals submerged in semantic-dense representations.

</details>


### [31] [Lesion-centered 3D mapping of colonoscopy procedures: validation of a hierarchical ensemble pipeline on public benchmark videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16672)
*Hyunjun Kim,Hyeonwoo Na,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并验证一套以病灶为中心的四层空间记录管线，无需全结肠3D重建即可检测重访、维持病灶身份并进行局部3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜记录目前仅保留文本报告和静态照片，视频中已有的空间信息（内镜行程、病灶位置及是否被再次观察）在操作结束时被丢弃。

Method: 构建四层分层管线：(1) 全局拓扑地图；(2) 病灶级时空轨迹；(3) 按需局部3D重建；(4) 重复观察下的持久病灶身份。在四个公开视频（两个带真值深度的C3VDv2序列、两个完整REAL-Colon操作，共40,245帧）上端到端运行；各组件均为已发表且单独验证的方法，贡献在于以病灶为中心的组装、连接规则与评估。

Result: 基于入口地图的贝叶斯定位检测到前向制图在原理上无法实现的重访：两个完整操作中分别有5,614和4,043次重访事件（56和68个不同节点）。在阈值0.5下，病灶身份合并保持真值纯度1.0，并在231个候选对中自动合并20对。内镜专用几何引擎在所有指标上优于通用基础模型（整体AbsRel 0.2276 vs. 0.3523）。

Conclusion: 结果虽为阶段性，但确立了近期的可行路径：重访检测、病灶身份与局部3D均可在不等待完整几何重建的情况下给出定量、可复现的输出；下一步是在临床数据上验证该记录。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Colonoscopy recording practice preserves text reports and still photographs, while the spatial information already present in the recorded video - where the scope traveled, where a lesion was observed, and whether the same lesion was seen again - is discarded when the procedure ends. This study determines whether a lesion-centered spatial record can be assembled and validated without full-colon 3D reconstruction. Methods: A four-layer hierarchical pipeline was assembled - (1) a global topological map, (2) lesion-level spatio-temporal tracks, (3) on-demand local 3D reconstruction, and (4) persistent lesion identity across repeated observations - and ran end to end on four public videos (two C3VDv2 sequences with ground-truth depth and two full REAL-Colon procedures; 40,245 frames). All components are published, individually validated methods; the contribution is their lesion-centered assembly, linking rules, and evaluation. Results: Revisits, impossible under forward-only mapping by construction, were detected by entry-map Bayesian localization: 5,614 and 4,043 revisit events (56 and 68 distinct nodes) in the two full procedures. Lesion-identity merging at the adopted threshold 0.5 maintained ground-truth purity 1.0 while auto-merging 20 of 231 candidate pairs. The endoscopy-specific geometry engine outperformed a general-purpose foundation model on all metrics (overall absolute relative error (AbsRel) 0.2276 vs. 0.3523). Conclusions: The results are partial but establish a concrete near-term path: revisit detection, lesion identity, and local 3D each returned quantitative, reproducible output without waiting for complete geometric reconstruction; validating the record on clinical data is the next step.

</details>


### [32] [MEgoVista: Multi-view Ego-aware Motion Estimation for Metric 4D Hands and Head in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16684)
*Jiangong Xiao,Zhihao Zhang,Yifei Dong,Chao Ma,Zhouyi Jin,Zhiwen Hou,Li Liu,Weihuang Chen,Hongbin Sun,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEgoVista离线流程，将单次未准备的头戴自我中心视频重建为同一重力对齐世界坐标系下的公制双手与头部运动，并在动捕体积内用独立光学动捕进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习操作需要高保真、以公制单位度量的手部运动。现有公制手部标注多来自摄影棚装备或带仪器的头显，二者受限于固定场地，且缺乏独立参考校验。无约束头戴式记录可随佩戴人数扩展，但精度与度量性存疑，因此需要新的重建与评估方案。

Method: MEgoVista将单次未准备的MEgo View录制离线处理，输出公制两手机头运动于同一重力对齐世界坐标系。三个特点：1）在摄影棚与桌面装置无法覆盖的场景中重建，并在检测阶段判定手部归属，排除旁观者的手；2）公制尺度来自标定双目而非单目先验，在初始化时即安装尺度，使策略获得物理单位；3）输出在动捕体积内对照独立Chingmu光学捕获评分，协议自审参考并对未预测部分计罚。

Result: 在手部归属、公制尺度安装与独立动捕评估三方面与现有自我中心重建系统区分开来；评估协议可自审参考并对方法未预测的内容计罚。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: MEgoVista提供了一条从自我中心视频到公制手部监督的可测量路径，拓宽了此类标签可采集的场景范围。

Abstract: Learning manipulation from human video requires high-fidelity hand-motion reconstruction in metric units. Today's metric hand labels come from studio rigs and instrumented headsets, and both are confined in the same two ways: neither leaves a prepared setting, and neither is checked against an independent reference. Unconstrained head-worn recording promises the opposite trade-off, scaling with the number of people wearing a device. We therefore introduce MEgoVista, an offline pipeline that turns a single unprepared MEgo View recording into metric two-hand and head motion in one gravity-aligned world frame. Three properties set it apart from existing egocentric reconstruction systems: first, it reconstructs in settings studio volumes and tabletop rigs cannot reach, settling hand ownership at detection so bystander hands stay out of the wearer's trajectory; second, it takes its metric gauge from calibrated stereo rather than a monocular prior, installing scale at initialisation so policies receive physical units, not arbitrary coordinates; third, both outputs are scored inside a motion-capture volume against independent Chingmu optical capture, under a protocol that audits its own reference and charges what a method declines to predict. MEgoVista is offered as a measured route from egocentric video to metric hand supervision, one that widens where such labels can be gathered.

</details>


### [33] [Efficient Quantization-Aware Distillation with Cross-Modal Alignment for Edge Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16689)
*Jinwoo Jeon,GyuYeop Do,Yubin Lim,Nam-Joon Kim,Hyun Gon Ryu,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向边缘部署的量化语义蒸馏统一框架，联合优化蒸馏与量化，并设计跨注意力适配器缓解非RGB模态失衡，在保持部署效率的同时提升非RGB模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等大规模视觉语言模型具备强开放词汇推理能力，但难以部署到资源受限的边缘设备。现有EdgeVL通过蒸馏与量化感知训练实现边缘开放词汇分类，但存在两点问题：一是两阶段优化目标不一致，对比学习在量化学生空间中进行，导致优化不一致、训练效率下降；二是RGB与非RGB模态使用相同监督，易造成模态失衡。

Method: 提出面向边缘部署的量化语义蒸馏统一框架：在统一的教师锚定框架下联合优化蒸馏与量化，保证量化下训练一致性，抑制难负样本并扩大决策边界；同时设计轻量级跨注意力适配器，通过RGB引导的语义迁移增强非RGB表征，缩小模态差距。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在非RGB模态上取得一致提升，同时保持部署效率。

Conclusion: 统一教师锚定的蒸馏与量化优化，并结合RGB引导的跨模态适配，可有效缓解量化训练不一致与模态失衡问题，为边缘设备上的开放词汇分类提供高效方案。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language models (VLM) such as CLIP enable strong open-vocabulary reasoning, yet deploying these capabilities on resource-constrained edge devices remains challenging. EdgeVL addresses this problem by distilling CLIP representations into lightweight multi-modal encoders and applying quantization-aware training (QAT) for efficient Open-Vocabulary Classification (OVC) on edge hardware. However, its two-stage optimization applies different objectives for distillation and QAT, and contrastive learning is performed within the quantized student space, which can result in inconsistent optimization and reduced training efficiency. Moreover, identical supervision across RGB and non-RGB modalities may lead to modality imbalance. We propose a unified framework for quantized semantic distillation tailored to edge deployment. By jointly optimizing distillation and quantization within a unified teacher-anchored framework, our method ensures consistent training under quantization, suppressing hard negatives and enlarging decision margins. Additionally, we design a lightweight cross-attention adapter that enhances non-RGB representations through RGB-guided semantic transfer, narrowing the modality gap. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on non-RGB modalities while maintaining deployment efficiency.

</details>


### [34] [Efficient 3D Whole-Body PET Image Denoising via Conditional Rectified Flow With Optimized Sampling Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16690)
*Jiale Shen,Guolin Wang,Chenhao Wang,Xinhui Su,Wei Luo,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一次性条件3D rectified flow框架（3D Flow），用于全身PET图像去噪，30秒完成全容积重建，显著快于3D DDPM，并在零样本迁移下保持图像质量与病灶可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低PET辐射暴露对患者安全重要，但超低剂量成像噪声严重。现有3D扩散模型重建保真度高却推理耗时数小时，而快速2D方法难以保持全身PET所需的容积一致性，因此需兼顾保真度与计算效率。

Method: 提出一次性（one-pass）条件3D rectified flow框架，并引入优化的非均匀采样策略；采用一次性线性插值速度匹配目标进行训练，直接重建完整3D容积。

Result: 约30秒完成全3D容积重建，而对比的3D DDPM基线需数小时推理；在包括独立临床数据集的零样本迁移及短采集数据上，全局图像质量与病灶可见度优于3D DDPM和DDIM基线；在未见剂量水平（低至标准剂量的1/100）上展现良好零样本迁移表现。

Conclusion: 该方法在重建保真度与计算效率之间取得平衡，是超低剂量全身PET图像去噪的候选方案；但基于伪影的视觉比较表明仍需进一步的病灶级验证。

Abstract: Reducing radiation exposure in Positron Emission Tomography (PET) is important for patient safety; however, ultra-low-dose imaging suffers from severe noise, which may affect diagnostic interpretation without appropriate image enhancement. While current 3D deep generative models, particularly diffusion models, have shown strong reconstruction fidelity, their practical use can be limited by long inference times. In contrast, faster 2D-based alternatives may have difficulty maintaining volumetric consistency, an important consideration for whole-body PET imaging analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose a one-pass conditional 3D rectified flow (3D Flow) framework for whole-body PET image denoising that incorporates a novel optimized non-uniform sampling strategy. The model is trained with a one-pass linear-interpolant velocity-matching objective. This approach reconstructs a full 3D volume in approximately 30 seconds in our implementation, compared with multi-hour inference for the evaluated 3D DDPM baseline. Evaluations including zero-shot transfer to an independent clinical dataset show that our model achieves favorable global image quality and lesion conspicuity compared with the evaluated 3D DDPM and DDIM baselines, including on challenging short-acquisition data. Furthermore, the proposed method shows promising zero-shot transfer performance across the evaluated datasets and unseen dose levels (down to 1/100 of the standard dose), with artifact-focused visual comparisons supporting the need for further lesion-level validation. By balancing reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency, this work presents a candidate approach for ultra-low-dose whole-body PET image denoising.

</details>


### [35] [MAETrack: Unleashing the Potential of Pretrained Geometric Priors for 3D Single Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16695)
*Sifan Zhou,Qiwei Wang,Linyue Tan,Ziyu Liu,Ziyu Zhao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAETrack，将3D MAE预训练表征迁移到3D单目标跟踪，通过浅层选择性初始化和几何残差门控缓解层级迁移失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练在2D视觉中成效显著，但其向3D单目标跟踪（SOT）的迁移性仍不清晰。直接微调MAE等自监督3D编码器效果不佳，因为重建目标与跟踪所需的时空匹配要求不一致；作者进一步观察到这是一种"层级迁移失配"：浅层保留可迁移的几何线索，深层则过度专精于重建预训练任务，不适合下游跟踪。

Method: 提出轻量适配框架MAETrack，包含两个模块：（1）Layer-Selective Initialization（LSI），仅用预训练权重初始化跟踪骨干的浅层阶段，深层阶段重新初始化；（2）Geometric Residual Gating（GRG），在模板-搜索融合之前，通过残差空间调制强化搜索BEV特征中的结构显著区域。

Result: 在标准3D SOT基准上的大量实验表明，MAETrack以有限的额外计算开销，持续优于普通微调基线。

Conclusion: 从3D重建预训练到3D跟踪的有效迁移，不仅是部分微调的问题，而取决于一种面向跟踪的迁移原则：保留浅层几何信息，同时让深层表征适配下游目标。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training has transformed representation learning in 2D vision, yet its transferability to 3D single object tracking (SOT) remains insufficiently understood. Directly fine-tuning self-supervised 3D encoders, such as masked autoencoders (MAE), often leads to sub-optimal adaptation because the reconstruction objective is not fully aligned with the spatial-temporal matching requirements of tracking. In this paper, we observe that this difficulty can be interpreted as a layer-wise transfer mismatch: shallow layers tend to preserve transferable geometric cues, while deeper layers become increasingly specialized to the reconstruction pretext task and are less suitable for downstream tracking. Based on this observation, we propose MAETrack, a lightweight adaptation framework for transferring pre-training MAE representations to 3D SOT. MAETrack includes Layer-Selective Initialization (LSI), which initializes only the shallow stages of the tracking backbone from pre-trained weights while re-initializing deeper stages, and Geometric Residual Gating (GRG), which reinforces structurally salient regions in the search BEV features before template-search fusion through residual spatial modulation. Extensive experiments on standard 3D SOT benchmarks show that MAETrack consistently improves upon vanilla fine-tuning baselines with limited computational overhead.
