The proposed approach is capable of detecting ROIs belonging to multiple object classes, including various vehicle types, pedestrians, traffic lights, and traffic signs, with accuracy up to 70.8%, while operating at millisecond temporal resolution, 16x finer than the temporal resolution provided by the dataset ground truth. These results highlight the potential of combining early event downscaling with saliency-based attention as an effective front-end for efficient edge neuromorphic vision systems.

</details>


### [73] [From Foundation Embeddings to Cropland Maps: Label Efficiency, Temporal Transferability and Independent Human Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17138)
*Mohammad Ammar Mughees,Giovanni Montefoschi,Zhongxin Chen,Maria Antonia Brovelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了AlphaEarth年度地理空间基础模型嵌入在美国缅因州耕地/非耕地二分类制图中的可用性。无需微调，轻量分类器在独立空间区块上达到93.7%总体精度、90.8%平衡精度；与CDL相比，在与双标注者共识对照时精度更高（95.3% vs 91.7%），与微调的TerraMind分割模型差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型可提供可复用的卫星影像表征，减少下游制图对任务专门建模的依赖。作者希望检验此类"冻结"嵌入在区域尺度耕地制图中的实际效果，并与现有参考产品（USDA CDL）及微调模型进行对比，从而判断其是否可作为低计算成本的替代方案。

Method: 使用AlphaEarth年度嵌入，在缅因州构建192个空间分离的图像块，标签来自USDA CDL。在不微调基础模型的条件下，采用逻辑回归、梯度提升集成、最近类质心等轻量分类器进行分类；并开展像素样本量效率实验（6万平衡像素 vs 860万全量像素）与跨年份（2018–2023）同区域迁移实验。最后在一个2023年连续区块的385个随机点上，用双解释者盲法共识进行人工验证，并与CDL及微调的TerraMind分割模型比较。

Result: 留出图像块上总体精度93.7%、平衡精度90.8%；逻辑回归与梯度提升集成差距在0.3个百分点内，无参数拟合的最近类质心规则达90.2%。6万平衡像素比全量860万像素低1.3个百分点以内（因像素空间自相关，该结果说明的是像素样本效率而非独立标注点效率）。跨2018–2023年份迁移中分类器保持准确。人工验证中，AlphaEarth+随机森林与共识一致性95.3%（κ=0.82），CDL为91.7%（κ=0.72，McNemar p=0.0161）；与微调TerraMind差异不显著（95.3% vs 93.5%，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 冻结的地理空间嵌入可作为区域耕地制图的低计算成本候选方案。但结论受限于单一州研究、30 m派生训练参考数据以及单区块人工验证；与CDL的局部差异可能反映CDL标签噪声的部分平滑，并不能证明对参考产品进行全州范围的纠错。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models provide reusable representations of satellite imagery that support downstream mapping with limited task-specific modelling. We evaluate whether annual AlphaEarth embeddings support binary cultivated-versus-non-cultivated mapping in Maine, USA, using 192 spatially separated patches and labels derived from the USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Without fine-tuning the foundation model, a lightweight classifier reaches 93.7% overall accuracy and 90.8% balanced accuracy on held-out patches. Logistic regression is within 0.3 percentage points of a gradient-boosted ensemble, while a nearest-class-centroid rule, which uses class centroids but fits no parameters, reaches 90.2%. A balanced sample of 60,000 labelled pixels is within 1.3 percentage points of the full pool of 8.6 million pixels; because pixels are spatially autocorrelated, this result concerns pixel-sample efficiency rather than 60,000 independent annotation sites. In a same-region transfer experiment, classifiers trained in one year remain accurate across 2018 to 2023. Against a blind, two-interpreter consensus at 385 randomly sampled points in one contiguous 2023 block, the AlphaEarth-plus-random-forest map agrees at 95.3% ($κ=0.82$), compared with 91.7% for the CDL ($κ=0.72$; exact two-sided McNemar $p=0.0161$). This local result is consistent with partial smoothing of CDL label noise, but it does not establish statewide correction of the reference product. On the same points, the difference from a fine-tuned TerraMind segmentation model is not statistically significant (95.3% versus 93.5%; $p=0.14$), and the experiment is not a controlled comparison of computational cost. These results support frozen geospatial embeddings as a low-compute candidate for regional cropland mapping, subject to the limits of a single-state study, a 30 m-derived training reference, and a one-block human validation.

</details>


### [74] [ResLRP: The Role of Residual Cancellation in Attribution Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17152)
*Jim Berend,Reduan Achtibat,Daniel Schäffer,Alexander Binder,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Maximilian Dreyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出ViT中残差连接的抵消效应导致LRP归因爆炸，提出ResLRP方法显式处理残差分支抵消，守恒且有界，大幅提升归因质量，尤其在VLM上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT是主流视觉模型，但难以获得细粒度、忠实且稳定的输入归因。LRP虽已适配Transformer注意力，但在ViT中常产生噪声大、不忠实的解释。作者发现问题关键在于残差连接的处理：残差路径中的抵消效应导致归因爆炸，且这种抵消在ViT中比语言Transformer更强。

Method: 提出Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP)，这是LRP的简单扩展，其传播规则显式考虑残差分支中的抵消，精确守恒，并可证明地限制归因爆炸。通过因果通道干预验证残差抵消（而非通用正则化）是稳定性问题的驱动因素。

Result: 在监督、自监督、对比、层次化及多模态等ViT架构以及可控真值的FunnyBirds基准上，ResLRP在忠实度和定位方面显著提升归因质量。最大增益出现在现代视觉语言模型(VLM)中，定位提升+27-29%，忠实度最高提升3.4倍。此外，ResLRP可在输入空间定位SAE特征，残差放大度量可作为架构级诊断预测归因退化位置。

Conclusion: 残差抵消是ViT归因不稳定的核心原因，ResLRP通过显式处理残差分支抵消有效解决该问题，并在多种架构和VLM上显著提升归因质量，同时提供架构级诊断工具。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are central to most modern vision models, yet obtaining input attributions that are fine-grained, faithful, and stable remains challenging. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) has been adapted to transformer attention, but in ViTs it often produces noisy, unfaithful explanations. We show that the missing ingredient is the treatment of residual connections: cancellation effects in residual pathways lead to attribution explosion. Moreover, we find that these cancellations are substantially stronger in ViTs than in language transformers. To address this issue, we introduce Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP), a simple extension of LRP whose propagation rules explicitly account for cancellations in residual branches, are exactly conservative, and provably bound relevance explosion. Causal channel-wise interventions confirm that residual cancellation, not a generic regularization effect, drives the instability. ResLRP substantially improves attribution quality across faithfulness and localization, evaluated on ViT architectures spanning supervised, self-supervised, contrastive, hierarchical, and multimodal families, as well as on the ground-truth-controlled FunnyBirds benchmark. The largest gains arise in modern Vision Language Models (VLMs), with +27-29% localization and up to 3.4x faithfulness scores. Beyond benchmarks, ResLRP localizes Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) features in input space, and our residual amplification measure serves as an architecture-level diagnostic predicting where attribution degrades.

</details>


### [75] [MUMINS: Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17169)
*Anna Oliveras,Roger Marí,Rafael Redondo,Oriol Guardià,Cynthia Ifeyinwa Ugwu,Ana Tost,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Carolina Migliorelli,Vicent Ribas,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MUMINS，一种高效扩散框架，通过单次反向扩散过程联合合成随访影像并预测空间不确定性图，在肺部CT和脑MRI上匹配或超越专用SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测肿瘤生长、神经退行等解剖变化是困难的生成视觉任务：形态演化相对静态解剖更细微、高度患者特异且内在随机。现有方法存在缺陷——确定性网络忽略生物学随机性，而标准扩散模型需多轮采样来量化不确定性，计算代价过高。

Method: 提出 MUMINS（元数据条件化、不确定性感知的医学图像下一状态合成）。它联合扩散基线扫描及其随访残差，求和得到随访影像；在单次反向扩散过程中同时预测空间不确定性图。以时间间隔和相关元数据为条件，并在每个去噪步骤动态重注入基线作为软锚点以保留细粒度解剖；使用负对数似然头学习不确定性图以显式标记易错区域。方法不含器官特定启发式，通过数据集特定重训练复用于不同解剖结构。

Result: 大量评估表明，经数据集特定重训练的 MUMINS 在肺部CT（PNG）和脑MRI（OASIS-3）上匹配或超越了专用的、领域特定的最先进方法。

Conclusion: MUMINS 以单次扩散同时实现随访影像合成与不确定性估计，兼具效率与通用性，为医学图像下一状态合成提供了可跨解剖复用的有效框架。

Abstract: Forecasting anatomical changes such as tumor growth and neurodegeneration is a challenging generative vision task. Morphological evolution is subtle relative to static anatomy, highly patient-specific, and inherently stochastic. Existing methods struggle with several issues: deterministic networks ignore biological stochasticity, while standard diffusion models require computationally prohibitive multi-pass sampling to quantify uncertainty. We propose MUMINS (Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis), an efficient diffusion framework that jointly diffuses a baseline scan and its follow-up residual, summed to synthesize the follow-up scan, while concurrently predicting a spatial uncertainty map, in a single reverse diffusion process. Conditioned on the time interval and relevant metadata, it preserves fine-grained anatomy by dynamically re-injecting the baseline as a soft anchor at every denoising step, and a negative-log-likelihood head learns the uncertainty map to explicitly flag error-prone regions. Designed without organ-specific heuristics, the same architecture is reused across anatomies via separate, dataset-specific retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that dataset-specific retraining of MUMINS matches or outperforms dedicated, domain-specific state-of-the-art methods on lung CT (PNG) and brain MRI (OASIS-3). Project page: https://github.com/aolivtous/MUMINS.

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Cultural Heritage Architectural Style Classification for Residential Buildings in the UAE Based on CLIP Embeddings and SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17181)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Iman Ibrahim,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CLIP的多模态框架，融合建筑图像与专家文本描述，用于阿联酋住宅建筑风格的分析与分类，SVM分类准确率达98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统建筑风格分类主要依赖CNN处理复杂建筑图像，忽视文本描述信息，且缺乏针对非西方地区的专门数据集，阿联酋住宅建筑风格研究存在空白。

Method: 采用OpenAI CLIP模型，将图像视觉特征与专家文本描述特征融合为统一的512维嵌入；使用UMAP降维进行可视化，K-Means进行无监督聚类；基于人工分析聚类结果生成标签，训练SVM分类器实现自动风格分类。

Result: 在八个已识别的风格聚类上取得98%的分类准确率，高于文献中其他所有研究，验证了视觉与文本模态结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态AI可有效支持建筑遗产分析，为探索区域建筑身份提供可扩展且可解释的工具。

Abstract: The analysis and classification of cultural heritage architectural styles remain challenging due to the complexity of visual images of buildings, which are highly relied on in traditional CNN-based classification approaches in comparison to textual descriptions, and the relative lack of non-western region-specific datasets. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a multimodal machine learning framework to analyze and classify Emirati residential architecture using OpenAI's CLIP model. We integrate visual features from images and textual features from expert descriptions into a unified 512-dimensional embedding, followed by dimensionality reduction with UMAP for visualization and unsupervised clustering using K-Means. Cluster labels, which are derived from manual analysis of the K-Means clusters, are used to train an SVM classifier for automated architectural style classification. Our approach achieves a classification accuracy of 98% across eight identified style clusters, higher than every other study in the literature, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining visual and textual modalities. Overall, this paper highlights the potential of using multimodal AI to support architectural heritage analysis, offering scalable and interpretable tools for exploring regional architectural identities.

</details>


### [77] [EventEgoHands++: Event-based Egocentric 3D Hand Mesh Reconstruction with Real Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17189)
*Ryosei Hara,Wataru Ikeda,Masashi Hatano,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EventEgoHands++，一种基于事件相机的第一视角 3D 手部网格重建框架，通过实例级手部检测和自适应注意力机制提升左右手辨识与重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机在低光和严重运动模糊下表现不佳，事件相机虽具高动态范围和时间分辨率优势，但在第一视角手部重建中，佩戴者运动产生密集背景事件干扰手部信号。已有方法用二值手部掩码缓解该问题，但无法区分左右手，导致模型缺乏实例级信息，在单手或无手场景下仍预测双手，造成手间关系错误和重建精度下降。

Method: 提出 EventEgoHands++ 框架。包含一个手部检测器（Hand Detector），估计左右手的实例级边界框和掩码；并引入自适应注意力（Adaptive Attention），根据检测结果动态门控注意力，以准确学习双手间的空间关系和相互交互。同时扩展合成 N-HOT3D 数据集，并新建 EEH-R 真实数据集。

Result: 构建了 EEH-R，目前最大的真实事件第一视角手部数据集，包含约 100 万标注帧，涵盖低光等环境。在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过实例级手部检测与自适应注意力，EventEgoHands++ 有效解决了左右手区分与手间交互建模问题，在合成与真实事件数据上均取得优于基线的表现，推动了事件相机第一视角 3D 手部重建的发展。

Abstract: 3D hand mesh reconstruction is a challenging yet essential task for downstream applications, including human-robot interaction and AR/VR. Although conventional cameras have been widely adopted for this task, methods that rely on them struggle in low-light environments and under severe motion blur. To address these limitations, event-based cameras have recently attracted attention for their high dynamic range and high temporal resolution. However, applying event cameras to egocentric hand reconstruction remains challenging because camera wearer's motion produces dense background events that obscure hand-specific signals. Although the first egocentric event-based approach mitigates this issue using hand segmentation, its binary hand mask does not distinguish between left and right hands. As a result, the model lacks instance-level hand information and predicts both hands even when only one or neither hand is present. This limitation leads to incorrect inter-hand relationships and degraded reconstruction accuracy. In this paper, we propose EventEgoHands++, a framework for event-based 3D hand mesh reconstruction from an egocentric viewpoint. The proposed method incorporates a Hand Detector that estimates instance-level bounding boxes and masks for both the left and right hands. Moreover, we introduce Adaptive Attention, which dynamically gates the attention based on these detection results to accurately learn the spatial relationship and mutual interactions between the hands. To train and evaluate our framework, we extend the synthetic N-HOT3D dataset and newly construct EEH-R, the largest real-world event-based egocentric hand dataset to date, comprising approximately 1M annotated frames captured in environments including low-light conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Probe-VAD: Ordinal Likelihood Probing for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17211)
*Jiawei Gu,Qilin Zhao,Tengkuo Guo,Zhiming Zhong,Shuangqing Zhang,Fan Lyu,Fang Zhao,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Probe-VAD 提出一种无需训练的序数二元探测框架，直接从冻结的视觉语言模型中探测异常严重程度偏好，生成连续且对排序敏感的异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的无训练视频异常检测方法在视觉理解与异常打分之间存在受限的接口：基于描述的流程将视觉证据压缩为文本，可能丢失细微线索；直接数值生成则强迫模型用少量预定义分数表达判断。这些接口会掩盖异常严重程度的细微差异，导致视觉上不同的片段获得相似表示或分数，限制了异常排序的分辨率。

Method: Probe-VAD 直接探测冻结视觉语言模型的严重程度偏好。对原始视频片段，查询十个有序的严重程度阈值，提取受约束的 YES/NO 续写似然；将其归一化偏好构成累积严重程度分布，从尾部证据聚合为连续异常分数，并通过保序投影强制序数一致性。

Result: 在公开视频异常检测基准上取得更优性能，且计算成本较低。

Conclusion: Probe-VAD 提供了一种简单接口，无需任务特定训练或基于描述的压缩，即能把冻结视觉语言模型的视觉理解转化为连续、对排序敏感的异常分数。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to localize anomalous events in untrimmed videos. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide rich visual understanding for training-free VAD, but existing approaches impose restrictive interfaces between visual understanding and anomaly scoring. Caption-based pipelines compress visual evidence into text, potentially discarding subtle cues, while direct numerical generation forces the model to express its judgment through a small set of predefined scores. Such interfaces can obscure subtle differences in anomaly severity, causing visually distinct clips to receive similar representations or scores and thereby limiting the resolution of anomaly ranking. We propose \textbf{Probe-VAD}, an ordinal binary-probing framework that directly probes severity preferences from a frozen VLM. Given raw video clips, Probe-VAD queries ten ordered severity thresholds and extracts constrained \textit{YES}/\textit{NO} continuation likelihoods. Their normalized preferences form a cumulative severity profile, from which tail evidence is aggregated into a continuous anomaly score, with isotonic projection enforcing ordinal consistency. Experiments on public VAD benchmarks demonstrate superior performance with low computational cost. Probe-VAD provides a simple interface for translating frozen VLM visual understanding into continuous, rank-sensitive anomaly scores without task-specific training or caption-based compression. Code is available at: https://github.com/yvestine/COVAS-VAD.

</details>


### [79] [InfoTaxa: Information-Calibrated Label-Free Clustering for Fine-Grained Visual Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17218)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过信息校准的聚类分析研究BIOSCAN-5M上冻融预训练视觉嵌入的无标签聚类，发现物种级别长期存在"聚类受限+表征受限"双重瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签聚类为生物多样性监测提供可扩展路径，但仅用图像的细粒度分类学聚类存在一致的"由粗到细"失效模式：聚类能恢复大类结构，却在物种级别停滞。作者希望判断这种停滞是方法受限还是信息受限。

Method: 在BIOSCAN-5M上采用BioCLIP~2特征配合UMAP与HDBSCAN进行聚类，并与oracle-K、图方法、学习式聚类头对比；提出InfoTaxa，结合"聚类效率"（无监督划分所恢复的探针估计图像信息比例）与成对DNA作为仅用于审计、不参与推理的信号，并做多种鲁棒性分析。

Result: 密度管道在科与属的AMI分别达0.79与0.67，优于先前图像基线；在目与科分别恢复约0.90与0.81的图像可用信息；留出晚期融合探针显示加入DNA可在物种级别降低约2比特预测误差。

Conclusion: 在测试设定下，物种级无标签聚类同时受聚类与表征限制：改进聚类可恢复更多图像暴露的结构，但仅靠图像无法弥合DNA审计所揭示的信息缺口。

Abstract: Label-free clustering of frozen pretrained visual embeddings offers a scalable route to biodiversity monitoring, but image-only fine-grained taxonomy exhibits a consistent coarse-to-fine failure mode: clusters recover broad taxonomic structure yet plateau at species level. We study this behaviour on BIOSCAN-5M through an information-calibrated clustering analysis. BioCLIP~2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN reach $0.79$ AMI at family and $0.67$ at genus, substantially improving over the prior image baseline and remaining competitive with oracle-$K$, graph-based, and learned clustering heads on the same frozen features. To diagnose whether the remaining plateau is method-limited or information-limited, we introduce InfoTaxa, which combines clustering efficiency---the fraction of probe-estimated image information recovered by an unsupervised partition---with paired DNA as an audit signal only, not an inference input. The density pipeline recovers approximately $0.90$ and $0.81$ of the image-available information at order and family, respectively. Held-out late-fusion probes show that adding DNA to the image embedding reduces species-level prediction error by approximately two bits. Robustness analyses cover multiple image encoders, described-species and rare-class subsets, probe diagnostics, and held-out-species coarse-rank generalisation and same-species retrieval. Thus, in the tested setting, species-level label-free clustering is both clustering-limited and representation-limited: improved clustering may recover additional image-exposed structure, but cannot close the DNA-audited information gap alone.

</details>


### [80] [FROD: Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17227)
*Yanbin Hou,Biao Xiong,Guojun Xu,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将甲骨文解读辅助任务建模为跨时代图像翻译，提出FROD方法（特征匹配残差去噪），在增强字符分离数据集上Top-1识别准确率超过基线，比OBSD提升3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲骨文解读传统上依赖领域专家通过语义语境与字形演变分析，过程劳动密集。作者希望借助计算方法辅助这一过程，将其形式化为跨时代图像翻译任务。

Method: FROD包含三部分：1）训练时利用快速特征匹配提供门控分割监督，高匹配对采用分块处理以对齐细粒度部首，低相似对整体训练以避免错配伪影；2）残差去噪扩散模型（RDDM）联合估计噪声与残差信号，减少位置漂移和笔画紊乱；3）多阶段字体风格化精修网络去除边缘噪声、稳定笔画结构。

Result: 在增强的字符分离数据集上，FROD取得高于所评估基线的Top-1识别准确率，相比OBSD有3.8%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: FROD通过特征匹配门控监督与残差去噪扩散机制，有效缓解了甲骨文到现代汉字的跨时代翻译中的错配与笔画紊乱问题，验证了其作为甲骨文解读辅助工具的有效性。

Abstract: Oracle bone script (OBS), one of the earliest Chinese writing systems, plays an important role in the study of Chinese etymology. Traditional decipherment relies heavily on domain experts who analyze characters through semantic context and structural evolution. To assist this labor-intensive process, we formulate OBS decipherment assistance as a cross-era image translation task and propose FROD (Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher). Although many OBS characters differ substantially from their modern counterparts, they often preserve local topological invariants at the radical level. During training, FROD leverages fast feature matching to provide gated segmentation supervision: paired samples with sufficient matches are processed patch-wise to align fine-grained radicals, whereas low-similarity pairs are trained holistically to avoid mismatched artifacts. In addition, a Residual Denoising Diffusion Model (RDDM) jointly estimates noise and residual signals, thereby reducing the positional drift and stroke disorder commonly observed in standard diffusion models. Finally, a multi-stage font stylization refinement network refines the generated images by eliminating edge noise and stabilizing stroke structures. On our augmented character-disjoint dataset, FROD achieves higher Top-1 recognition accuracy than the evaluated baselines, with a 3.8% absolute gain over OBSD.

</details>


### [81] [DecoGS: Adaptive Static-Dynamic Decoupling of 3D Gaussians for Free-Viewpoint Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17230)
*Idil Sulo,Alexey Supikov,Ilke Demir,Sainan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoGS 提出一种高效在线训练 3D 高斯的方法，通过自适应地仅优化发生运动或光度变化的时空区域，避免对静态区域冗余更新，实现流式视频重建的速度与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 3D 重建需要兼顾速度与时序保真度，但现有方法每帧都更新所有高斯，即使在静态区域也进行更新，导致闪烁和漂移等冗余问题。

Method: DecoGS 引入自适应机制，选择性地将优化集中在出现运动或光度变化的时空区域；并结合区域感知的高斯管理，包括梯度门控和高效可见性过滤，以维持时序连贯性和紧凑的内存占用。

Result: 在 N3DV 与 MeetRoom 数据集上分别达到 34.55 和 31.60 dB PSNR，优于所有流式与离线基线；渲染速度 261 FPS，时序闪烁比最优先前方法低 70 倍，且无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦变化区域的选择性优化策略，DecoGS 在流式 3D 高斯重建中同时实现了高速度、高保真度与强时序一致性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction demands both speed and temporal fidelity, goals that existing methods undermine by updating every Gaussian every frame, even in static regions. We present DecoGS, a method for efficient online training of 3D Gaussians from streaming videos. Unlike prior methods that update the entire scene indiscriminately, DecoGS introduces an adaptive mechanism that selectively focuses optimization on spatiotemporal regions exhibiting motion or photometric changes. This targeted training strategy eliminates redundant updates that cause flickering and drift in nominally static regions, while enabling fast, high-fidelity scene updates. The pipeline further integrates region-aware Gaussian management through gradient gating and efficient visibility filtering to maintain temporal coherence and a compact memory footprint. On N3DV and MeetRoom, DecoGS achieves 34.55 and 31.60 dB PSNR respectively, outperforming all streaming and offline baselines, while rendering at 261 FPS with $70\times$ lower temporal flicker than the best prior method, requiring no large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [82] [Video-HolmesV2: Can MLLMs Reason with Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Evidence in Long Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17248)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Zipeng Wang,Yushe Cao,Chenhui Qiang,Shuaibing Cheng,Xuesong Yang,Sen Nie,Bowen Jiang,Wenchao Ding,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Xuehui Yu,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准与音频-文本引导的 Token 压缩框架，聚焦深度音视频耦合与基于证据的评测，解决现有视频理解评测过度依赖视觉、长上下文处理低效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型视频理解评测存在两大缺陷：一是过度依赖视觉启发式、边缘化听觉线索，使模型沦为"沉默观察者"，绕过真正的跨模态推理；二是标准密集采样导致"证据-上下文权衡"，增加帧数虽能捕捉证据，却带来注意力分散与 token 爆炸。因此需要新基准与新方法来推动深度音视频耦合推理。

Method: 提出三点：1）构建 Video-HolmesV2 基准，面向深度音视频耦合，采用基于证据的评测，要求模型以精确的时空音视频证据佐证答案；2）引入多模型交叉验证流程保证任务严谨性，并提出时空证据感知度量进行细粒度校准；3）提出音频-文本引导的 Token 压缩框架，融合任务意图与听觉锚点，蒸馏高价值推理线索以缓解长上下文噪声。

Result: 在评测中，即使强大的闭源模型准确率也低于 60%；所提方法优于同类开源全模态模型。

Conclusion: Video-HolmesV2 通过基于证据的评测与音视频耦合设计，揭示了现有模型在长叙事推理上的不足，同时其音频-文本引导 Token 压缩方法有效缓解长上下文噪声并提升性能，为深度音视频推理研究提供了新基准与方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated impressive video understanding, yet their ability to reason over long-form narratives is often masked by visual-centric evaluations and inefficient context processing. Existing benchmarks over-rely on visual heuristics while marginalizing auditory cues, effectively reducing models to "silent observers" that bypass genuine cross-modal reasoning. Moreover, standard dense sampling creates an evidence-context trade-off: increasing frames to capture evidence inevitably leads to attention distraction and token explosion. To bridge these gaps, we present Video-HolmesV2, a novel benchmark designed for Deep Audio-Visual Coupling. Unlike previous works, it enforces an Evidence-Based Evaluation, requiring models to justify answers with precise spatio-temporal audio-visual evidence, thereby reducing confounding effects of guessing and hallucinated evidence. To support this, we introduce: (1) a Multi-Model Cross-Verification pipeline to ensure task rigor; (2) a Spatio-temporal Evidence-Aware Metric for fine-grained calibration. Furthermore, we propose an Audio-Text Guided Token Compression framework. By fusing task intent with auditory anchors, our method distills high-value reasoning cues to mitigate long-context noise. In our evaluation, even strong proprietary models achieve below 60% accuracy, while our approach outperforms comparable open-source omni-models.

</details>


### [83] [Exploring 2D backbone effects for indoor semantic occupancy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17257)
*Shizhang Fanga,Wanling Yea,Qi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究RGB-D语义占据预测中2D图像主干的选择，发现更换图像主干带来的精度提升超过多种专门设计的3D占据架构或模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在EmbodiedScan等RGB-D流程中，图像编码器常被当作默认模块，但其特征正是后续采样到3D体素网格的视觉证据，因此有必要直接考察这一设计选择对最终3D占据预测的影响。

Method: 固定RGB-D投影、深度分支和占据头，仅替换图像主干，在受控设置下对比CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2与DINOv2四种编码器。

Result: mIoU差异显著：DINOv2为30.55%，BLIP2为29.49%，CLIP-ViT为24.33%，CLIP-ResNet为17.41%；更强的编码器无需改动下游3D融合流程即可超过原始EmbodiedScan ResNet-50基线。类别层面，DINOv2在布局与结构类更强，BLIP2在部分物体中心类接近，CLIP-ViT明显优于CLIP-ResNet。

Conclusion: 图像主干并非具身语义占据中的次要工程细节，而是最终3D预测变异的主要来源之一；CLIP特征以密集token形式暴露的方式对体素提升也很关键。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy prediction gives an embodied agent a voxel-level account of where space is free, occupied, and semantically meaningful. In RGB-D pipelines such as EmbodiedScan, the image encoder is often left as a default module, even though its features are the visual evidence later sampled into the 3D grid. We study this design choice directly. A central finding is that changing the 2D backbone improves occupancy accuracy more than several carefully designed occupancy architectures or modules. We keep the main RGB-D projection, depth branch, and occupancy head fixed, and replace only the image backbone. The compared encoders are CLIP-ResNet, CLIP-ViT, BLIP2, and DINOv2. Under the controlled setting, the measured mIoU changes substantially: DINOv2 obtains 30.55\%, BLIP2 obtains 29.49\%, CLIP-ViT obtains 24.33\%, and CLIP-ResNet obtains 17.41\%. The stronger encoders also exceed the original EmbodiedScan ResNet-50 baseline without modifying the downstream 3D fusion pipeline. Class-level results give a more detailed picture: DINOv2 is stronger on many layout and structural categories, whereas BLIP2 remains close on several object-centered classes. CLIP-ViT improves clearly over CLIP-ResNet, showing that the way CLIP features are exposed as dense tokens matters for voxel lifting. These results indicate that the image backbone is not a secondary engineering detail in embodied semantic occupancy, but a major source of variation in the final 3D prediction.

</details>


### [84] [Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification for Mitigating Object Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17269)
*Ziheng Ren,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法SSAV，通过跨查询语义稳定性与区域一致性验证多模态大模型的物体声明，有效缓解物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在用药辅助、无障碍感知和环境决策等场景中会提及图中不存在的物体，这类幻觉带来现实安全风险，需要一种可靠的物体声明验证手段。

Method: SSAV是免训练方法：聚合多个提示估计语义支持以降低对措辞的敏感度；查询诱导区域验证（QIRV）结合跨查询区域持续性、空间重叠和相对候选优势识别孤立高响应和分散定位；用几何平均融合语义与空间证据，任一支路缺乏支持时降低验证分数。

Result: 在三个基座模型和多种评估协议上有效缓解物体幻觉。LLaVA-1.5-7B在COCO、A-OKVQA、GQA上，POPE Popular平均准确率提升1.81个百分点，Adversarial提升3.17个百分点，CHAIRs从49.40%降至32.80%。

Conclusion: 跨查询语义稳定性和区域一致性可为物体声明提供可解释的外部视觉证据。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models generate natural-language responses from visual inputs, yet may mention objects absent from an image. In medication assistance, accessible perception, and environmental decision-making, such hallucinations can create real-world safety risks. We propose Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification (SSAV), a training-free method for verifying object claims. A visually grounded claim should remain stable across semantically equivalent queries and repeatedly localize to the same image region. SSAV aggregates multiple prompts to estimate semantic support and reduce sensitivity to query wording. Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) combines cross-query region persistence, spatial overlap, and relative candidate dominance to identify isolated high responses and dispersed localizations. A geometric mean fuses semantic and spatial evidence, lowering the verification score when either branch lacks support. Experiments on three base models and multiple evaluation protocols show that SSAV effectively mitigates object hallucination. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, accuracy averaged across COCO, A-OKVQA, and GQA improves by 1.81 and 3.17 percentage points under POPE Popular and Adversarial, respectively, while CHAIRs decreases from 49.40% to 32.80%. These results show that cross-query semantic stability and regional consistency provide interpretable external visual evidence for object claims.

</details>


### [85] [Optical-Flow Wingbeat Counting in MuJoCo: A Comparison of Convolutional, Spiking, and Attention-Based Temporal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17308)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在MuJoCo仿真中用Crazyflie虚拟相机拍摄三种扑翼模型的光流，比较卷积+时间卷积、脉冲网络和自注意力三种时序架构的拍翅计数性能，全对计数准确率在1.5 m为92.22%–96.67%，3.0 m为92.22%–95.00%，架构间差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器的视觉监测需要从运动强度和平均频率中区分出单次拍翅，即实现个体拍翅事件级计数，而非仅估计总次数或平均频率。

Method: 构建受控MuJoCo仿真：虚拟相机装在Crazyflie上，观测三种扑翼模型在1.5 m和3.0 m光学距离下的带符号光流，240个配对场景配置生成1440个片段，按场景3:1划分训练测试。共享空间卷积编码器分别接因果时间卷积网络、循环泄漏积分发放脉冲网络或因果自注意力；每个模型预测相位与活动度，再用统一的定向穿越事件计数器输出计数。六个已有卷积模型保留，十二个新模型冻结参数后生成测试预测。

Result: 1.5 m处全对计数准确率分别为96.67%、95.00%、96.67%；3.0 m处为94.44%、92.22%、95.00%。所有配对场景自助法区间均包含零，即架构间差异不显著。有7个远距离脉冲模型片段总次数正确但事件定时不匹配，说明总计数与事件级指标须同时报告。

Conclusion: 结果支持在测试设定下基于因果光流进行拍翅计数的可行性，并识别出边界敏感型误差；但不能据此得出架构优劣排序，也无法说明重复训练、真实飞行鲁棒性或硬件效率方面的表现。

Abstract: Visual monitoring of flapping-wing vehicles requires distinguishing individual wingbeats from motion strength and average frequency. This paper presents a controlled MuJoCo evaluation of wingbeat counting from signed optical flow observed by virtual cameras mounted on Crazyflie vehicles. Three flapping-wing models were recorded at optical distances of 1.5 and 3.0 m, producing 1,440 clips from 240 paired scene configurations with a scene-level 3:1 training-test split. A common spatial convolutional encoder was combined with a causal temporal convolutional network, a recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire spiking network, or causal self-attention. Each model predicted phase and activity, followed by the same directed-crossing event counter. The six existing convolutional models were retained, and all twelve new models were frozen before their test predictions were generated. Exact-count accuracies at 1.5 m were 96.67%, 95.00%, and 96.67%, respectively; at 3.0 m they were 94.44%, 92.22%, and 95.00%. All paired scene-bootstrap intervals for differences in exact-count accuracy included zero. Seven far-distance spiking-model clips had correct totals despite event-timing mismatches, demonstrating why total-count and event-level measurements must be reported together. The results support the feasibility of causal optical-flow counting in the tested setting and identify boundary-sensitive errors. They do not establish an architecture ranking across repeated training, real-flight robustness, or hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [PanoGS-SLAM: Panoramic 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17387)
*Yongqi Mao,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Xiangfei Guo,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoGS-SLAM是首个基于3D高斯泼溅的全景稠密SLAM系统，在球面域进行可微渲染与位姿优化，实现全向光度约束，跟踪精度与渲染质量均优于基线，并达到实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时稠密SLAM对动态或快速变化环境中的鲁棒定位与高质量建图至关重要。现有基于3DGS的SLAM多面向窄视场针孔相机，角度覆盖有限，削弱位姿可观测性，在快速运动和大幅视角变化下光度优化不稳定。作者希望利用全景相机的全向感知能力解决这一问题。

Method: 在球面域直接进行可微渲染与位姿优化，提供全向光度约束；提出球面一致光度损失以补偿等距柱面投影的面积畸变；提出深度引导的高斯初始化策略，稳定新观测区域的增量建图。

Result: 在真实与合成全景基准（PALVIO、SynPano）上，跟踪精度与渲染质量持续优于几何类及GS类基线，前端收敛快且具备实时性；受控视场实验显示随角度覆盖增大，优化条件与收敛稳定性呈清晰单调改善。

Conclusion: PanoGS-SLAM验证了全景3DGS稠密SLAM的可行性，并揭示感知几何对可微高斯SLAM优化景观的根本性作用。源代码将公开。

Abstract: Real-time dense SLAM is a core capability for robotics applications that require robust localization and high- quality mapping in dynamic or fast-changing environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based SLAM methods have shown promising performance, but most are designed for narrow-FoV pinhole cameras, where limited angular coverage weakens pose observability and often leads to unstable photo- metric optimization under rapid motion and large viewpoint changes. We present PanoGS-SLAM, the first panoramic dense SLAM system built on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method per- forms differentiable rendering and pose optimization directly in the spherical domain, enabling omnidirectional photometric constraints for more stable tracking. To improve geometric consistency and robustness, we introduce (1) a sphere-consistent photometric loss that compensates for the area distortion of equirectangular projection, and (2) a depth-guided Gaussian initialization strategy that stabilizes incremental mapping in newly observed regions. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic panoramic benchmarks (PALVIO and SynPano) show that PanoGS-SLAM consistently outperforms geometric and GS-based baselines in tracking accuracy and rendering quality, while achieving fast front-end convergence and real-time perfor- mance. In addition, controlled field-of-view experiments reveal a clear monotonic improvement in optimization conditioning and convergence stability as angular coverage increases, high- lighting the fundamental role of sensing geometry in shaping the optimization landscape of differentiable Gaussian-based SLAM. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [SSC-Priors: Exploring Semantic and Visibility Priors to Boost Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17413)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 无需重新设计网络架构，仅通过向现有点云语义场景补全（SSC）网络输入额外数据——语义伪标签和传感器可见性信息——即可显著提升性能，使旧模型在SemanticKITTI上可与SOTA媲美，并在SSCBench-nuScenes上同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来SSC方法多追求架构创新，导致模型日益笨重复杂（如联合训练点云语义分割分支）。作者希望"后退一步"，寻找无需复杂架构改造、仅靠简单先验就能提升现有SSC网络性能的易行策略。

Method: 提出两种简单"配料"作为额外输入：（1）来自现成分割器的语义伪标签，直接附加到输入点云上；（2）传感器可见性信息，区分空空间（雷达与扫描点之间）和未知空间（视线之外）。两者仅需对原架构做极小适配。此外，通过oracle对比研究语义先验质量对mIoU的影响，并系统探索可见性信息的表示设计空间。

Result: 语义伪标签显著提升现有SSC模型性能；高质量语义先验是语义增益（mIoU）的主要驱动力，SSC模型仅用真值语义训练一次即可免重训地配合任意分割器使用。可见性信息在多种架构上带来二次提升，并界定剩余提升空间。在SemanticKITTI上，四种架构的旧模型达到与SOTA相当的水平，其中一个甚至超越SOTA；在SSCBench-nuScenes的32线稀疏传感器上两种先验同样可迁移。

Conclusion: 简单的内容先验（语义伪标签与可见性信息）是一种低成本、易迁移的SSC性能提升手段，无需复杂架构重设计，可让老模型重焕竞争力并跨数据集、跨传感器配置泛化。

Abstract: This paper investigates easy strategies to boost the performance of existing networks for lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. The fact is that, over the last years, SSC methods have mostly pursued architectural innovations, making the models heavier and more complex, e.g., by jointly training a point cloud semantic segmentation branch. In this work, we take a step back and explore two priors used as simple ingredients (possibly noisy) to improve existing approaches: semantic pseudo-labels and sensor visibility information. Concretely, we provide both kinds of information directly as additional inputs to a given SSC network, requiring only a minimal adaptation of the original architecture. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmenters significantly improves the performance of existing SSC models. In fact, by evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of semantic gains (mIoU), and that the SSC model can be trained just once with ground-truth semantics and then exploited without retraining using any segmenter. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar point cloud with visibility information that distinguishes between empty spaces (between the lidar and a scanned point) and unknown spaces (outside of lines of sight), providing a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. We study the design space of data for representing visibility information and bound the remaining headroom with a ground-truth oracle on the free-space labels. On SemanticKITTI, these enhancements make older models competitive with state-of-the-art systems across four architectures, in one case even outperforming them. On the SSCBench-nuScenes benchmark, both priors also transfer with the sparser 32-beam sensor.

</details>


### [88] [SlotDiT: Object-Centric Representations for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17414)
*Gjergj Plepi,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotDiT，一种在object-centric slot隐空间中进行文本引导扩散的Diffusion Transformer，用于机器人视频生成与场景动态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件潜在扩散模型依赖像素级或VAE隐表示，缺乏显式语义结构，representation space的影响未被充分探索。Slot-based object-centric表示虽在动力学建模与规划中有效，但尚未用于扩散生成建模。

Method: SlotDiT将给定参考图像和语言指令分解为object-centric slots，在指令与场景上下文条件下自回归地去噪未来slot轨迹以预测场景动态。作者在统一DiT框架下系统对比slot-based、VAE-based与semantics-aligned隐表示。

Result: 使用slots作为DiT隐变量可在四个机器人数据集上取得有竞争力的视频生成质量，并一致提升任务完成率，同时更紧凑、计算更高效。

Conclusion: object-centric结构是机器人环境下扩散生成建模的有效归纳偏置。项目页：https://slot-dit.github.io/

Abstract: Text-conditioned latent diffusion models perform strongly in video generation and are promising backbones for robotic applications. However, existing approaches rely on pixel-level or VAE-based latent representations that lack explicit semantic structure, leaving the impact of the representation space largely unexplored. Slot-based object-centric representations offer a structured alternative by decomposing scenes into object-level latents, or slots. While they have shown success in dynamics modeling and planning, they have not yet been explored for diffusion-based generative modeling. We introduce SlotDiT, a text-guided Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in a slot-based latent space. Given a reference image and a language instruction, SlotDiT decomposes the scene into object-centric slots representing individual entities. Conditioned on the instruction and observed scene context, the model autoregressively denoises future slot trajectories to predict scene dynamics. To systematically investigate latent-space design for diffusion transformers, we compare slot-based representations against VAE-based and semantics-aligned alternatives within a unified DiT framework. Our experiments show that using slots as DiT latents yields competitive video generation quality while consistently improving task-completion rates across four robotic datasets. Furthermore, their compact representation provides a computationally efficient alternative to VAE-based and semantics-aligned latent spaces. Overall, our results demonstrate that object-centric structure is a powerful inductive bias for diffusion-based generative modeling in robotic environments. The project page is available at https://slot-dit.github.io/.

</details>


### [89] [Tracking the Unseen: An Occlusion-Robust Framework for Target Tracking Under Full and Long-Term Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17427)
*Mais Mohammed,Sharifa Mohammed,Hanan Awadh,Haneen Bamaas,Raghad Bawazeer,Elham Alghamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种抗遮挡多目标跟踪框架，融合YOLOv11n检测、卡尔曼滤波预测与遮挡感知外观重识别，在OVIS及自定义数据集上显著优于OccluTrack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多目标跟踪系统在完全遮挡和长期遮挡下极易失效，目标暂时或完全离开视野时，传统跟踪器会过早终止轨迹，导致身份丢失、态势感知能力下降，这在防务与监控等应用中尤为关键。因此需要一种能在可见性丧失期间保持目标身份与轨迹连续性的跟踪框架。

Method: 框架包含三个阶段：目标检测、遮挡期间位置估计、目标重现后的身份恢复。具体集成YOLOv11n目标检测、卡尔曼滤波运动预测以及遮挡感知的外观重识别。在相同跟踪框架与相同条件下评估了六种Re-ID架构，其中遮挡感知掩码网络（OAMN）综合性能最佳，被选入最终流程。

Result: 在公开OVIS数据集上对比OccluTrack，MOTA相对提升18.1%，IDF1相对提升25.1%，身份切换减少12.8%。在模拟监控与战场类环境、含长期遮挡的自定义数据集上，MOTA达0.734、IDF1达0.729，较OccluTrack相对提升14.2%和5.8%。

Conclusion: 该系统在困难遮挡条件下展现出较强的跟踪连续性、稳健的身份保持与可靠的轨迹估计能力，验证了其在可见性丧失时仍需连续跟踪的防务类监控应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time multi-object tracking systems remain highly vulnerable to full and long-term occlusion, where targets temporarily or completely disappear from the camera's field of view. Conventional trackers may terminate trajectories prematurely, resulting in identity loss and reduced situational awareness in applications such as defense and surveillance. This work proposes an occlusion-robust target tracking framework that maintains target identity and trajectory continuity through the integration of YOLOv11n object detection, Kalman Filter motion prediction, and occlusion-aware appearance-based re-identification. The framework consists of three stages: object detection, position estimation during occlusion, and identity recovery after target reappearance. Six Re-Identification (Re-ID) architectures were evaluated within the same tracking framework under identical conditions, with the Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) achieving the best overall performance and therefore selected for the final pipeline. The framework was benchmarked against OccluTrack on the public OVIS dataset, achieving relative improvements of 18.1 percent in Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA) and 25.1 percent in Identity F1 Score (IDF1), while reducing identity switches by 12.8 percent. On a custom military dataset simulating surveillance and battlefield-like environments with long-term occlusion, the framework achieved a MOTA of 0.734 and an IDF1 of 0.729, corresponding to relative improvements of 14.2 percent and 5.8 percent over OccluTrack. The system demonstrated strong tracking continuity, robust identity preservation, and reliable trajectory estimation under challenging occlusion conditions, highlighting its effectiveness for defense-related surveillance applications requiring continuous target tracking during visibility loss.

</details>


### [90] [BrainFocus: EEG-Guided ROI Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17443)
*Yihui Peng,Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BrainFocus，一种可靠的 EEG 引导高效 VLM 框架，通过脑电信号预测目标类别、YOLO 定位 ROI，仅在置信度达标时裁剪输入，在提升 VQA 准确率的同时大幅降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理大型杂乱图像时，VLM 计算开销大，而实际相关区域往往很小。EEG 信号能提供人类对视觉刺激的语义线索以指示感兴趣区域（ROI），但 EEG 引导的视觉类别解码尚不完美，直接进行 ROI 路由并不可靠，因此需要一种更稳健的引导机制。

Method: BrainFocus 使用 EEG 分类器预测目标类别，用 YOLO 检测器定位匹配的 ROI。仅当两个预测都通过置信度阈值时，VLM 才只处理裁剪后的 ROI；否则处理完整图像。评估上基于 EEG-ImageNet 构建 40 类基准，包含生成的杂乱图像与真实物体中心图像，配有目标 ROI 标注及 600 个英文视觉问答对。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-VL 2B、4B、9B 模型上，BrainFocus 在杂乱场景中使 VQA 准确率提升 4.14–9.87 个百分点；同时输入 token 与总 token 减少 23.2%–39.4% 和 23.2%–39.3%，端到端浮点运算量（FLOPs）降低 23.2%–39.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使 EEG 的语义解码并不完美，EEG 仍能有效引导 VLM 的高效推理，实现准确率与效率的双赢。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual question answering (VQA) performance, but processing large cluttered images is computationally expensive when only a small region is relevant. Electroencephalography (EEG) signals, which capture human neural responses to visual stimuli, can provide a human-derived semantic cue about the region of interest (ROI). However, EEG-guided visual category decoding remains imperfect, making direct ROI routing unreliable. In this work, we propose BrainFocus, a reliable EEG-guided efficient VLM framework for VQA. An EEG classifier predicts a target category, and a YOLO detector localizes the matching ROI. The VLM receives the cropped ROI only when both predictions pass confidence thresholds; otherwise, it processes the full image. For evaluation, we build on EEG-ImageNet to construct a 40-class benchmark comprising generated cluttered images and real object-centric images, with target-ROI annotations and 600 English visual question-answer pairs. Across Qwen3.5-VL 2B, 4B, and 9B models, BrainFocus improves VQA accuracy by 4.14-9.87 percentage points (pp) on cluttered scenes while reducing input tokens and total tokens by 23.2%-39.4% and 23.2%-39.3%, and end-to-end floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 23.2%-39.5%. These results demonstrate that EEG can guide efficient VLM inference even when its semantic decoding is imperfect.

</details>


### [91] [ORCA: Occlusion-Aware Refinement and Completion for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17450)
*Weronika Jakubowska,Maciej Zięba,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA 通过引入遮挡感知的 3D 高斯锚点表示，区分小空洞与大区域，优先用已有 RGB-D 信息修复，仅在必要时才使用生成式补全，从而减少幻觉、提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图新视角合成本质上是病态问题：当相机偏离输入视角时，原本被遮挡的区域会暴露出来，产生缺失几何与空洞。现有方法普遍依赖生成模型补全这些区域，但许多伪影其实只是深度边界附近的小缝隙，并不需要生成全新的场景内容，过度生成会引入幻觉、破坏原始内容与结构。

Method: 提出 ORCA（遮挡感知方法），用于从单图重建并补全可探索的 3D 场景。首先利用单目深度将 3D 结构引入高斯锚点表示，同时保持原始相机射线对应关系。在场景探索中按缺失区域的大小与结构分类处理：小范围去遮挡用重建中已有的 RGB-D 信息修复，生成式修补只保留给无法可靠恢复的大区域。新增高斯锚点在局部添加并优化，不改动已有表示。

Result: 在 DIV2K 数据集上，ORCA 在所有报告指标上均优于 VistaDream：MUSIQ 从 61.60 提升至 68.71，CLIP-IQA 从 0.474 提升至 0.574，表明相当多的新视角伪影可通过复用场景中已有信息有效修复。

Conclusion: 许多新视角合成伪影并不需要生成新内容，只需复用重建场景中已有的信息即可修复。通过降低对生成式修补的不必要依赖，ORCA 有效抑制了生成导致的幻觉，更好地保持了原始场景的内容与结构。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis from a single image is a fundamentally ambiguous problem. As the camera moves away from the input viewpoint, previously hidden regions become visible, exposing missing geometry and holes in the reconstructed scene. Existing methods often rely on generative models to complete such regions. However, many of these artifacts are small gaps near depth boundaries and do not require generating new scene content.
  In order to eliminate expensive process of generating image we introduce ORCA, an occlusion-aware method for reconstructing and completing explorable 3D scenes from a single image. ORCA first introduces 3D structure into a Gaussian-anchor representation using monocular depth while preserving the original camera-ray correspondence. During scene exploration, missing regions are handled based on their size and structure. Small disocclusions are repaired using RGB-D information already available in the reconstruction, while generative inpainting is reserved for larger regions that cannot be reliably recovered from the scene. New Gaussian anchors are added and optimized locally without modifying the existing representation. By reducing unnecessary reliance on generative inpainting, ORCA limits generation-induced hallucinations and better preserves the content and structure of the original scene.
  On DIV2K, ORCA improves novel-view quality over VistaDream across all reported metrics, increasing MUSIQ from 61.60 to 68.71 and CLIP-IQA from 0.474 to 0.574. These results show that many novel-view artifacts can be repaired effectively by reusing information already present in the reconstructed scene.

</details>


### [92] [Tables Decoded: DELTA for Structure, TARQA for Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17458)
*Jahanvi Rajput,Dhruv Kudale,Saikiran Kasturi,Utkarsh Verma,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DELTA 与 TARQA，用结构化文本表示（OTSL）替代图像化 VLM 方案，统一处理表格重建与表格问答，在多语言场景下更具扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格理解方法主要依赖视觉语言模型处理表格图像，存在处理难度大、与 LLM 对齐不自然、需要语言相关视觉编码器等问题，难以适配多语言文档。

Method: DELTA 将物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别与 OCR 解耦，精确抽取布局与内容，并以紧凑统一的 OTSL 格式编码单元格排列与文本；进一步基于 OTSL 序列微调 LLM，得到 TARQA。

Result: 在 FinTabNet、PubTabNet、PubTables-1M 上，DELTA 的 TEDS-Structure 与最先进方法相当；在自建印地语基准 TORQUE 上验证鲁棒性；WTQ 提升 9.3 个百分点，FinTabNetQA 提升 9.2 个百分点；在 TORQUE 上排名第二。

Conclusion: 结构化文本表示是表格理解中比图像 VLM 更可扩展有效的路径，OTSL 可统一支撑表格重建与表格问答，且在多语言场景表现良好。

Abstract: Table understanding is a core task in document intelligence, encompassing two key subtasks: table reconstruction and table visual question answering (TabVQA). While recent approaches predominantly rely on vision- language models (VLMs) operating on table images, we propose a more scalable and effective alternative based on structured textual representations. These representations are easier to process, align more naturally with LLMs, and eliminate the need for language-specific visual encoders, making them particularly suitable for multilingual documents. We present DELTA, which separates physical structure recognition, logical structure recognition, and OCR to extract both layout and content accurately. DELTA outputs tables in Optimised Table Structure Language (OTSL), a compact and unified format that encodes cell arrangements and textual content. On table structure recognition (TSR), DELTA achieves TEDS- Structure scores comparable with state-of-the-art methods across FinTabNet, PubTabNet, and PubTables-1M. We further establish its robustness on non-English tables through our curated Hindi benchmark, TORQUE. Building on this, we introduce TARQA, an LLM fine-tuned on OTSL sequences. Our approach yields gains of 9.3 p.p. on WTQ (TabQA) and 9.2 p.p. on FinTabNetQA (TabVQA), respectively. On TORQUE, our method ranks second among all VLMs and DELTA + LLM variants. We release our code, models, and benchmark at: https://github.com/Tihiitborg/Tables-Decoded

</details>


### [93] [Det-LIME: Detector-Aware, Multi-Instance Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations for Automated Marine Mammal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17479)
*Jiayi Zhou,David W. Johnston,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Det-LIME，一种面向目标检测器的多实例可解释性方法，通过逐检测加权、邻近核与 IoU 匹配生成实例级、贴合边界框的解释，在海豹与海鸟检测任务上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱目标检测器在海洋哺乳动物研究中被快速采用，但可解释性技术很少融入保护工作流；且现有面向分类的可解释工具不适合含多个体或群居生物的检测图像，因为它们忽略场景中的多个检测，只输出单一实例结果、混淆个体间证据，且生成的分辨率低、生物学意义弱的可视化，难以用于调试、定向数据增强和精准数据采集。

Method: 提出 Det-LIME，即检测器感知的多实例 LIME 适配方法：结合逐检测加权、强调每个边界框邻近区域的邻近核，以及基于 IoU 的匹配以在扰动过程中追踪同一实例，从而生成实例特异、与边界框对齐的解释。在无人机航空影像的海豹检测任务上评估，并增加海鸟案例研究考察通用性，与 vanilla LIME、Stabilized LIME、Deterministic LIME 及基于梯度的归因方法进行对比。

Result: 使用 Attribution Ratio 和 Max Saliency Hit Rate 两个指标，Det-LIME 在多实例归因上持续优于对比方法；其解释分辨率更高、具备实例感知能力。

Conclusion: Det-LIME 的实例感知高分辨率解释能揭示模型输出依据，并支持后处理、调试，以及在建模和数据采集或增强方面的可操作改进。

Abstract: Despite the rapid uptake of black-box object detectors in marine mammal research and monitoring, explainability techniques are rarely integrated into conservation workflows. Furthermore, most classification-oriented explainability tools are ill-suited to detection tasks involving imagery of social organisms or those with colonial life histories, as they ignore multiple detections within a scene and produce single-instance outputs that blur evidence across individuals. These methods also generate low-resolution, often biologically irrelevant visuals, limiting their utility for debugging, targeted data augmentation, and refined data collection.
  We proposed Det-LIME, a detector-aware, multi-instance adaptation of Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) that produced instance-specific, box-aligned explanations by combining per-detection weighting, a proximity kernel that emphasizes regions near each box, and Intersection-over-Union-based matching to track the same instance across perturbations. We evaluated Det-LIME on aerial drone imagery for harbor seal detection, with an additional seabird case study to assess generality, and compared it with vanilla LIME, Stabilized LIME, Deterministic LIME, and gradient-based attribution methods.
  Using the Attribution Ratio and Max Saliency Hit Rate metrics, we showed that Det-LIME consistently improved multi-instance attribution. In practice, these higher-resolution, instance-aware explanations provide insight into model outputs and support post-processing, debugging, and actionable improvements in modeling and data collection or augmentation.

</details>


### [94] [PhysStream: Streaming Physics-Grounded Video Generation with Structured Scene Memory and Fine-Grained Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17521)
*Chuhao Chen,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang,Chen Wang,Qiao Feng,Sergey Tulyakov,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysStream，一种自回归的物理基础图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化场景记忆与稀疏速度增量信号实现生成中的交互式精细运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法存在两大局限：一是需要在生成开始前提供完整控制计划，无法在生成中途交互调整；二是使用像素空间信号，只能指定物体位置，而非物理动力学，难以刻画真实运动规律。

Method: PhysStream 采用两阶段训练：先对双向模型进行运动控制条件微调；再训练因果自回归模型，并引入结构化的场景记忆（位置图与目标跟踪图，均从先前生成的帧在线推导）。同时支持稀疏速度增量信号作为运动控制条件，以编码物理量，使模型学习底层动力学。该方法面向多物体桌面刚体场景，支持生成中控制。

Result: 在合成基准上，相比最强基线，运动分布距离（FVMD）降低 33%，轨迹误差降低 12%；在真实场景对比中，超过 85% 的情况下被人类评估者偏好。

Conclusion: PhysStream 实现了先前方法不支持的多物体桌面刚体场景生成中交互控制，通过物理量信号与结构化场景记忆提升了物理一致性与运动可控性。

Abstract: Interactive control for video generation is moving from coarse prompts toward fine-grained, physically meaningful manipulation of dynamic scenes. Yet existing controllable methods either require the full control schedule before generation starts, or use pixel-space signals that dictate object positions rather than physical dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose PhysStream, an autoregressive model for physics-grounded image-to-video synthesis that incorporates structured scene memory---positional maps and object tracking maps derived online from previously generated frames---and supports fine-grained motion control via sparse velocity-increment signals that encode physical quantities, letting the model learn the underlying dynamics. We train our model in two stages: a bidirectional model is first finetuned with motion-control conditioning, then a causal autoregressive model is trained with additional structured scene memory, further improving physical consistency. PhysStream enables interactive, mid-generation control over multi-object tabletop rigid-body scenes---a capability not supported by prior methods---reducing motion distribution distance (FVMD) by 33% and trajectory error by 12% over the strongest baselines on synthetic benchmarks, and is preferred by human evaluators in over 85% of in-the-wild comparisons. Please check our website for more details: https://czzzzh.github.io/PhysStream

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Few-Shot Degradation Is Not What It Seems: Behavioral Evidence, Representation Analysis, and a Random-Text Control Across 12 Models, 2 Tasks, and 2 Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15990)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究少样本提示为何有时会损害语言模型性能，通过区分提示长度效应与示例内容效应，提出内容偏移指标，发现表征因内容重构越多，少样本收益越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本提示有时反而降低语言模型性能，但原因未知。已有工作通过测量零样本与少样本模式下隐藏状态的变化来探究这一问题，但少样本提示长度远大于零样本提示，长度差本身就会导致表征变化，从而混淆了真正的示例内容效应。

Method: 在12个开源权重模型上评估两个乌克兰语任务（新闻分类和法律案件结果预测）。提出一种简单修正方法：用长度匹配的随机文本替换示例，测量由提示长度引起的表征偏移，然后将其减去，得到"内容偏移"指标，以隔离示例内容（而非长度）导致的表征变化。并在Llama 3.3 70B上通过掩码示例进行因果验证。

Result: 少样本提示效果高度依赖任务：同一批模型在新闻任务上提升+24个百分点，在法律文本上仅提升+3.4个百分点，且有两个模型性能下降。原始表征偏移无法预测少样本是否有帮助（r=0.20），而内容偏移可以预测（rho=+0.65，p=0.043）。在Llama 3.3 70B上掩码示例的因果实验验证了该发现，恢复的准确率超过零样本基线。

Conclusion: 示例内容导致表征重构越多的模型，从少样本提示中获益越多，这与直觉上的"失真"解释相反。该研究表明少样本提示的收益取决于模型是否利用示例内容来重构其内部表征，而非仅仅是表征偏移的大小。

Abstract: Few-shot prompting sometimes degrades language models instead of helping them, but why this happens is unknown. We evaluate 12 open-weight models on two Ukrainian tasks news classification and legal case outcome prediction and find that the effect is strongly task-dependent: the same models that gain +24 pp on news show only +3.4 pp on legal text, with two models degrading. To understand why, we look inside the models. Prior work measures how much hidden states shift between zero-shot and few-shot modes, but few-shot prompts are much longer, and that length difference alone moves representations. We propose a simple fix: replace demonstrations with length-matched random text to measure the shift caused by prompt length, then subtract it. The resulting metric content delta isolates how much the model's representations change because of what the demonstrations say, not how long they are. This changes the picture entirely: raw shift does not predict whether few-shot helps or hurts (r = 0.20), but content delta does (rho = +0.65, p = 0.043). Models that restructure representations more from demonstration content benefit more the opposite of the intuitive "distortion" explanation. Masking demonstrations in Llama 3.3 70B confirms the finding causally, recovering accuracy above the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [96] [The Functionalizer: Lossless Functional Decomposition for Subword Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15991)
*Connor Makowski,Willem Guter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Functionalizer无损预分词框架，将拼写变化分解为组合式操作码/操作数前缀流，实现词汇效率与结构感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准子词分词器要么将同一单词的不同拼写变体（如hello、Hello、HELLO、Héllo）视为无关词表项，导致嵌入空间碎片化；要么通过有损归一化丢弃这些变化。作者希望无损地处理拼写与结构变化，提升词表效率并保持结构信息。

Method: 提出Functionalizer，一种无损预分词框架，在分词前将拼写和结构变化分解为组合式的操作码/操作数前缀流：以规范基础词元（操作数）为底，前缀用Unicode私用区编码的参数化变换算子（操作码）。引入覆盖大小写（CAPITALIZE）、变音符号（13个专用操作码）和字符重复（REPEAT、MULTIREPEAT）的算子，且完全可逆。

Result: 在六个自然语言和代码语料库上，Functionalizer在无约束条件下实现完整语料覆盖并显著缩小词表，实际词表槽位需求最多降低16%。序列长度呈现明显的领域相关权衡：压缩缩进密集的代码序列，但膨胀自然语言散文序列。在25M参数GPT-2规模模型上的初步下游评估显示，该规模下Functionalizer大幅提升代码语法有效性、改善代码字符困惑度，同时在散文上保持相似的文本连贯性。

Conclusion: 功能分解可作为词表高效、结构感知语言建模的有效机制，并需在生产规模上进一步验证。

Abstract: Standard subword tokenizers either treat every orthographic variation of a word (such as hello, Hello, HELLO, and Héllo) as unrelated vocabulary entries, which fragments the embedding space, or discard this variation through lossy normalization. We present the Functionalizer, a lossless pre-tokenizer framework that factors orthographic and structural variations into a compositional opcode/operand prefix stream before tokenization: a canonical base token (operand) prefixed by parametric transformation operators (opcodes) encoded in the Unicode Private Use Area. We introduce operators covering casing (CAPITALIZE), diacritics (13 dedicated opcodes), and character repetition (REPEAT, MULTIREPEAT), which are fully reversible. Across six natural language and code corpora, the Functionalizer enables complete corpus coverage with significantly smaller vocabularies under unconstrained conditions, reducing actual vocabulary slot requirements by up to 16%. When looking at sequence lengths, we observe a sharp domain-dependent tradeoff: it compresses indentation-heavy code sequences but inflates natural-language prose sequences. Preliminary downstream evaluations on 25M parameter GPT-2 scale models show that at this scale, the Functionalizer drastically improves code syntax validity and improves code character perplexity while maintaining similar text coherence on prose. These findings demonstrate that functional decomposition can be an effective mechanism for vocabulary-efficient, structurally aware language modeling, and motivate further validation at production scale.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal Model Activation Policies for Inference Networks of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15992)
*Foivos Charalampakos,Md Ibrahim Ibne Alam,Iordanis Koutsopoulos,Koushik Kar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"推理网络"图框架，研究多个专家LLM协同推理的最优拓扑与激活策略，证明串联结构下最优激活具有阈值结构，可显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本高昂，实践中常将多个专家模型以集成或串联方式协同使用，但缺乏关于如何最优利用现有模型的原则性方法。自适应路由虽可将简单查询交给廉价模型、复杂查询交给昂贵模型，但仍缺少对如何最佳利用专家模型的清晰理解。

Method: 提出推理网络（inference networks）——一种基于图的框架，节点表示不同LLM，边表示条件式模型激活。从专家LLM串联的基本拓扑出发，各模型具有不同成本与专业水平（由模型置信度刻画）。将问题形式化为在满足目标性能约束下最小化期望推理成本的最优激活问题，并求解该问题。

Result: 证明该特殊类推理网络的最优激活策略具有阈值结构：先查询最低成本LLM，仅当置信度低于某阈值时才调用更昂贵的LLM。判别式任务的最优策略是一组阈值（每类一个阈值），生成式任务则为单一阈值。给出计算阈值的结构化方法及两类任务的实用置信度估计机制。在开源LLM上的实验显示，在满足指定性能预算的同时实现了显著的推理成本降低。

Conclusion: 推理网络为多专家LLM协同推理提供了原则性框架，阈值式激活策略在保证性能的前提下有效降低推理成本，为成本-性能权衡问题提供了理论指导与实用方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have rendered them necessary for Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, and their high inference cost motivates the study of cost-performance trade-offs. In practice, several expert LLMs are used in synergy for inference, either in an ensemble mode or in series, yet without a principled approach on how to best use the available models. An adaptive approach can route simple queries to cheaper LLMs and complex ones to more capable, costly models. However, a clear understanding on how to best leverage available expert models is missing. We introduce inference networks, a graph-based framework, where nodes denote different LLMs, and links denote conditional model activations. The inference network design problem is to determine the best topology, namely the best way to use the models that best addresses the cost-performance trade-off. We start from the basic topology of a series of LLM experts, each of which has a different cost and a different level of expertise, which is captured via model confidence. We formulate the problem of optimal activation of these models so as to minimize the expected inference cost subject to a target performance constraint. For this special class of inference networks, we prove that the optimal activation policy has a threshold structure: query the lowest-cost LLM first, and invoke the more expensive LLM only if the confidence falls below a defined threshold. For discriminative tasks, the optimal policy consists of a set of thresholds, one threshold for each class, while for generative tasks, it consists of a single threshold. We provide a structured method to compute the thresholds, and practical confidence estimation mechanisms for both task types. Experiments with open-source LLMs show substantial cost reductions while meeting the specified performance budget.

</details>


### [98] [Single Document Extractive Summarization using Domination in Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15993)
*Aamir Miyajiwala,Aabha Pingle,Sheetal Sonawane,Surajit Kr. Nath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超图支配集性质的单文档抽取式自动文本摘要新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本摘要（ATS）是信息检索中的重要任务，需将文档压缩并保留关键信息。现有基于图的方法多使用普通图，本文希望探索超图在抽取式摘要中的潜力，并与当前最优的图方法进行性能比较。

Method: 构建句子超图：每个句子作为节点，边为关键词或命名实体，连接所有包含该关键词的句子；每条边代表一个关键词或重要主题。随后采用贪心算法求解该超图的支配集，选出的句子集合即构成抽取式摘要。

Result: 摘要中未提供具体实验结果或量化指标。

Conclusion: 利用超图的支配性质可为单文档抽取式摘要提供一种新颖思路，并计划与现有最优图方法进行对比验证其效果。

Abstract: Automatic Text Summarization (ATS) in Natural Language Processing has been an important task in Information Retrieval. It compresses a document to create a summary that captures all the relevant and important information conveyed in the document. This study explores Hypergraph for extractive text summarization of single documents. Objective: This study explores a novel method of leveraging the property of domination in hypergraphs to generate an extractive summary and compare its performance with state of the art graph based methods. Method: Our work aims to generate an extractive summary by creating a sentence hypergraph where each sentence represents a node and the edge is a keyword or a named entity that contains the sentences in which it occurs. We generate a hypergraph where each edge is a keyword or an important topic and the nodes are sentences containing those keywords. Then we apply a greedy algorithm to find the dominating set of the hypergraph which will contain sentences that will form the extractive summary.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Undertow: How Ordinary Typos Break Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15994)
*Elad David,Max Fomin,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 轻微打字错误或标点缺失对人类理解无害，但会显著扰乱基于隐藏状态的恶意提示检测探针；本文量化了这种"潜流"扰动，并提出KV-cache fork方法加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对表面打字变化鲁棒，但用于检测恶意提示的隐藏状态探针却可能因同一编辑而失效。作者希望揭示并量化探针在面对此类普通输入扰动时的脆弱性。

Method: 作者通过探针读出向量的旋转与衰减几何进行测量，考察单位置与多位置聚合探针；并提出KV-cache fork：在用户消息后追加固定短后缀，使探针读取扰动下游的token。与扰动增强训练进行对比，并在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-4-E4B上验证几何规律。

Result: 同一编辑使扰动token处读出向量旋转43–56°，约10个下游token内衰减至15%以下；叠加约3个典型打字错误使单位置探针TPR@FPR=1%下降12.0pp，仅靠重校准无法弥补。多位置聚合可修复局部扰动（≤0.5损失），但对分布式扰动仅能减弱，注意力与max聚合仍下降约3.8pp。KV-cache fork将差距缩小95%（残余-0.6pp），优于扰动增强训练（-3.7pp）。

Conclusion: 普通打字变化会以可预测的旋转-衰减几何破坏隐藏状态探针，该现象在多模型上可复现；KV-cache fork利用扰动的快速空间衰减，有效且远优于扰动增强训练。

Abstract: LLMs handle ordinary typing variation fluently: a typo or missing punctuation leaves both user intent and the model's response substantively unchanged. Yet probes that detect malicious prompts by reading the model's hidden states tell a different story: the same edit rotates the readout vector by 43--56 at the perturbed token, decaying below 15% within ~10 downstream tokens. Stacking ~3 common typos per message cuts a single-position prompt-injection probe's TPR@FPR$=1% by 12.0pp, a gap recalibration alone cannot close. Multi-position aggregation cures localized perturbations (<= 0.5 loss) but only attenuates distributed ones, where even attention- and max-based aggregators still drop ~3.8pp. For single-position probes, we introduce a KV-cache fork: a short fixed suffix appended after the user message lets the probe read a few tokens downstream of the perturbation, exploiting its rapid spatial decay. This closes 95% of the gap (-0.6pp residual) -- an order of magnitude better than perturbation-augmented training (-3.7pp). The rotation-and-decay geometry replicates on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-4-E4B; probe evaluation is on Llama-3.1-8B. Code: https://github.com/eladd-ai/latent-undertow

</details>


### [100] [Bias Audits Detect Bias but Disagree on Ranking: Evidence from Ten Instruments and Ten Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15995)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault,Wei Zhang,Vitor D. de Moura,José O. Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验"不同偏见审计工具可互相比较"这一假设，发现十个工具能检测出偏见，但跨工具排名一致性等同于随机，说明它们测量的是不同构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI监管要求对高风险系统进行偏见审计，审计分数开始被用于模型排名。这两种用途都假设不同的审计工具测量的是同一事物且足够可靠以供比较，但这一假设从未被直接检验。

Method: 通过统一的推理网关，将十个外在审计工具用于同一组十个前沿模型面板，先测职业性别偏见，再测年龄与社会经济地位；并设计一个含六个刻意较弱模型的正对照来区分"不同构念"与"同一构念但有噪声"两种解释。

Result: 十个工具中有八个以置信区间明显偏离零检测出偏见，两个被广泛引用的直接探测基准因模型回答中性而饱和；但跨工具排名一致性等同于随机（Kendall's W=0.07, p=0.83）。正对照显示，当面板存在真实能力差距时工具内可靠性恢复，但跨工具排名始终不恢复。偏见方向还随审计格式分化：强制选择工具多过度纠正（倾向女性，278次招聘决策中273次倾向工薪阶层候选人），而自由生成与默认共指仍保持刻板印象一致。该模式在社会经济地位上可复现，年龄上的表面排名一致性按本文工具纳入规则后被消解。

Conclusion: 单次审计可在其自身操作化范围内检测偏见并估计偏向，但不支持用一个模型对另一个模型进行排名。所有原始回答、代码及可重算全部报告数值的分析均已开源。

Abstract: Emerging AI regulation mandates bias audits of high-risk systems, and audit scores are beginning to be used to rank models. Both uses assume different audit tools measure the same thing well enough to compare. We test that assumption directly, running ten extrinsic audit instruments over a shared panel of ten frontier models through one pooled inference gateway, first on occupational gender bias, then on age and socioeconomic status. Detection succeeds while ranking fails. Eight of ten tools detect bias with confidence intervals clear of zero; two widely cited direct-probe benchmarks are saturated because frontier models now answer neutrally. But cross-tool rank agreement is indistinguishable from chance (Kendall's W=0.07, p=0.83). A positive control with six deliberately weaker models separates two explanations: within-tool reliability recovers once the panel spans real capability gaps, yet cross-tool ranking never recovers, which points to the tools measuring different constructs rather than one construct noisily. Even the direction of bias splits by audit format: forced-choice decision tools mostly over-correct (toward women, and toward working-class candidates in 273 of 278 hiring decisions), while free generation and default coreference stay stereotype-congruent. The pattern replicates on socioeconomic status; an apparent ranking agreement on age dissolves under the paper's own tool-inclusion rules. The practical message: a single audit can detect bias and estimate its direction within its own operationalization, but no single audit supports ranking one model against another. All raw responses, code, and the analysis that recomputes every reported number from source are available at https://github.com/williamguey/bias-audit-agreement.

</details>


### [101] [Comment on arXiv:2607.01233: Survivorship Bias in Published-Paper Baselines for Research-Idea Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15996)
*Fredrik A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该评论指出，Chen、Zhao与Cohan对LLM生成研究想法的分布评估存在幸存者偏差：人类基线来自已发表论文，而LLM基线来自一次性提案，两者可比性存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评论旨在对原研究的识别策略提出更窄但关键的方法论质疑：人类基线与LLM基线在"筛选阶段"上不对等，可能导致对人类-LLM差距的误判。

Method: 通过逻辑论证而非实验：若"桥接型"或"综合型"想法更易生成、但更不易被发表，则已发表的人类基线会低估这类想法在未观察人类想法池中的占比，从而造成有偏比较。

Result: 观察到的"人类-LLM差距"可能部分甚至大部分源于幸存者偏差，而非真实的生成能力差异。原评估的分布结论因此可能被高估或误读。

Conclusion: 评估LLM研究想法时应使人类与LLM基线的筛选过程可比，例如引入未发表的人类提案作为对照，或对发表筛选机制建模校正，以避免幸存者偏差对结论的污染。

Abstract: Chen, Zhao, and Cohan introduce a valuable distributional evaluation of LLM-generated research ideas. This comment raises a narrower identification concern: their human baseline consists of published papers, whereas the LLM baseline consists of one-shot proposals. If bridge-like or synthesis-like ideas are relatively easy to generate but relatively unlikely to survive publication, then the published human baseline will understate their prevalence in the unseen human idea pool. The observed human--LLM gap may therefore be partly, or even largely, a consequence of survivorship bias.

</details>


### [102] [Crash Narrative-Guided Countermeasure Recommendation Using Large Language Models: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Intersection Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15997)
*Abu Saif Md Nasim Uddin,Mohamed Abdel-Aty,Zubayer Islam,Parvez Anowar,Chenzhu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于事故叙述引导的检索增强生成（RAG）框架，将事故机理自动转化为具体地点的对策建议，并在佛罗里达州115个交叉口数据上取得F1=0.82的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉口安全改善需识别事故机理并提出对策，但传统依赖专家判断，劳动密集、难以规模化，且受制于资深交通安全工程师的可获得性；同时事故叙述虽富含机理信息，却长期未被充分利用。

Method: 从事故叙述中提取交通控制、信号指示、驾驶员过错、车辆运动、行驶方向等机理属性，并与FHWA成熟安全对策及CMF Clearinghouse的循证治理措施关联。框架整合：基于嵌入的历史相似交叉口检索、关联规则挖掘、相关对策期望数量的统计指导，以及引导LLM按领域一致决策流程进行工程推理的指引，最终选择对策。

Result: 在佛罗里达州Lake与Sumter县115个交叉口的312起致命及严重伤害事故上，采用五折交叉验证，框架精确率0.82、召回率0.85、F1为0.82；每个地点平均推荐3.91条对策，其中3.14条匹配，接近实际平均值3.86。

Conclusion: 该框架证明检索增强LLM可作为可解释、可扩展的决策支持工具，帮助交通机构将事故叙述转化为对策建议。

Abstract: Improving safety at intersections requires identifying crash mechanisms and recommending appropriate countermeasures. However, this process traditionally relies on expert judgment, making it labor-intensive, difficult to scale, and dependent on the availability of experienced traffic safety engineers. Although crash narratives contain rich description of crash mechanisms, this unstructured information remains largely underutilized in safety analyses. This study presents a crash narrative-guided retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that translates narrative-derived crash mechanisms into site-specific countermeasure recommendations. Key mechanism attributes including traffic control, signal indication, driver fault, vehicle movement, and travel direction were extracted from crash narratives and linked to evidence-based treatments from the FHWA Proven Safety Countermeasures and the CMF Clearinghouse. The framework integrates embedding-based retrieval of historically similar intersections, association-rule mining, statistical guidance on the expected number of relevant countermeasures, and an engineering reasoning guidance that directs LLM through a domain-consistent decision process before selecting countermeasures. Evaluated on 312 fatal and serious-injury crashes across 115 intersections in Lake and Sumter Counties, Florida, using five-fold cross-validation, the framework achieved a precision of 0.82, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.82, while recommending an average of 3.91 countermeasures per location with 3.14 matching, closely matching the actual average (3.86). Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of retrieval-augmented LLMs as an interpretable and scalable decision-support tool for transportation agencies for translating crash narratives into countermeasure recommendations.

</details>


### [103] [Self-reported archetypes and behavioral failures in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15998)
*Tabia Tanzin Prama,Calla Glavin Beauregard,Christopher M. Danforth,Peter Sheridan Dodds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究将22个LLM的自评人格投射到六维原型空间，发现闭源模型呈现类人的连贯特质结构，开源模型自评较弱且矛盾，且自评与实际行为存在显著落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具有影响其交互、顺从、抵抗与出错方式的行为特质和道德偏好，但LLM"性格"的结构尚不清楚，因此需要一个可复现的、以性格为核心的评估框架。

Method: 选取22个模型（闭源前沿系统与开源Llama、DeepSeek、OLMo、Qwen系列），让每个模型在464组双极语义差异特质对上自评，再用Archetypometrics框架将画像投射到由2000个虚构角色众包评分导出的六维原型空间，并与开发者准则交叉比对。

Result: 闭源模型自评特质与人类评定的虚构角色的特质共现结构一致，围绕Hero、Angel、Traditionalist、Geek四个原型维度组织，最接近的类比为Data、Vision、Janet；开源模型自评较弱、噪声大且内部矛盾，位于原型空间中结构稀疏的弥散区域；自评与开发者准则间存在落差（幻觉削弱宣称的精准、谄媚影响宣称的善意、智能体失败违背宣称的服从）。

Conclusion: 自评不应被视为中立的性格测量，而是塑造模型行为的同一优化过程的结构化产物；该工作提供了以性格为基础、可复现的框架来评估LLM"是什么"而非仅"做什么"。

Abstract: Every large language model (LLM) has behavioral traits and moral preferences that comprise its character. Whether by design or as an emergent property of training, these systems exhibit persistent dispositions that shape how they interact, comply, resist, and err, yet the structure of LLM character remains poorly understood. We map the self-reported personality archetypes of 22 LLMs spanning closed-source frontier systems (GPT-4.0-5.2, Grok-3/4, Gemini 2.5 Pro/Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.5/4.6) and open-source models (Llama, DeepSeek, OLMo, and Qwen series). Each model self-rated across 464 bipolar semantic-differential trait pairs, and the resulting profiles were projected into a six-dimensional archetypal space derived from crowd-sourced ratings of 2,000 fictional characters using the Archetypometrics framework. Closed-source models' self-rating traits align with the empirical trait co-occurrence structure of human-rated fictional characters, suggesting coherent, human-like self-representations organized around combinations of four recurring archetypal dimensions: Hero, Angel, Traditionalist, and Geek. Their closest analogues include Data, Vision, and Janet. Open-source models show weaker, noisier, and internally contradictory self-representations, occupying a diffuse region of archetype space with weak structure. Cross-referencing self-reported profiles with developer constitutions reveals a consequential gap between claimed character and enacted behavior: hallucination undermines claimed precision, sycophancy complicates claimed kindness, and agentic failures contradict claimed obedience. These self-ratings should therefore be interpreted not as neutral measurements of model character, but as structured outputs of the same optimization processes that shape model behavior. This work provides a reproducible, character-grounded framework for evaluating what LLMs are, not just what they do.

</details>


### [104] [NepKANUN: A RAG-Based Nepali Legal Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15999)
*Bhabuk Thapa,Prasiddha Koirala,Ranjit Raut,Sunil Regmi,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向尼泊尔法律文本的AI法律助手，基于微调大语言模型并结合RAG框架，用自建问答数据集训练，在BERTScore上取得简单0.82、中等0.77、复杂0.71的F1分数，专家评审也验证了可用性，展示了生成与检索结合以普及法律知识获取的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔法律信息获取困难，主要受限于术语复杂、资源匮乏以及错误信息泛滥。

Method: 构建面向尼泊尔法律文本的AI法律助手，基于微调的大语言模型，并集成检索增强生成（RAG）框架以提供精准简洁的自然语言法律问答；使用自建高质量问答对数据集进行训练。

Result: 依据BERTScore评估，该模型在简单、中等、复杂问题上分别取得0.82、0.77、0.71的F1分数；专家评审进一步确认了其可用性。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦定制化法律数据并融合生成与检索（RAG），可有效推动尼泊尔法律知识获取的普及化。

Abstract: Accessing legal information in Nepal is difficult due to complex terminology, limited resources, and misinformation. We introduce an AI-powered legal assistant that is tailored for Nepali legal texts and is built on a fine-tuned large language model. The technology provides precise, streamlined answers to natural language legal inquiries when integrated into a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework. It was trained using a custom dataset of high-quality question-answer pairs, and according to BERTScore, it obtained strong F1 scores of 0.82 (simple), 0.77 (moderate), and 0.71 (complex). Its usability is further confirmed by expert reviews. Our method shows how merging generation and retrieval can effectively democratize access to legal knowledge in Nepal by focusing on customized legal data and incorporating RAG.

</details>


### [105] [Nepali Legal Expertise through Generative and Extractive Pre-trained Transformers (NepLEGiT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16010)
*Ranjit Raut,Tishya Dhakal,Aaryan Shakya,Bhabuk Thapa,Prasiddha Koirala,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NepLEGiT，一个从零预训练的尼泊尔语法律小语言模型（SLM），旨在提升尼泊尔法律服务的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔法律语言复杂、法律信息可及性低，加之语言障碍、信息碎片化以及法律专业人才（尤其农村地区）严重短缺，导致司法服务难以惠及众多公民。

Method: 基于约400万token的尼泊尔语法律文本语料，从零预训练一个约3000万参数的GPT-2解码器模型（6层、6头、384维），采用预热余弦衰减调度、梯度累积与混合精度训练；同时对mBERT和MuRIL进行持续掩码语言模型预训练以作编码器基线对比。

Result: NepLEGiT在验证集上交叉熵损失0.5684、困惑度1.8、下一词预测准确率82.9%；mBERT困惑度2.35（评估损失0.8565），优于MuRIL（困惑度6.07，评估损失1.8026）。

Conclusion: NepLEGiT作为面向尼泊尔法律领域的生成式SLM表现良好，所训练的mBERT可作为互补的强编码器基线，二者共同为尼泊尔法律服务普及提供支撑。

Abstract: The complexity of legal language and limited accessibility to legal information pose significant challenges to justice delivery in Nepal. Traditional legal services remain inaccessible to many citizens due to language barriers, information fragmentation, and a critical shortage of legal expertise, particularly in rural areas. We present NepLEGiT (Nepali Legal Expertise through Generative and Extractive Pre-trained Transformers), a specialized small language model (SLM) designed to democratize legal knowledge and enhance legal-service delivery in Nepal. We pre-train a decoder-based GPT-2 SLM from scratch on a curated corpus of ~4 million tokens of Nepali legal text, covering constitutional law, civil and criminal codes, and administrative regulations. The model comprises ~30 million parameters in a 6-layer, 6-head, 384-dimensional transformer trained with warmup cosine-decay scheduling, gradient accumulation, and mixed-precision arithmetic. On a held-out validation split, NepLEGiT attains a cross-entropy loss of 0.5684, a perplexity of 1.8, and a next-token prediction accuracy of 82.9%. We further evaluate continual masked-language-model pre-training of mBERT and MuRIL on the same corpus; mBERT achieves a perplexity of 2.35 (eval loss 0.8565), outperforming MuRIL (perplexity 6.07, eval loss 1.8026), providing a strong encoder baseline complementary to NepLEGiT's generative orientation.

</details>


### [106] [ViCo: Visual-oriented Coding with Self-Reflection for Chart Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16014)
*Jiaxin Duan,Dian Jiao Shuai Zhao,Jiabing Leng,Yiran Zhang,Feng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ViCo是一个面向学术图表生成的视觉导向编程训练框架，通过迭代反思使生成图表逐步对齐参考图，结合自监督预热与多步强化学习，在8B模型上接近专有LLM水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体虽能生成结构化文本和代码，但生成的图表在风格和语义保真度上远不及人类设计；采用自我反思机制的编码智能体视觉推理能力差、反思遵循度低，导致奖励信号稀疏，严重削弱强化学习效果。

Method: 提出ViCo框架：1）自监督预热阶段，在蒙特卡洛树搜索中引入一致性剪枝，合成高质量反思轨迹，确保每步编码严格遵循先前反思结果；2）多步强化学习算法，利用反事实基线估计每个精炼周期内反思与动作步骤的优势，缓解奖励稀疏；3）自动多面评估框架，通过分层异构布局图结构评估图表的风格、布局与语义一致性，实现大规模训练中的高效奖励计算。

Result: 在三个公开基准上的实验表明，基于8B模型训练的ViCo性能接近具备充分反思能力的专有LLM。

Conclusion: ViCo通过迭代反思与多步强化学习有效提升学术图表生成质量，验证了自监督轨迹合成与反事实优势估计在缓解奖励稀疏问题上的有效性，使中小规模模型达到接近专有大模型的图表生成水平。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of generating high-quality academic charts that match the visual standards of human-authored papers. While existing AI agents can produce well-structured text and code, their generated visualizations often lack the stylistic and semantic fidelity of human designs. Advanced coding agents that employ self-reflection mechanisms exhibit poor visual reasoning and limited reflection following, resulting in sparse reward signals that severely undermine their reinforcement learning (RL). We propose ViCo, a training framework for visual-oriented coding that employs iterative reflections to align generated chart images progressively with the reference.
