### [143] [RiskChainBench: A Benchmark for Obfuscated Platform Message Restoration and Evidence-Grounded Web Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16900)
*ZhuoXin Liu,Zhiming Ma,Ying Zhang,Mengzheng Yang,Yifan Wang,Zhengqi Huang,Yanhan Zhou,Zekun Lin,Jun Zhang,Shun Zhang,Yue Chen,Qiao Zhao,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RiskChainBench，将"混淆文本还原"与"风险网页调查"分为两阶段串联评估，用600个源会话生成3600个还原输入并配对600个人工标注本地网页环境，通过离线门控组合两阶段评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台滥用活动常用emoji、谐音、字符拆分和冗余符号隐藏跳转指令，将用户引向色情、欺诈、赌博或非法交易类服务。现有基准将混淆文本评估与风险网页评估相互割裂，无法揭示"目标还原质量如何影响下游证据获取"这一关键问题。

Method: 构建 RiskChainBench：将600个源会话配对3600个合成的token-text还原输入和600个人工标注的本地网页环境。模型先还原消息、操作意图与目标；同一底层模型再作为VLM驱动的网页代理，在无消息语义与域名信誉线索条件下调查正确关联的网站，并生成冻结的、带证据引用的风险报告。两阶段分别打分，并用冻结的primary-entry预测作为门控离线组合。任务正确性由人工标注判定，多维证据质量由固定多模态评判器评估。

Result: 十个模型中，Entry Top-1 从35.2%到95.2%，网页决策准确率从26.3%到62.8%；领先系统在入口还原、完整重构、网站决策和细粒度分类上各不相同。执行失败占网页运行的31.9%，而决策后类型错误仅占0.9%。

Conclusion: 稳定探索与风险判断是主要瓶颈，而非最终分类环节。作者开源基准、协议与可重置本地沙箱，支持对端到端风险链路进行可复现评估。

Abstract: Platform abuse campaigns conceal redirection instructions with emojis, homophones, character decomposition, and redundant symbols, then route users through disguised links to services associated with pornography, fraud, gambling, or illicit transactions. Existing benchmarks evaluate obfuscated text and risky webpages separately, obscuring how target recovery affects downstream evidence acquisition. We introduce RiskChainBench, pairing 3,600 synthetic token-text restoration inputs from 600 source sessions with 600 corresponding human-labeled local web environments. A model first restores the message, operational intent, and destination; the same underlying model then acts as a VLM-driven web agent that investigates the correctly associated website and produces a frozen, evidence-cited risk report without message-side semantics or domain-reputation cues. We score restoration and correct-routing web investigation separately and compose them offline by applying the frozen primary-entry prediction as a gate to the same Task 2 result. Human labels determine task correctness, while a fixed multimodal evidence judge assesses faithfulness, sufficiency, completeness, and consistency. Across ten models, Entry Top-1 ranges from 35.2% to 95.2% and web decision accuracy from 26.3% to 62.8%; the leading systems differ across entry recovery, full reconstruction, website decisions, and fine-grained typing. Execution failures account for 31.9% of web runs, whereas post-decision type errors account for only 0.9%, identifying stable exploration and risk judgment as the principal bottlenecks. We release the benchmark, protocol, and resettable local sandbox.

</details>


### [144] [Deconstructing Stereotypes: Scope-Conditioned Generation for Effective Multilingual Counterspeech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16906)
*Greta Damo,Elias Urios Alacreu,Elena Cabrio,Paolo Rosso,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于刻板印象特征条件化的反仇恨言论生成框架，通过将结构化刻板印象信息融入大语言模型提示，在多语言数据集上显著优于通用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动反仇恨言论（CS）生成方法常产生泛化、无效的回复，无法针对仇恨言论背后的隐性刻板印象，因此需要更精准的生成方式。

Method: 提出一种新颖的"范围条件化"生成框架，明确将结构化的刻板印象特征整合进大语言模型的提示中；并构建了一个经人工标注、覆盖英语、意大利语和西班牙语的新数据集用于验证。

Result: 大量评估表明，刻板印象条件化提示在三种语言上均显著优于通用基线，在事实性、具体性、说服力和有效性方面均取得显著提升，对显性和隐性刻板印象均有效。

Conclusion: 将结构化刻板印象特征融入提示可有效提升反仇恨言论生成的质量，为替代内容删除策略提供了更具针对性的方案。

Abstract: Counterspeech (CS) - direct responses that counter online Hate Speech (HS) using reasoning and alternative viewpoints - has emerged as an alternative to content removal. Current automatic CS generation methods, however, frequently produce generic, ineffective replies that fail to target the implicit stereotypes behind HS. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel scope-conditioned generation framework that explicitly integrates structured stereotype characteristics into Large Language Models prompts. We validate our approach on a novel, human-curated dataset annotated in English, Italian, and Spanish. Extensive evaluations show that stereotype-conditioned prompting substantially outperforms generic baselines across all three languages, obtaining significant gains in factuality, specificity, cogency, and effectiveness for both explicit and implicit implied stereotypes.

</details>


### [145] [Lit3R: Retrieve-Relate-Read for Evidence-Grounded Question Answering over Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16912)
*Akira Ise,Kotaro Kumagai,Yuta Yamaguchi,Hisanori Ozaki,Yukio Uematsu,Ikuya Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: tus-nlp 的 Lit3R 系统面向 LitTraceQA 文献问答任务，采用检索-关联-阅读流程，无需任务特定训练，在官方测试集排名第 4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LitTraceQA 要求系统检索相关论文、识别支撑证据并生成答案，需要一种能够在文献基础上完成问答的整体方案。

Method: Lit3R 组合现成的检索、重排和 LLM 组件，无需任务特定训练。检索器迭代融合 BM25 稀疏检索与稠密检索、cross-encoder 重排和 LLM 验证，并在基于问题的检索之外加入论文到论文的扩展。阅读器先在单篇论文内定位支撑证据，再跨论文综合证据，生成最终答案与证据链。

Result: 在官方测试集排行榜上，该系统排名第 4。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 无需任务特定训练的检索-关联-阅读组合方案可在文献问答任务中取得有竞争力的表现。

Abstract: We describe tus-nlp's Lit3R (Retrieve-Relate-Read) system for LitTraceQA, a shared task for literature-grounded question answering that requires systems to retrieve relevant papers, identify supporting evidence, and generate answers. Lit3R combines off-the-shelf retrieval, reranking, and large language model (LLM) components without task-specific training. The retriever iteratively combines BM25-based sparse and dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and LLM-based verification, and complements retrieval based on the question with paper-to-paper expansion. The reader first identifies supporting evidence within individual papers and then synthesizes evidence across papers to produce the final answer and evidence trace. On the official test set, our system ranked 4th on the leaderboard. Our code is available at https://github.com/tus-ist-nlp/littraceqa.

</details>


### [146] [HUMAID-NER: A Disaster Tweet Dataset for Joint Named Entity Recognition and Event Classification via Uncertainty-Weighted Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16964)
*Aijaz Ali,Nazish Basir,Sarfaraz Nawaz,Danish Nazir Arain,Haris Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个基于HumAID的灾害推文命名实体识别数据集HUMAID-NER，并提出联合多任务学习框架同时进行灾害实体识别与人道主义事件分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体结构化信息提取对人道主义响应很重要，但现有灾害推文资源主要只提供文档级类别标签，缺少跨度级实体标注。

Method: 构建HUMAID-NER数据集：6万条英文灾害推文，BIO格式，10类操作导向实体，约17.5万实体跨度，通过spaCy transformer、灾害领域EntityRuler规则和正则表达式组成的三阶段混合流水线并采用优先级重叠消解生成。提出基于共享RoBERTa-large编码器的联合多任务框架，使用同方差不确定性加权与两阶段训练（第二阶段冻结24层中下部18层）以缓解任务冲突。

Result: 在HUMAID-NER验证集上，NER跨度micro-F1达0.841，分类macro-F1达0.761，并通过实时网页仪表板实现端到端部署。

Conclusion: 数据集、模型与流水线代码均已开源，以支持可复现性和未来危机信息学研究。

Abstract: Rapid extraction of structured information from social media is important for humanitarian response, yet existing disaster tweet resources mainly provide document-level category labels without span-level entity annotations. We introduce HUMAID-NER, the first named entity recognition dataset built on the HumAID benchmark, containing 60,000 English disaster tweets annotated in BIO format across ten operationally motivated entity types and yielding approximately 175,000 labelled entity spans. Annotations are generated through a reproducible three-stage hybrid pipeline combining a spaCy transformer model, disaster-domain EntityRuler patterns, and structured regular expressions with priority-based overlap resolution. We also propose a joint multitask learning framework that performs disaster-specific named entity recognition and humanitarian event classification using a shared RoBERTa-large encoder. To reduce task conflict during joint training, the model uses homoscedastic uncertainty weighting with learnable task parameters and a two-stage training schedule that freezes the lower 18 of 24 encoder layers in the second stage. On the HUMAID-NER validation set, the proposed system achieves NER span micro-F1 of 0.841 and classification macro-F1 of 0.761 simultaneously. A real-time web dashboard demonstrates end-to-end deployment. The dataset, models, and pipeline code are released to support reproducibility and future crisis informatics research.

</details>


### [147] [Target-Language Generation in Multilingual Models: Activation Steering and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16967)
*James A. Michaelov,Carmen Amo Alonso,Tyler A. Chang,Roger P. Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最优控制的激活引导方法，用于多语言模型的目标语言文本生成，并构建了评估框架，在多数模型上效果不逊于差值均值法且超参数调优更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型在生成特定目标语言的连贯文本方面存在困难，且现有激活引导方法（如差值均值法）需要大量超参数调优，亟需更高效的控制方法。

Method: 提出一种用于目标语言文本生成的最优控制方法（激活引导），并建立从语言依从性、语言连贯性和语义连贯性三个维度评估生成文本质量的框架；与差值均值激活引导方法进行对比。

Result: 在测试的大多数模型上，所提方法表现至少与差值均值激活引导方法相当，且所需超参数调优显著更少。

Conclusion: 最优控制方法可在减少超参数调优的同时实现与主流方法相当的目标语言生成效果，为多语言语言模型的受控生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ensuring that multilingual language models generate coherent text in a specific target language is a major issue in multilingual language modeling. We develop an optimal control method for target-language text generation as well as a framework for evaluating the quality of generated text in terms of language adherence, linguistic coherence, and semantic coherence. We find that the proposed method performs at least as well as the prominent difference-in-means activation steering method for the majority of models tested, with substantially less hyperparameter tuning required.

</details>


### [148] [Nameless Tokenization: A Lossless Tokenizer-Level Defense Against Control-Token Forgery in Open-Weight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16984)
*Kisu Yang,Yoonna Jang,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文审计了256个已部署的聊天分词器，发现全部可被伪造回合边界；提出"无名分词"（nameless tokenization）方案，使控制标记无表面字符串，从而无法被内容编码器生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型的聊天模板字符串与保留标识符存在映射，任何能控制提示文本的人都能写出与serving stack所写无法区分的回合边界；现有推荐的修复标志因遗漏工具与推理标记仍留下56.6%的可伪造性。

Method: 审计256个已部署聊天分词器评估可伪造性；提出nameless tokenization，让控制条目保留保留标识符但无表面字符串，使内容编码器无法发出这些标记，从而消息内容原样到达模型；在五个分词器家族上进行验证。

Result: 在无攻击数据上精确复现标准token流；在含分隔符文本的探针上准确率从8.5%提升到59.9%，而sanitizer方案在此失败；分离分隔符外观与其标识符后发现：标识符对普通任务指令影响很小，但在系统消息指示将用户内容视为数据时，标识符承担了伪造工具结果与伪造回合的大部分作用。

Conclusion: 现有聊天分词器普遍存在回合边界伪造漏洞，且常用修复不充分；nameless tokenization在不影响正常数据的前提下消除了该漏洞，并揭示了标识符在伪造攻击中的关键角色。

Abstract: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.

</details>


### [149] [Autoformalizing Argumentative Material Inferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16991)
*Xin Quan,Reto Gubelmann,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出将论辩性实质推理的自动形式化建模为"守卫补全"（guard completion），并实现GUARD神经符号框架，在验证忠实度和泄漏降低上显著优于现有LLM定理证明方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言论证依赖于隐含的可废止担保、背景承诺和例外条件，而形式验证要求显式、严密的逻辑。将论证转为机器可检验需要构造缺失的承诺而非单纯翻译，但构造会带来风险：系统可随意添加前提从而使任意主张可证，导致形式有效的证明可能直接断言主张、脱离原始前提或证明超出主张本身。

Method: 提出将论辩性实质推理的自动形式化表述为"守卫补全"，即相对于显式构造的守卫集，把非单调的实质支持转化为单调形式推理。补全只有在证明同时通过定理证明器并通过前提依赖性对比测试与主张选择性对比测试时才被接受。实现GUARD神经符号框架：LLM构造并形式化候选守卫，Isabelle/HOL验证理论并返回步骤级反馈以迭代精炼，当无法达成忠实补全时系统弃权。

Result: 在Debatepedia和ARCT数据集上使用不同LLM，GUARD相比最先进的LLM驱动定理证明方法显著提升验证忠实度（+35.3、+32.9个百分点），并大幅降低泄漏（-25.9、-21.9个百分点）。消融显示符号软批评和显式假设层贡献了大部分增益，软批评还提升了所引出上下文的初始有效性并减少成功验证所需的迭代次数。

Conclusion: 通过守卫补全形式化，并借助对比性测试保证前提依赖与主张选择性，GUARD能够在自动形式化论辩推理时有效抑制前提泄漏与过度证明，实现忠实且可验证的机器可检验论证，同时具备弃权机制以应对无法忠实补全的情形。

Abstract: Natural language arguments are compelling before they are formally explicit. A premise supports a claim through defeasible warrants, background commitments, and exception conditions that the text leaves implicit. However, formal verification requires the opposite. Making such arguments machine-checkable requires constructing the missing commitments, not only translating given sentences into logic. Construction, however, carries a risk that translation does not: a system free to add premises can make any claim provable, and a formally valid proof may assert the claim outright, prove it without the original premise, or establish more than the claim itself. We address this problem by formulating autoformalization for argumentative material inference as guard completion, in which non-monotonic material support is turned into monotonic formal inference relative to an explicitly constructed guard set. A completion is accepted only when its proof both passes the theorem prover and survives contrastive tests of premise dependence and claim selectivity. We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [150] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控提示实验，测试了大型语言模型在教育评估任务中对学生人口统计特征的敏感度，发现显性和隐性人口信号均会改变模型输出，且隐性条件下产生不可预测的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已广泛用于学生评估，但学生人口统计特征如何影响其使用尚不明确。考虑人口特征有时是必要的（如为教育水平较低的用户提升可读性），但也存在歧视风险（如给社会经济背景较低的学生打更低分），因此需要系统探究这一影响。

Method: 设计受控提示，区分两种情形：1）显性人口效应，直接提及人口细节；2）隐性效应，用对话历史作为人口信号。在三个任务上测试：自动作文评分、形成性反馈、元语言问答。并在这些任务上评估六种最先进的大语言模型。

Result: 在显性和隐性两种情况下，模型都会捕捉人口线索并相应地改变评分、反馈和答案。当教育水平被明确提及时，模型频繁调整反馈的可读性以适应教育水平；而隐性条件则产生不可预测的偏见，例如在问答任务中，来自较低教育水平的回答获得更低的情感分数。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了明确证据，表明大语言模型在教育评估任务中对人口统计特征具有敏感性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了UNRESTSENT200K，一个约20万条Facebook和YouTube评论的孟加拉语危机情绪数据集，覆盖2024年7-8月孟加拉国事件的五个阶段，并提供父帖以便评估语境影响；通过14名母语标注者人工标注（kappa=0.73，alpha=0.71，盲审一致率94.2%），基准测试显示父帖语境提升性能、时间阶段迁移导致大幅下降，强LLM仍难处理讽刺、隐性指代与阶段性语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的危机情绪分析面临语言、语境与社会反应快速变化的多重挑战。现有资源缺乏事件对齐、时间敏感、带话语语境的危机情绪数据，尤其缺少标注质量可验证的孟加拉语数据集。

Method: 构建UNRESTSENT200K：从Facebook与YouTube采集约20万条评论，对应2024年7-8月孟加拉国事件的五个事件对齐阶段（早期升级、断网、政权过渡、后续洪灾危机等）；每条评论关联其父帖以支持有/无语境的对比评估。采用全人工标注流程，由14名孟加拉语母语者标注并经资深校验，报告kappa=0.73、alpha=0.71及94.2%盲审一致率。基准评测涵盖微调编码器、提示式LLM与LoRA微调LLM。

Result: 父帖语境一致提升模型性能；跨阶段时间迁移造成明显性能下降；强LLM整体表现良好，但在讽刺、隐性表述及阶段依赖语义上仍有困难。

Conclusion: UNRESTSENT200K为低资源危机话语中的语境感知与时间鲁棒情绪分析提供了基准，并已公开数据集。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at https://sami0055.github.io/UNRESTSENT200K/

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多跳问答失败可分解为检索失败与提取失败两类，后者占比近半且无法被检索改进解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将多跳问答失败归因于未检索到正确文档，但这一假设在单个推理步骤层面是否成立尚缺乏检验。

Method: 在三个标准多跳QA基准上进行实验，将每一跳的失败分解为检索失败（所需段落未被检索到）与提取失败（段落已检索到但所需事实无法被提取），并测试多种检索干预手段的效果。

Result: 提取失败占全部单跳缺陷的近一半，且对标准检索指标不可见；所有测试的检索干预方法均无法解决该问题，其严重程度因基准和问题类型而异，但在所测量的每个数据集上均存在。

Conclusion: 检索失败与提取失败是两种本质不同的瓶颈，需要不同的解决方案，而当前评估实践并未区分二者。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [153] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviScope 是一个配对反事实基准，通过固定问题、增删/干扰/矛盾其证据，暴露被最终答案准确率掩盖的模型接地行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接地语言模型系统多以最终答案准确率评估，但正确答案可能缺乏证据支持、来源于错误来源，或在证据不足或矛盾时仍被给出，因此需要评测证据与答案之间的真实支撑关系。

Method: 提出 EviScope，一个配对反事实基准，对同一问题添加、移除、干扰或矛盾其证据。EviScope-v1.1 含 40 组四条件四元组，包含修复后的反事实断言与 span 级支撑标签，支持自动评估；并在 Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash 上生成 960 条 gold-blind 输出进行配对指标分析。

Result: 配对指标揭示出答案准确率所掩盖的、依赖模型的接地行为。两个本地开源模型上，显式 evidence-action gate 在 QCS 上不如 vanilla RAG：Qwen 为 0.15 vs. 0.50，Llama 为 0.10 vs. 0.375。Gemini 在两种提示下均达到 0.944 的联合成功率，但在插入矛盾后仍对 5% 的冲突案例给出回答。

Conclusion: EviScope 能区分无证据回答、冲突盲视与错误的非回答动作，而非仅对答案打分，从而更细粒度地评估模型的证据接地能力。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [154] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过"反事实自解释"评估大模型自解释的忠实性，发现模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能轻易为自己的输出生成解释，但这些自解释未必忠实反映模型的真实行为，因此需要实证检验其可靠性。

Method: 提出反事实自解释方法：让模型对输入做最小编辑，使其自身预测发生改变。在情感分析和自然语言推理任务上，评估LLaMA-3与Qwen-2.5系列的十个指令微调模型，衡量忠实性、最小编辑程度以及与人工标注理由的一致性。

Result: 模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素：模型越大，越能生成翻转自身预测、且针对决策相关证据的反事实。理由引导条件能产生编辑更少、更符合人类标注的反事实，但并未持续提升忠实性。

Conclusion: 反事实自解释可提供关于模型决策的有用行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖模型能力，应经实证验证而非默认信任。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [155] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析跨越百余年、数百万首歌词，发现越战、9·11和新冠疫情等文化剧变显著改变了艺术家的用词模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多考察剧变对语言模式的影响，但较少在流行媒体中大规模分离剧变对个体的影响。

Method: 以五年为基线，分析数百万首歌词，比较艺术家内部与艺术家之间的痛苦信号，考察自我指涉、认知加工词汇及内容主题变化。

Result: 越战和9·11后自我指涉率显著下降；剧变后认知加工词汇上升；内容上更多书写"生活与自由"，更少书写"求偶与夜生活"。

Conclusion: 文化剧变会改变个体与集体的言语行为，体现了其对社会深远的影响。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [156] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AraMIP——首个面向阿拉伯语的隐喻标注指南，基于MIPVU框架并针对阿拉伯语特性进行改编，区分Isti'ara、kinaya、tashbih三类修辞，在300句/5277词试点数据上完成标注并总结阿拉伯语特有的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻研究因与语言创造性、语言使用和认知过程相关而备受关注，英语及其他语言的隐喻识别与标注已有很多工作，但阿拉伯语在该领域资源匮乏，缺乏标准化的标注规范与标注资源。

Method: 提出阿拉伯语隐喻识别程序AraMIP，以广泛使用的MIPVU框架为基础，结合阿拉伯语的语言特性进行适配；将阿拉伯语修辞语言分为Isti'ara（隐喻）、kinaya（转喻/间接表达）和tashbih（明喻）三类，并据此标注了300句、共5277词的试点数据集。

Result: 分析揭示了阿拉伯语特有的关键挑战：形态复杂性、词典义项排序不一致，以及缺乏供标注者使用的标准化语境材料。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向标准化阿拉伯语修辞实例的第一步，有助于推动更大规模标注资源的建设，从而支持未来阿拉伯语修辞语言的研究。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [157] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO提出一种分层双循环框架，利用LLM各层功能不对称性，实现无草稿模型的高效推测解码，获得2.4-2.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无草稿模型的推测解码方法常受限于过时的草稿候选以及验证的高计算成本，需要一种更高效的方案。

Method: ECHO利用早期层的高判别效率与末层的权威分布，将推理分为高频内循环与低频外循环。内循环用早期层bonus logits以极低代价快速进行多步草稿树探索；外循环通过状态复用机制执行全模型权威验证，并利用末层bonus logits修正已有路径、补充高置信候选。

Result: 在多样基准上，平均接受token数显著提升，实现2.4-2.9倍加速，超越现有最先进基线，工程开销可忽略且无需额外部署参数（但最优加速依赖一次性微调）。

Conclusion: ECHO通过分层双循环有效缓解草稿陈旧与验证成本高的问题，验证了利用层间功能不对称提升推测解码效率的可行性，代码已开源。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/whucs21Mzy/ECHO.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在6层解码器transformer的上下半层间插入一个持久循环状态模块（仅增加3.7%参数），通过交叉注意力观测隐表示、GRU更新、门控相加调制，将验证损失降低28.5%，且提升完全源于持久记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模语言模型中，标准注意力机制在捕捉如叙事位置等序列信息时效率不足，且容易过拟合、泛化差距较大，作者希望以轻量方式改善泛化能力。

Method: 在解码器-only transformer中引入持久循环状态：通过交叉注意力观测隐表示，用GRU更新自身，并通过门控相加调制后续处理；该模块插在6层transformer的上下半之间，仅增加3.7%参数。通过受控消融（有/无辅助自预测目标、随机辅助损失）分离改进来源，并用表征探测分析状态编码的信息。

Result: 验证损失从2.438±0.004降至1.743±0.018，即降低28.5%；在5个随机种子下统计显著（p<0.01）；泛化差距从0.26降至0.12，过拟合减少。消融显示改进完全来自持久记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标。表征探测显示持久状态编码叙事位置（52% vs 33%随机水平）。

Conclusion: 用轻量循环记忆桥接transformer层是提升小规模语言模型泛化的简单有效方法，其收益来自持久记忆拓扑本身，而非辅助训练目标。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"设计幻觉"概念，并构建一套识别、分类、刻画与利用幻觉的概念化流程，将失效假设转化为可复用的设计知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统（CPS）在开放环境中运行，无法预知所有情境，利益相关者依赖假设并将其形式化为设计模型。但系统面对运行现实时，假设可能失效，导致期望与观测行为不符，即"现实差距"。现有方法多通过提高仿真保真度来减少或克服该差距，缺乏统一方法论来结构化地利用失效假设，将其作为可复用设计知识。

Method: 作者提出"设计幻觉"概念，指对已失效假设的持续依赖及由此产生的对设计模型运行有效性的错误信心；并设计一套面向幻觉觉察的概念化流程，用于识别、分类、刻画并利用幻觉，将其转化为可操作的设计知识。

Result: 本文属于概念性工作，提出以"幻觉觉察"为核心的统一方法论框架，而非给出实验验证结果。

Conclusion: 应使设计幻觉显式化、结构化并作为知识加以利用，以支持更优的设计决策，弥补现有仅聚焦仿真保真度方法的不足。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究验证了大语言模型在零样本设定下识别社交信息中隐藏叙事的能力，发现结合人类撰写的叙事描述与简单集成方法，其表现可媲美有监督系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交信息中的隐藏叙事识别通常依赖有监督方法，但绝大多数领域缺乏训练数据。研究假设LLM的广泛语境知识能让其在语用层面理解信息，超越基础情感或主题分析，从而实现零样本叙事检测。

Method: 在Dipromats和SemEval数据集上进行零样本实验，比较提供人类撰写叙事描述、自动生成描述以及少样本示例三种条件的效果，并考察集成方法（尤其是多数投票）的作用，同时分析模型规模与提示变化的敏感性。

Result: 提供人类撰写的叙事描述显著提升性能且无需训练样本；自动生成描述或少样本示例常因框架的细微偏移而降低准确率；多数投票等集成方法增强鲁棒性；更大模型表现最佳且对提示变化更不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM可在零样本设定下有效检测策略性叙事，结合简单集成与人类撰写描述可媲美有监督系统，为缺乏训练数据的众多领域提供了可扩展的叙事检测方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASURRE 数据集，用于检测 LLM 辅助的在线问卷作答，并揭示人格化智能体作答难以检测，但可通过行为线索聚合提升识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 被用于完成在线问卷会损害基于问卷研究的有效性，但如何检测此类使用行为仍缺乏系统研究，尤其缺少针对多种作答策略的基准数据。

Method: 构建 ASURRE 基准数据集，涵盖从完全生成、修改到基于人格的智能体化完成等策略；在三个不同学科的真实问卷上，用多个 LLM 生成辅助作答，并与真实人类作答配对；评估现有机器生成文本检测器，并提出一种基于行为线索的少样本、免训练聚合方法。

Result: 朴素 AI 使用容易被检测，而模拟完整受访者的人格化智能体使检测器性能接近随机水平；智能体化完成无法完全复制受访者层面的行为，并留下独特行为痕迹；简单的少样本免训练聚合器在智能体场景下比最佳现有检测器平均 AUROC 提升 +0.14。

Conclusion: 检测 LLM 辅助问卷作答具有挑战性，尤其面对人格化智能体；但利用其行为痕迹并通过线索聚合可显著提升检测性能。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at https://github.com/mike-qz-wang/ASURRE.

</details>


### [162] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多个微调视觉语言模型作为独立标注者、通过字符级多数投票融合预测的方法，用于检测和分类视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉字符片段，并在SHROOM-Visions共享任务中取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉问题需要跨语言、更细粒度的检测与分类，为此参与SHROOM-Visions共享任务以探索有效的幻觉字符片段识别方法。

Method: 采用多个微调视觉语言模型作为独立标注者，对其片段预测进行字符级多数投票融合，并额外探索激活探针方法。

Result: 该方法在四种语言中的三种排名第一，并在所有语言和指标上均进入前三名。分析表明，不同模型之间的分歧与人类标注者之间的分歧具有一致性。

Conclusion: 多模型集成投票能有效检测与分类多语言视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉字符片段，且模型间分歧可近似反映人类标注分歧。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [163] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在五个知识密集型基准上对基于表示（KV缓存）和基于参数（微调）的适应方法进行了受控比较。在oracle设定下，Cartridges（KV）在几乎所有存储预算下最准确，比参数方法高10分；在更真实的多文档检索场景中，Cartridges是唯一能与ICL匹敌的方法，领先参数方法29分、Compaction 15分，但Cartridges也面临灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型回答预训练数据之外的问题时需要获取新信息，可通过上下文窗口文档、编码进模型参数或潜在表示注入等方式实现。然而这些方法在效率、成本和性能上各有权衡，没有单一最优方案，因此需要受控比较来明确各方法的适用场景。

Method: 在五个知识密集型基准上，对基于表示的（KV缓存）和基于参数的（微调）适应方法进行受控比较。具体涉及Cartridges（KV）、Compaction（KV）、参数化微调方法以及大MLP适配器，并在oracle设定和多文档检索两种场景下评估，同时考察灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在oracle设定下，Cartridges在几乎所有存储预算下最准确，比参数方法高10分；Compaction仅在低压缩率下与Cartridges相当，压缩率高于50倍时落后参数方法10分。在多文档检索场景中，Cartridges是唯一能与ICL匹敌的方法，领先参数方法29分、Compaction 15分。但Cartridges是除全量微调和大MLP适配器外，唯一遭受灾难性遗忘的方法，控制基准下降6%，编码任务下降13%。

Conclusion: 基于KV缓存的表示方法（尤其是Cartridges）在知识注入的准确性和检索场景中具有显著优势，但存在灾难性遗忘的风险。不同方法在效率、成本和性能间存在权衡，需根据具体场景选择适应方法。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [164] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ECHO——一个面向中文全双工对话轮次转换的配对诊断基准，用于区分需要让出话轮的"打断"与允许继续的"反馈信号"，并指出仅评估打断会高估系统实际可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工对话基准通常独立评估事件，可能奖励固定的动作偏好，而非真正依赖上下文的决策，因而无法有效衡量轮次转换的可靠性。

Method: ECHO 将相同重叠文本的样本配对，赋予相反的前置多轮对话上下文，使一对中一个需 Yield、另一个需 Keep；并加入 off-talk 样本来诊断不必要的让出。同时提出 pair accuracy 指标，要求一对中两个样本均正确，从而不给恒定动作策略任何分数。

Result: 对多个全双工系统的实验表明，多数系统表现出明显的 Yield 偏向，在打断上表现显著优于反馈信号，而另一个系统则相对均衡。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，仅评估打断会高估实际轮次转换的可靠性；ECHO 及其元数据将公开发布。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro和LLaMA等大语言模型，结合Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）技术，进行医疗领域的通俗语言适配（PLA）研究，通过提示策略对比、QLoRA微调及MoA集成，验证了LLM在提升医疗文本可读性同时保留关键内容方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗信息高度专业化，与患者的阅读能力之间存在显著鸿沟，亟需自动化的通俗语言适配（PLA）来简化技术性医疗语言，帮助患者理解健康信息。近年来GPT、BART等大语言模型的发展，为零样本和少样本场景下的PLA提供了新的可能，尤其是在任务特定数据有限的条件下，因此有必要系统探索LLM在医疗文本简化中的应用潜力。

Method: 研究采用多种大语言模型（GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA）进行文本简化，并引入Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）技术以增强PLA任务的适应性与鲁棒性。主要工作包括三部分：不同提示策略的对比分析、基于QLoRA的不同LLM微调，以及MoA技术的集成。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM驱动的PLA方法具有良好效果，能够在保留关键内容的同时使医疗信息更易理解，体现了其在提升医疗文本可读性方面的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型结合MoA技术可有效支持医疗领域的通俗语言适配，为弥合医疗信息复杂度与患者理解能力之间的差距提供了可行路径，并展示了LLM驱动PLA的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [166] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提交了MéTRON-FR，一个在9247万法语词上预训练的125M GPT-2模型，用于BabyLM 2026 Strict赛道，并在本土法语基准与跨语言迁移实验中揭示了儿童规模下的语法能力与诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在儿童规模（child-scale）数据下训练语言模型时，现有评测常依赖单token零样本打分，但这类方法可能被分词器和模板伪影主导，难以真实反映模型的语法能力；同时缺乏针对本土语言的语法最小对基准与可靠的跨语言对齐评估手段。

Method: 训练125M GPT-2模型于92.47M法语词；在原生魁北克法语语法最小对基准QFrBLiMP上评测；采用结合法语任务数据翻译与rank-16 LoRA的跨语言GLUE协议；使用双语词典归纳（BLI）将法语嵌入对齐到GPT-2；并开展消融研究考察单token零样本打分受分词器与模板伪影影响。

Result: QFrBLiMP得分85.97±0.17%，BabyLM加权排行榜62.80%；跨语言GLUE显示明显任务类型梯度——关系型任务有可测提升，世界知识型任务出现回退；BLI对齐p@1=68.84±8.61%，为随机水平的18倍；消融表明儿童规模下单token零样本打分被分词器与模板伪影主导。

Conclusion: 跨语言对齐反映的是已获得的语法能力而非训练时长；建议将分词器替换敏感性测试、安慰剂对照提示以及母语最小对基准作为标准诊断手段。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [167] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了剪枝对LLM上下文接地工具调用的影响，发现密集模型安全剪枝区间窄、MoE模型容忍度高，且剪枝先损害接地的具体性再损害模式级意图，激进剪枝会导致系统性拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝可降低LLM部署成本，但其对上下文接地工具调用能力的影响尚不清晰，需要系统研究剪枝引起的性能退化。

Method: 覆盖四种LLM（密集Transformer、密集混合、MoE架构），采用深度、宽度、混合及专家剪枝方法，经剪枝后SFT，在三个智能家居数据集上评估超19,500个实例，并从动作组件（操作、设备、参数、取值）和任务复杂度两个维度刻画退化。

Result: 密集模型安全剪枝区间窄且退化陡峭，MoE模型可容忍更多剪枝；剪枝先损害接地的具体性、后损害模式级意图；激进的密集剪枝会引发系统性过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 在选择剪枝LLM用于可靠工具执行时，应超越聚合准确率，从多维度评估剪枝影响。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning. Pruning degrades grounded specificity before schema-level intent, and aggressive dense pruning can induce systematic over-refusal. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating pruning beyond aggregate accuracy when selecting pruned LLMs for reliable tool execution.

</details>


### [168] [When Should LLMs Abstain? Chain-of-Self-Questioning for Selective Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17516)
*Ali Şenol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Self-Questioning（CoSQ）纯提示框架，让模型在明确评估回答问题所需信息之后才决定是否作答，在TruthfulQA上显著降低了错误作答率并提升了有效作答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事实依据薄弱时仍能生成流畅答案，容易产生无依据的错误承诺；在错误作答代价高于转介或人工复核的场景下，需要让模型具备可调、显式的"作答或弃答"能力。

Method: 提出CoSQ纯提示框架，将答案承诺建立在"回答问题所需信息"的显式自评之上；在TruthfulQA多选题验证集（817题）上，用11个开源与托管模型家族、17种条件评估三种CoSQ变体，并采用平衡选项协议；另用Natural Questions短答案任务做开放形式验证。

Result: 在最终平衡选项协议下，Grounded-CoSQ（τ=0.90）将平均无条件错误承诺率从思维链提示的13.1%降至8.9%（相对下降32.1%），作答准确率从86.9%升至89.7%，覆盖率达87.6%；两项改进在全部11个模型和所有阈值下均成立。Critical-CoSQ与Adaptive-CoSQ覆盖率分别为88.6%和86.5%，可靠性仍优于基线。

Conclusion: 自评估机制可支持显式且可调的"作答或弃答"决策，适用于无依据承诺代价高于转介或复核的场景。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers when their factual support is weak. This paper introduces Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ), a prompt-only framework that makes answer commitment conditional on an explicit assessment of the information required to answer a question. We evaluate three CoSQ variants under seventeen conditions on the 817-item TruthfulQA multiple-choice validation set using eleven open-weight and hosted model families. In the final balanced-option protocol, Grounded-CoSQ at τ=0.90 reduces the mean unconditional wrong-commitment rate from 13.1% under chain-of-thought prompting to 8.9%, a 32.1% relative reduction, while increasing answered accuracy from 86.9% to 89.7% and answering 87.6% of questions. Both improvements hold for all eleven models and at every evaluated threshold. Critical-CoSQ and Adaptive-CoSQ provide neighboring operating points with 88.6% and 86.5% coverage, respectively, while remaining more reliable than the baseline. A secondary Natural Questions Short-Answer evaluation provides convergent open-form evidence. These findings show that self-assessment can support explicit, tunable answer-or-abstain decisions when an unsupported commitment is more costly than referral or review.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Coaching Qwen3 Coder 30B to Think Like a CodeClash Arena Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16096)
*Ivy Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对开源代码智能体在长程竞技场任务中表现不佳的问题，论文以 Qwen3-Coder-30B 为案例，提出 ReAct SFT 与轨迹质量加权 SFT，通过改写教师轨迹为 [obs][thought][act] 链并对样本重新加权，显著提升策略行为，微调后模型在锦标赛中超过 Qwen3 Coder Plus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型编码智能体已能用于软件任务，但较弱的或开源智能体仍难以可靠理解用户意图并执行复杂多步工作流。这一差距在长程场景中尤为明显：智能体需反复检查先前结果、诊断失败并在交互约束下选择下一步代码编辑。因此论文提出核心问题：如何改进弱代码智能体的思考过程。

Method: 在 CodeClash 代码竞技场基准（原工作通过多轮锦标赛评估 8 个商业编码智能体、6 个竞技场）中，以排名最后的 Qwen3 Coder Plus 对应的开源模型 Qwen3-Coder-30B 为案例，利用更强智能体的蒸馏知识进行改进。分析发现该模型未针对竞技场式交互优化，常出现语法和协议破坏性错误，且跨轮策略适应能力弱，单纯指令微调难以纠正，因为离线 SFT 无法直接验证生成动作是否有效或有益。为此提出两种方法：ReAct SFT，将教师轨迹改写为显式的 [obs][thought][act] 链；以及轨迹质量加权 SFT，通过对样本重新加权来鼓励编辑后的检查行为。

Result: ReAct SFT 显著改善了策略行为，微调后的模型在锦标赛评估中优于原始 Qwen3 Coder Plus。

Conclusion: 通过将强智能体的蒸馏知识以 ReAct 结构化轨迹和轨迹质量加权的方式注入弱代码智能体，可以有效提升其在竞技场式长程交互任务中的策略表现，弥补开源模型与商业模型之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language model coding agents have recently become useful for software tasks, but weaker or open-weight agents still struggle to reliably interpret user intent and execute complex multi-step workflows. This gap is especially visible in long-horizon settings, where an agent must repeatedly inspect prior outcomes, diagnose failure, and choose the next code edit under interaction constraints. It motivates a natural question: what can we do to improve the thinking process of a weak code agent? We study this question in CodeClash, a code-arena benchmark where the original work evaluates 8 commercial coding agents across 6 arenas through multi-round tournaments. Since Qwen3 Coder Plus ranks last among them, we take the open-weight Qwen3-Coder-30B as a case study and investigate how to improve it with distilled knowledge from stronger agents. Our analysis shows that Qwen3-Coder-30B is not well optimized for arena-style interaction: it frequently produces syntax and protocol-breaking errors and exhibits weak strategic adaptation across rounds. These failures are difficult to correct with vanilla instruction tuning alone, since offline SFT cannot directly verify whether a generated action is valid or beneficial. To address this, we propose ReAct SFT, which rewrites teacher trajectories into explicit [obs][thought][act] chains, and trajectoryquality weighted SFT, which reweights samples to encourage post-edit checking. ReAct SFT substantially improves strategic behavior, and our fine-tuned model outperforms the original Qwen3 Coder Plus in tournament evaluation.

</details>


### [170] [Docker Containers vs. Virtual Machines: A Comparative Study of Architecture, Performance, Configuration, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16148)
*Faraz Gurramkonda,Akanksha Malla,Sayma Tamboli,Shayesta Nazneen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于文献对比分析了虚拟机与 Docker 容器在架构、配置与生命周期管理、性能、可扩展性和安全性方面的差异，指出效率与隔离构成设计权衡，容器内置于加固虚拟机中的混合架构往往是实用折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用平台需要在隔离工作负载的同时兼顾部署速度、可移植性、资源效率与安全性，而虚拟机与容器在不同抽象层应对该需求，因此有必要对两类技术进行系统比较以明确各自适用场景。

Method: 采用基于文献的比较分析方法，围绕架构、配置与生命周期管理、性能、可扩展性及安全性等维度，对虚拟机与 Docker 容器进行对照分析。

Result: 已发表研究普遍表明，容器具有更短的启动时间、更小的镜像、更高的工作负载密度，且在许多工作负载下接近原生执行性能；但这些优势依赖工作负载特征、存储与网络驱动、资源控制及实验设计。虚拟机开销更大，但提供独立内核、异构来宾操作系统和更强的隔离边界。

Conclusion: 效率与隔离应被视为设计权衡，而非判定某一技术普遍更优；将容器运行于加固虚拟机中的混合架构，通常能为云和多租户系统提供实用的平衡方案。

Abstract: Modern application platforms must isolate workloads while preserving deployment speed, portability, resource efficiency, and security. Virtual machines (VMs) and Docker containers address this requirement at different abstraction layers: VMs virtualize hardware and run independent guest operating systems, whereas containers isolate processes while sharing the host kernel. This paper presents a comparative, literature-based analysis of the two approaches across architecture, configuration and lifecycle management, performance, scalability, and security. Published studies generally associate containers with shorter startup times, smaller images, higher workload density, and near-native execution for many workloads. These benefits depend on workload characteristics, storage and network drivers, resource controls, and experimental design. VMs introduce greater overhead but offer independent kernels, heterogeneous guest operating systems, and a stronger isolation boundary. The comparison therefore treats efficiency and isolation as a design trade-off rather than declaring one technology universally superior. A hybrid architecture, in which containers run inside hardened VMs, often provides a practical balance for cloud and multi-tenant systems.

</details>


### [171] [Models as Governed Interfaces for AI-Native MBSE: Read-Side Adequacy and Write-Side Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16252)
*Jason Gower,Michael J. de C. Henshaw,Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，SysML v2 等模型的可程序化访问只是 AI 参与系统工程的前提而非充分条件；真正瓶颈在于模型周边的数据架构缺乏"认知充分性"（epistemic adequacy）。作者以公开的阿波罗11号 SysML v2 重建模型为案例，提出由读侧四项准则与写侧参与假设构成的五准则模式，并给出"受治理查询架构框架"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍把模型的可程序化访问当作 AI 参与系统工程的使能条件，但作者认为访问只是必要条件：即便模型结构完整，AI 在查询推导链时仍会遇到推导链缺失、认知状态未标注、来源不可追溯、证据无法解析等空缺，从而不是选择弃权，而是用训练数据填补——这一来源既不可验证也不受治理。为了在一个按当前实践已属优秀的模型（而非缺陷模型）上论证问题，作者选择阿波罗11号 SysML v2 重建作为探测对象。

Method: 以公开的阿波罗11号 SysML v2 重建模型为案例进行探测，将缺失属性命名为"认知充分性"（epistemic adequacy），并作为候选数据架构模式分为两半：读侧充分性使推导、状态与来源能回答查询而非诱发猜测；写侧可准入性在 AI 贡献进入记录前加以把关。该属性拆解为五项准则，其中四项属读侧（由案例与收敛文献佐证），第五项属参与侧（作为本文尚未检验的假设提出）。架构空间从内联元数据扩展到基质原生的多模型存储，并据此提出通过工程师既有视角约定来治理智能体参与的 Governed-Query Architecture Framework。

Result: 阅读侧的四项准则由阿波罗案例证据与收敛文献共同支撑；参与侧的第五项准则仅作为假设提出，本文尚未对其开展检验。作者将整个重构置于可证伪框架下：若该认知层无法在同一模型上击败检索增强基线，则视为被证伪，并计划先在阿波罗推导链上测试，随后进入工业试点。

Conclusion: AI 在系统工程中参与的关键不在于建模语言本身，而在于模型周边的数据架构。通过引入"认知充分性"属性、读侧四项准则与写侧参与假设，以及 Governed-Query Architecture Framework，作者主张以工程师熟悉的视角约定来治理智能体参与；该重构的可证伪条件为无法在同一模型上超越检索增强基线，验证路径为阿波罗推导链测试与工业试点。

Abstract: Machine-readable models such as SysML v2 are now programmatically accessible, and a growing body of work treats that access as the enabling condition for AI participation in systems engineering. Access is necessary, but not sufficient. The remaining work lies not in the modelling language but in the data architecture around it. An AI reader that queries a structurally complete model for a derivation still runs into absent derivation chains, untagged epistemic status, missing provenance, and evidence that the model cannot resolve. Faced with these gaps, it does not abstain; it fills them from training data, a source that is neither verifiable nor governed. To make the case on a model that is exemplary by current practice rather than deficient, we probe the public Apollo 11 SysML v2 reconstruction. We name the missing property epistemic adequacy and offer it as a candidate data-architecture pattern in two halves. Read-side adequacy lets derivation, status, and provenance answer a query rather than invite a guess; write-side admissibility gates an AI contribution before it enters the record. The property is broken down into five criteria. Four sit on the read side, evidenced by the case and convergent literature; the fifth sits on the participation side, advanced as a hypothesis this paper does not yet test. The architecture space runs from an inline metadata extension up to a substrate-native multi-model store, and over it, we propose the Governed-Query Architecture Framework, which governs agent participation through the viewpoint conventions that engineers already use. We commit the reframing to falsification: the epistemic layer counts as refuted if it cannot beat a retrieval-augmented baseline on the same model, tested first on the Apollo chain and then in an industrial pilot.

</details>


### [172] [AgentGuard: Learning Execution Guardrails from Anomalous Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16287)
*Wuyang Dai,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentGuard 是一种指令级防护栏框架，从编码智能体的异常轨迹中自动学习条件执行约束，用轻量技能动态激活相关规则，显著降低异常执行率并提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体依赖执行环境与仓库和外部工具交互，但任务成功不代表执行可靠：智能体可能修改无关文件、重写测试、发出不安全命令或忽略失败验证。因此需要行为防护栏来保障可靠执行，而无需依赖人工指定安全规则。

Method: AgentGuard 自动提取反复出现的执行失败模式，将其泛化为指令级行为约束，并组织为轻量级防护栏技能，仅在当前指令相关时动态激活相应规则，从而在提供行为引导的同时尽量减少对正常执行的限制。评估使用 642 条真实编码智能体执行记录的失败轨迹，覆盖 382 个仓库任务；防护栏从 461 条轨迹（282 个任务）中学习，在不相交的 100 个任务上评估。以 Claude Code 搭配 Claude Haiku 4.5 为底层智能体，对比基线智能体与加入 AgentGuard 的同一智能体。

Result: 实验显示，AgentGuard 将异常执行率从 69.0% 降至 26.7%，并将任务成功完成率从 21.7% 提升至 35.0%。

Conclusion: 从历史失败中学习得到的执行防护栏能显著提升 AI 编码智能体的可靠性，同时表明在安全性与任务完成之间取得平衡仍是尚待解决的挑战。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on execution harnesses to interact with repositories and external tools. However, task success does not guarantee reliable execution. Agents may still modify unrelated files, rewrite tests, issue unsafe commands, or ignore failed validations, motivating behavioral guardrails for reliable execution. We present AgentGuard, an instruction-level guardrail framework that learns conditional execution constraints from anomalous trajectories of coding agents. Rather than relying on manually specified safety rules, AgentGuard automatically extracts recurring execution failure patterns, generalizes them into instruction-level behavioral constraints, and organizes them as a lightweight guardrail skill that dynamically activates only the rules relevant to the current instruction.
  This design enables behavioral guidance while minimizing unnecessary restrictions on normal execution. We evaluate AgentGuard using 642 documented failure traces collected from real coding-agent executions across 382 repository tasks. Guardrails are learned from 461 traces covering 282 tasks and evaluated on a disjoint set of 100 tasks. Using Claude Code with Claude Haiku 4.5 as the underlying coding agent, we compare the baseline agent with the same agent augmented by AgentGuard. Experimental results show that AgentGuard reduces the Abnormal Execution Rate from 69.0% to 26.7% and increases the Successful Task Completion Rate from 21.7% to 35.0%. These results demonstrate that execution guardrails learned from historical failures can substantially improve the reliability of AI coding agents while highlighting the remaining challenge of balancing safety and task completion.

</details>


### [173] [Assurance Envelopes for Autonomous Coding Agents: Minimum-Cost Evidence for Software Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16302)
*Anjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"任务条件化保证包"概念：在编码智能体回到已有软件时，用类型化推理图建模证据与组合规则，通过前向链式闭包验证，寻找重新确立任务义务的最小成本证据子集，并将其建模为优化问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体返回已有软件时继承大量早期工程证据（测试、类型检查、证明、静态分析、踪迹）。全部重载浪费资源，但丢弃变更所依赖的某块证据又可能使必需属性失去支撑。因此需要确定：给定变更必须保持的属性（义务），哪些可用证据的最小子集能重新确立它们。

Method: 将证据及组合规则构建为类型化推理图；义务被满足定义为从所选证据前向链式推导可达该义务；用该闭包而非直接信任优化器来验证每个选择。评估所用图来自既有AI编码智能体运行的保留结果，冻结这些产物后询问后续任务应恢复哪些累积证据。另用249个实例的预设合成基准刻画计算特性。

Result: 来自Rust、IronBlocks和Pong结果的小图显示：最小保证包取决于任务；当现有证据无法重新确立必需属性时可能不存在保证包；某些属性需多块证据共同支撑；扩大需求会增加而非替换证据。合成基准中，丢弃"多块证据共同"结构的基线必然无法重新推导这些属性；所有完成的精确交叉校验都与CP-SAT优化器一致；500证据图的中位求解时间低于20 ms，但每个目标有多种替代推导的图在远更小规模即超时——难度由结构而非原始规模决定。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将成熟优化方法有限地应用于为软件变更选择保证上下文；发现义务以及智能体在下游获得的收益仍属开放问题。

Abstract: When a coding agent returns to existing software, it inherits evidence from earlier engineering work: tests, type checks, proofs, static analyses, and traces. Reloading all of it is wasteful, but dropping a piece the change depends on can leave a required property unsupported. Given the properties a change must preserve, its obligations, we ask which least-cost subset of the available evidence re-establishes them, and we call such a subset a task-conditioned assurance envelope. Evidence and the rules that combine it form a typed inference graph; an obligation is met when forward chaining from the selected evidence reaches it, and we validate every selection by that closure rather than by trusting the optimizer. The software-derived graphs in our evaluation come from preserved outcomes of prior AI coding-agent runs; we freeze those artifacts and ask which accumulated evidence should be restored for a later task. Small graphs from Rust, IronBlocks, and Pong outcomes show that the minimum envelope depends on the task, that none may exist when current evidence cannot re-establish a required property, that some properties need several pieces of evidence together, and that expanding the requirements adds evidence rather than replacing it. A prespecified synthetic benchmark of 249 instances characterizes computation: a baseline that discards the 'several pieces together' structure necessarily fails to re-derive them; every completed exact cross-check agreed with the CP-SAT optimizer; and median solve time stayed below 20 ms at 500-evidence graphs, except that graphs with many alternative derivations per target timed out at far smaller sizes, so structure, not raw size, drives difficulty. The contribution is a bounded application of established optimization to selecting assurance context for a software change; discovering the obligations and downstream agent benefit remain open.

</details>


### [174] [FairLint-DL: An IDE-Native Tool for Fairness Debugging of Deep Learning Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16321)
*Archit Rathod,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FairLint-DL 是一个 VS Code 插件，通过"左移"策略在训练前直接在 IDE 中对表格数据集进行偏差检测，基于信息论 QID 指标与两阶段梯度引导搜索，可在 12 秒内完成公平性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性分析工具多为训练后评估框架，需完成整个模型开发流程后才能评估偏差，无法在开发早期介入，导致返工成本高、集成到开发者工作流不顺畅。作者希望通过 shift-left（左移）思路，将公平性测试前移到训练前、并嵌入 IDE 环境。

Method: 提出 FairLint-DL（VS Code 扩展）：训练一个可配置深度神经网络作为代理模型，应用基于 Shannon 熵与最小熵的信息论 QID（定量个体歧视）指标，量化受保护属性对预测的因果影响；实现两阶段梯度引导搜索算法发现歧视性实例；构建因果调试流水线，通过敏感性分析将偏差定位到具体网络层与神经元；并集成 SHAP 与 LIME 双可解释性引擎进行特征级归因。

Result: 在三个表格基准数据集（Adult Census Income、German Credit、Bank Marketing）上评估，公平性问题在不同数据集间差异显著：在 Adult 上，96.0% 的实例 QID 超过 0.1-bit 显著性阈值，平均 QID 为 0.619 bits，差异影响比（DIR）为 0.581，违反四分之五法定规则。在缓存模型上，系统 12 秒内即可产出上述结果。

Conclusion: FairLint-DL 证明了将公平性分析无缝集成到开发者工作流中且不带来显著开销是可行的，实现了训练前的 IDE 原生偏差检测。

Abstract: Existing fairness analysis tools predominantly operate as post-training evaluation frameworks, requiring practitioners to complete the full model development lifecycle before assessing bias. We present FairLint-DL, a Visual Studio Code extension that implements a shift-left approach to fairness testing by enabling pre-training, IDE-native bias detection directly on tabular datasets. FairLint-DL trains a configurable deep neural network as a proxy model and applies information-theoretic Quantitative Individual Discrimination (QID) metrics. Grounded in Shannon and min-entropy, QID quantifies the causal influence of protected attributes on predictions. The system implements a two-phase gradient-guided search algorithm for discovering discriminatory instances, a causal debugging pipeline that localizes bias to specific network layers and neurons via sensitivity analysis, and dual explainability engines using SHAP and LIME for feature-level attribution. Evaluation on three tabular benchmarks (Adult Census Income, German Credit, and Bank Marketing) reveals fairness concerns that vary widely across datasets: on Adult, 96.0% of analyzed instances exhibit QID above the 0.1-bit significance threshold, with a mean QID of 0.619 bits and a disparate impact ratio of 0.581, violating the four-fifths legal rule. FairLint-DL produces these results within 12 seconds on cached models, demonstrating the feasibility of integrating fairness analysis into the developer workflow without significant overhead.

</details>


### [175] [Protocol-Preserving Context Trimming for Agentic Workflows: Benefits, Failure Regimes, and Budget Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16461)
*Harish Gaggar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估"协议保护式上下文裁剪"作为可靠性约束方法，在多步智能体工作流中比较五种裁剪策略。结果显示自适应预算护栏最优（任务成功率96.0%、协议遵循96.3%、级联失败仅1.0%、节省56.0% token），说明可靠上下文缩减关键在于保留协议关键状态而非最大化删除token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM系统依赖长交互历史来保留指令、工具状态、中间决策和未解决依赖，但上下文无限制增长会增加计算成本并可能降低效率。因此需要在保证可靠性的前提下缩减上下文，探索"协议保护式裁剪"是否能在多步工作流中兼顾效率与可靠性。

Method: 比较五种裁剪策略：基于最近性、基于相关性、摘要、协议感知裁剪、自适应预算护栏。评估维度包括保留上下文水平和流程复杂度类别，指标涵盖任务成功率、协议遵循度、有效工具调用、token节省、延迟降低、级联失败和关键上下文阈值。采用统计检验（p值、优势比）。

Result: 常规策略平均节省约60% token，但任务成功率较低（66.6–77.3%），协议遵循度85.5–88.6%。协议感知裁剪将任务成功率提升至92.2%；自适应护栏达96.0%任务成功率、96.3%协议遵循度、1.0%级联失败率，平均节省56.0% token。保留上下文预算≤25%相比≥50%使失败几率增加10.92倍（p<0.001）。激进预算下协议感知裁剪成功完成几率是常规方法的5.24倍，自适应护栏又比固定协议感知裁剪再提高2.11倍（p<0.001）。关键上下文阈值随流程复杂度上升。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文缩减更多取决于保留协议关键状态，而非最大化token删除；自适应护栏能在长时程智能体系统中提升效率、可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems rely on long interaction histories to preserve instructions, tool states, intermediate decisions, and unresolved dependencies, but unrestricted context growth increases computational cost and can reduce efficiency. This study evaluates protocol-preserving context trimming as a reliability-constrained approach for multi-step agentic workflows. Five trimming strategies - recency-based, relevance-based, summarization, protocol-aware trimming, and adaptive budget guardrails - were compared across retained-context levels and workflow-complexity classes using task success, protocol adherence, valid tool calls, token savings, latency reduction, cascading failures, and critical context thresholds. Conventional strategies achieved about 60% mean token savings but lower task success (66.6-77.3%) and protocol adherence (85.5-88.6%). Protocol-aware trimming improved task success to 92.2%, while adaptive guardrails achieved 96.0% task success, 96.3% protocol adherence, and 1.0% cascading failure with 56.0% mean token savings. Retained-context budgets of 25% or less increased failure odds 10.92-fold relative to budgets of 50% or more (p < 0.001). Protocol-aware trimming produced 5.24-fold greater odds of successful completion than conventional methods under aggressive budgets, while adaptive guardrails further increased success odds 2.11-fold versus fixed protocol-aware trimming (p < 0.001). Critical context thresholds also increased with workflow complexity. These findings indicate that reliable context reduction depends more on preserving protocol-critical state than on maximizing token removal, and that adaptive guardrails can improve efficiency, scalability, and reliability in long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [176] [AI Policies: Help or Hindrance? A Software Developer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16496)
*Samuel Ferino,Rashina Hoda,John Grundy,Christoph Treude,Hashini Gunatilake*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于19位软件开发者的访谈，探讨AI政策如何帮助与阻碍开发者，并提出以开发者为中心的政策引入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件组织为缓解大语言模型相关风险（如敏感信息泄露、未授权使用）而制定的AI政策，若开发者不参与、不遵守则形同虚设，因此需要理解开发者的真实体验。

Method: 对19位软件开发者进行访谈研究。

Result: 揭示了AI政策对开发者的双重作用：既有帮助，也存在阻碍。

Conclusion: 为管理者和决策者提供建议，倡导以开发者为中心的AI政策引入方式。

Abstract: AI policies introduced by software organisations to mitigate LLM-related risks such as sensitive information leaks and unauthorised usage are not useful if software developers do not engage with them. We draw on 19 software developer interviews to show how AI policies help and hinder developers. We suggest approaches to support managers and decision makers with a developer-centric approach to introducing AI policies.

</details>


### [177] [ExecuCritic: Calibrated Critic Shaping for Code Generation with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16604)
*Junjie Cao,Yingjie He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ExecuCritic 联合训练框架，让 coder 与 critic 在同一执行 rollout 上共同更新，critic 预测通过/失败并提供简短诊断反馈，仅在 critic 与执行器判断一致时供 coder 使用，从而缓解 RLVR 的信用分配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈（单元测试）客观且直接衡量程序正确性，但其弱点是把整个程序压缩为一个通过/失败位，使 RLVR 面临困难的信用分配问题。同时，现有编码系统中的 reviewer/tester 角色通常靠提示词驱动，未经训练，也未与执行结果校准。

Method: ExecuCritic 联合训练框架：coder 与 critic 在同一执行 rollout 上同步更新。critic 预测 pass/fail 结果并给出简短诊断反馈；仅当 critic 与执行器在当前 rollout 组上判断一致时，coder 才使用该信号。

Result: 在八个代码基准和两个近期开源骨架上，ExecuCritic 优于无 critic 的 GRPO、提示式 reviewer 系统以及标量奖励模型基线，同时需要更少的策略梯度步数和更少的沙盒执行次数。

Conclusion: 消融与可靠性分析表明，性能提升来自更好的信用分配，而非更大的采样预算。

Abstract: Execution feedback is a useful supervision signal for code models because unit tests are objective and directly measure program correctness. Its weakness is that an entire program is often reduced to one pass or fail bit, leaving RLVR to solve a difficult credit assignment problem. At the same time, coding systems often include separate reviewer or tester roles, but these critics are usually prompted rather than trained and are not calibrated against execution. We propose ExecuCritic, a joint training framework in which a coder and a critic are updated on the same execution rollouts. The critic predicts pass or fail outcomes and gives short diagnostic feedback; the coder uses this signal only when the critic agrees with the executor on the current rollout group. Across eight code benchmarks and two recent open backbones, ExecuCritic improves over GRPO without a critic, prompted reviewer systems and scalar reward model baselines, while requiring fewer policy gradient steps and fewer sandbox executions. Ablations and reliability analyses suggest that the gains come from better credit assignment rather than larger sampling budgets.

</details>


### [178] [An Exploratory Study of Dependabot Cooldown Adoption in Open-Source GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16605)
*Hidetake Tanaka,Rikuto Tsuchida,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 GitHub Dependabot 冷却（cooldown）功能的早期采用情况进行探索性实证研究，分析流行开源仓库如何采用与配置该功能及其动机，发现安全担忧是主要驱动力，且用户偏好简单默认延迟而非细粒度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化依赖更新可能在维护者和社区及时发现之前快速传播恶意包版本。2025 年 7 月 GitHub 将 Dependabot cooldown 正式发布，作为防御软件供应链攻击的手段，但其早期采用效果尚不清楚，因此需要实证研究其采用与配置现状。

Method: 对流行开源 GitHub 仓库进行探索性实证研究，考察其如何采用和配置 cooldown 功能，并调查采用动机。统计了 92 个已知动机的采用事件、75 个仅出于安全考虑的采用事件，以及保留 cooldown 的仓库中 251 个生态系统的配置情况。

Result: 在 92 个已知动机的采用事件中，83 个由安全担忧驱动；75 个仅出于安全的采用中有 43 个由安全 linter 警告触发。在保留 cooldown 的 251 个生态系统中，97.2% 设置了通用延迟，其中 64.3% 使用七天，而各类更新类型设置的采用率均低于 10%。

Conclusion: 早期采用者倾向于简单的默认延迟而非细粒度控制。这表明工具应提供反映生态系统支持情况的稳健默认值，并将细粒度控制保留给具有明确更新优先级的依赖项。

Abstract: Automated dependency updates can rapidly propagate malicious package releases before maintainers and the broader community have enough time to detect them. In July 2025, GitHub made Dependabot cooldown generally available as a defense against software supply chain attacks. However, the effects of its early adoption remain unknown. In this exploratory study, we empirically examine how popular open-source GitHub repositories adopt and configure the feature and investigate their motivations. We find that security concerns motivated 83 of 92 adoption events with known motivations. Security linter warnings triggered 43 of 75 security-only adoptions. Among 251 ecosystems within repositories that retained cooldown, 97.2% set a general delay. Of these, 64.3% used seven days, while use of each update type setting was below 10%. Early adopters therefore favor simple default delays over fine-grained controls. These findings suggest that tools could provide robust defaults reflecting ecosystem support and reserve fine-grained controls for dependencies with clear update priorities.

</details>


### [179] [Memory-Skill Isomorphism: One Skill Carrier, Two Native Uses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16669)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出以Skill作为记忆与技能的统一载体，用渐进披露（L0描述/L1索引/L2细节）与受治理写入路径承载经验沉淀，并用部署数据刻画其成本与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆与技能若各自建设存储、路由、检索、反思与更新路径，复用机制会随积累而重复膨胀。由于Skill本身已是蒸馏记忆的天然载体，可避免重复建设。

Method: 记忆组件即Skill：常驻描述承载热线索，按需加载的SKILL.md承载冷索引与策展策略，reference/*.md承载细节记忆（L0→L1→L2）；以1,024字符描述预算压缩常驻索引。读取侧共享，写入侧分叉：历史追加、当前状态重写并重验证。

Result: 治理是识别度最高的环节：4条写入条目仅1/4进入结算账本。定价运行点上，一条L1经验点增加1,313首轮token（基线1,462），k=1时1,313对1,365基本持平，k=5为4,949；会话总量仅差1.18倍且末轮区间重叠，价格分解不可得。单个任务单模型下，暴露该经验减少失败（0/8或1/8，对比非并发历史下限6/6，未做多重性校正）。常驻与BM25在两次小样本比较中未检出差异。

Conclusion: 结果支持一条候选RSI设计规则：单一Skill载体族、共享读侧、受治理的差异化写入。该结论属选定任务的事后存在性信号而非确证率；身体交付h:=P(D|A)在RQ1—RQ2中未加测量，评估对象为常驻索引实现。

Abstract: Memory and skills improve agents without changing weights: memory carries prior experience, skills carry reusable procedures. Wrapping both in stores, routers, retrieval, reflection, and update paths makes reuse machinery grow with accumulation. Part of this duplication need not be rebuilt: a Skill is already a natural carrier for distilled memory. Here the memory component is a Skill: resident description holds hot cues, on-demand SKILL.md a colder index and curation policy, and reference/*.md files detailed memories (levels L0 -> L1 -> L2). A governed 1,024-character description budget keeps the resident index compressed. Memory and capability thus share one progressive-disclosure carrier; reflection evolves either Skill. Writes fork: history appended, current state rewritten and revalidated. In one deployed system, the sharpest identification is governance: 4 write entries exist, but only 1/4 reaches the settlement ledger. At the priced operating point, one L1 lesson-1 point adds 1,313 first-turn tokens against a 1,462-token baseline--tied at k=1 (1,313 versus 1,365), 4,949 at k=5; session totals differ by only 1.18x with overlapping last-turn ranges; price decomposition is unavailable. On one selected task with one model, exposing the lesson means fewer failures (0/8 or 1/8 with the lesson versus a shared non-concurrent 6/6 historical floor, unadjusted for multiplicity). The task was selected on prior floor evidence, so this is a selected-task post-selection existence signal, not a confirmatory rate. Resident and BM25 show no detected difference in two small comparisons. This motivates a candidate RSI design rule: one Skill carrier family, a shared read side, governed distinct writes. Body delivery h := P(D|A) is uninstrumented in RQ1--RQ2; this evaluates a resident-index implementation.

</details>


### [180] [RECTIFY: An Interactive Workbench for Post-Evaluation RAG Diagnosis, Repair, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16764)
*Keerthana Murugaraj,Salima Lamsiyah,Martin Theobald*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RECTIFY 是一个交互式 Streamlit 工作台，将已评估的 RAG 案例转化为可审计的修复流程，帮助开发者把评估结果变成可检查的修复决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评估器能识别弱检索、接地不良、答案不完整等问题，但很少帮助开发者决定接下来该修什么，缺少从评估到修复的桥梁。

Method: RECTIFY 先过滤无需修复的案例，将剩余失败路由为可操作的失败家族与细粒度修复切片，并生成可编辑的修复卡片，供开发者批准、拒绝或通过沙盒重跑验证。

Result: 在受控 RAG 基准上，RECTIFY 跨 BM25、稠密与混合检索呈现可解释的失败画像：BM25 主要触发噪声检索修复，稠密与混合检索留下较少的多部分欠检索与证据未充分利用案例。分析还显示，预过滤减少不必要的修复候选，切片级路由比宽泛家族级诊断产生更有针对性的修复卡片。

Conclusion: RECTIFY 作为开源 Streamlit 工作台公开可用，帮助开发者将评估结果转化为可检查的修复决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) evaluators can identify failures such as weak retrieval, poor grounding, incomplete answers, and unsupported generation, but they rarely help developers decide what to repair next. We present RECTIFY, an interactive Streamlit workbench that turns evaluated RAG cases into auditable repair workflows. RECTIFY filters cases that do not require repair, routes remaining failures into actionable families and finegrained repair slices, and generates editable repair cards that developers can approve, reject, or verify through sandbox reruns. On a controlled RAG benchmark, RECTIFY surfaces interpretable failure profiles across BM25, dense, and hybrid retrieval: BM25 mainly triggers noisy-retrieval repairs, while dense and hybrid retrieval leave smaller sets of multi-part underretrieval and underused-evidence cases. Additional analyses show that pre-filtering reduces unnecessary repair candidates and that slicelevel routing yields more targeted repair cards than broad family-level diagnosis. RECTIFY is publicly available as an open-source Streamlit workbench 1 for helping developers turn evaluation results into inspectable repair decisions.

</details>


### [181] [RepoAtlas: Guiding Coding Agents via Evolving Multimodal Repository Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16936)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Haiquan Wang,JiaWei Guo,Hanyang Xia,Yan Chen,Tong Chen,Zhang Zhiwei,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoAtlas 是一个免训练模块，通过“选择—投影—刷新”循环为代码智能体维护动态多模态仓库视图，在 SWE-bench Verified 上提升解决率并降低输入 token 与模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级问题解决仍具挑战：智能体不仅要生成合理补丁，还需在相互依赖的文件间定位相关代码，并维持充分且聚焦的仓库上下文。代码图能揭示非局部关系，但线性文本接口掩盖其拓扑；渲染完整仓库图过于密集难以可靠感知，而一次性局部视图会随探索推进而过时。

Method: RepoAtlas 是一个免训练模块，在仓库代码图上通过 select–project–refresh 循环维护不断演化的多模态仓库视图。它结合问题证据与智能体当前探索状态，在固定预算下选择任务相关区域；将所选结构投影为互补的视觉与文本表示；当探索状态变化使视图过时则刷新视图。
