Based on MOCC, we propose MOCC-R1, a two-stage framework for optimizing reasoning-response consistency. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning trains the model to generate a structured trajectory consisting of client-state understanding, a response intent that links a counseling principle to a planned action, and the final response. Reinforcement learning (RL) then rewards grounded plan coherence and plan execution, encouraging the inferred state and plan to be supported by the dialogue context and the response to realize that plan. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed MOCC-R1.

</details>


### [251] [End-to-End Latency-Minimizing and Load-Balanced Request Scheduling for Edge LLM Inference in Agentic AI Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17193)
*Zhen Li,Jun Cai,Haoran Gao,An Li,Tan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘LLM推理的在线请求调度问题，提出LYREO方法，联合优化长期平均端到端时延并平衡异构边缘服务器负载，仿真中优于代表性基线与启发式方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体服务对低延迟推理需求日益增长，推动LLM部署于分布式边缘服务器。但异构的通信与计算能力、动态演化的推理状态，使每个请求的服务器选择随时间变化且跨时隙强耦合。现有方法面临两大挑战：传统时延模型无法准确刻画多阶段LLM执行的细粒度动态；调度决策的时延后果仅在请求完成后才可观测，难以即时评估决策。

Method: 提出跨时隙推理模型，刻画传输、预填充（prefill）、逐迭代解码（iteration-level decoding）及各请求的KV缓存演化，并用KV缓存内存-时间消耗度量服务器负载。设计LYREO方法：通过Lyapunov优化将长期负载均衡约束转化，并采用奖励重分配与基于序列的回报预测，把延迟结果转化为对早期决策的及时学习信号。

Result: 在多种配置下的仿真表明，LYREO相较代表性基于学习的方案与启发式基线方案，始终取得更低的时延和更均衡的负载分布。

Conclusion: 面向边缘LLM推理的在线调度可通过跨时隙推理建模与Lyapunov优化结合延迟奖励重分配学习来有效解决，实现时延与负载均衡的联合优化，LYREO在仿真中表现稳定优越。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agentic AI services increasingly demand low-latency inference, motivating the deployment of LLMs across distributed edge servers. However, heterogeneous communication and computing capabilities, together with dynamically evolving inference states, make the edge server selection for each incoming request time-varying and tightly coupled across slots. In this paper, we investigate an online request scheduling framework for edge LLM inference that jointly minimizes long-term average end-to-end latency and regulates workload distribution across heterogeneous edge servers. Two main challenges arise in this context. First, conventional latency models cannot accurately capture the fine-grained dynamics of multi-stage LLM execution. Second, the latency consequence of a scheduling decision is observed only after request completion, making immediate decision evaluation difficult. To address these challenges, we develop a cross-slot inference model that captures transmission, prefill, iteration-level decoding, and key-value (KV) cache evolution for each diverse request, and characterize server workload through a KV cache memory-time consumption metric. We propose the LYREO approach that transforms the long-term load-balancing constraint via Lyapunov optimization and employs reward redistribution with sequencebased return prediction to convert delayed outcomes into timely learning signals for earlier decisions. Simulations under various configurations demonstrate that LYREO consistently achieves lower latency and more balanced load distribution than representative learning-based and heuristic baseline schemes.

</details>


### [252] [Extracting ontology-compliant knowledge from scientific text describing irradiated materials using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17291)
*Marco Luca Sbodio,Marcos Martínez Galindo,Vanessa Lopez,Blanca Biel,Pablo Canca,Pedro Delgado,Jesús I. Mendieta-Moreno,Raphael Tack,Maria J. Caturla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 eolas,一个模块化流水线,利用大语言模型将科学文献自动转化为与指定本体对齐的知识图谱,并以核聚变反应堆材料为应用场景进行验证,同时发布了首个辐照材料领域知识图谱构建基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新材料研究依赖跨原子到宏观尺度的预测模型与仿真,但所需关键数据多以非结构化文本形式散落在科学文献中,难以复用。传统的基于大语言模型的信息抽取通常只生成简单模式的键值对,表达能力有限。

Method: eolas 是一个模块化流水线,使用大语言模型将科学文档自动转换为与指定本体对齐的知识图谱,并以表格形式结合分面导航呈现,便于人工验证。同时构建了首个面向辐照材料领域、用于评估大语言模型知识图谱构建能力的基准数据集。

Result: 人类专家从一篇文章中抽取相关数据需 30 至 90 分钟,而 eolas 只需几分钟即可生成高质量知识图谱。基于该数据集开展了 168 组实验,覆盖多种大语言模型与提示技术。

Conclusion: 实验分析总结出有效抽取与输入本体对齐的知识图谱的实用指南,验证了基于大语言模型的知识图谱构建在材料科学等专业领域的可行性与效率优势。

Abstract: The quest for new materials increasingly relies on predictive models and comprehensive simulations that span scales from atomic to macroscopic levels. However, essential data necessary for these models and simulations are often embedded in scientific literature as unstructured text, limiting reusability and posing challenges for researchers seeking to leverage existing knowledge effectively. While extracting structured data from unstructured text using large language models is gaining popularity, traditional methods typically generate key-value pairs data with straightforward schemas. In contrast, we introduce eolas, a modular pipeline that uses large language models to automatically transform scientific documents into knowledge graphs aligned with a specified ontology. We demonstrate eolas effectiveness in extracting useful information for scientists studying materials designed to endure the extreme temperatures and radiation levels found in fusion reactors. While a human expert might spend between thirty to ninety minutes extracting relevant data from an article, eolas can generate high-quality knowledge graphs in just a few minutes. These are presented in a tabular format with faceted navigation for easy human validation. Additionally, we introduce the first benchmark dataset designed to assess large language models capabilities in constructing knowledge graphs within the domain of irradiated materials. The analysis of 168 experiments using our dataset, various large language models and prompting techniques provides key insights that we summarize into practical guidelines for effectively extracting knowledge graphs aligned with an input ontology.

</details>


### [253] [Intrinsic Motivation in Reinforcement Learning: A Research Agenda for Adaptive Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17325)
*Anatoly Belikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述并探讨内在奖励能否在无共享外部目标下支持人工神经系统的适应、功能特化与高层自组织，并指出现有方法的失败模式，提出三个实验方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物细胞可视为相互作用的个体智能体，其集体动力学在个体、组织到多细胞生物等多层次上产生适应性行为。作者希望探究人工神经网络系统中的内在奖励，在不依赖共享外部目标的情况下，是否也能支持类似的适应、功能特化与自组织。

Method: 这是一篇视角与教程性文章。作者综述了赋权（empowerment）、好奇心、学习进展、信息增益、无监督技能发现、互信息估计及世界模型用于内在奖励计算等方法，并重点分析了这些目标在何种情况下无法产生持续探索或越来越复杂的行为。

Result: 研究指出内在奖励目标存在典型失败模式，无法持续驱动探索与复杂度增长。作者认为更强的系统可能需要互补目标、通信、记忆、多时间尺度学习和环境约束，并提出三个实验方向：资源受限环境、带各智能体内在奖励的循环智能体网络、以及先发展探索性运动能力再发展目标导向行为的层级世界模型智能体。

Conclusion: 内在学习有望在逐步更高的层次上实现适应性组织，但需结合互补目标、通信、记忆、多尺度学习与环境约束，所提三个实验方向可用于检验这一假设。

Abstract: Biological cells can be viewed as individual, interacting agents whose collective dynamics give rise to adaptive behaviour at multiple levels of organisation, from individual cells through tissues to whole multicellular organisms. In this perspective and tutorial article we discuss whether intrinsic rewards in artificial neural systems can support adaptation, functional specialisation and higher-level self-organisation without a shared external objective. We review empowerment, curiosity, learning progress, information gain, unsupervised skill discovery, mutual information estimation and the use of world models for intrinsic reward computation. Particular attention is given to failure modes showing when such objectives do not produce sustained exploration or increasingly complex behaviour. We argue that more capable systems may require complementary objectives, communication, memory, learning at multiple temporal scales and environmental constraints. Based on this perspective, we outline three experimental directions. These include a resource-constrained environment in which otherwise stable behavioural attractors become unsustainable, allowing us to test whether environmental constraints can mitigate characteristic failure modes of intrinsic objectives. The network of recurrent agents with per-agent intrinsic rewards, and a hierarchical world-model agent in which exploratory motor competence develops before goal-directed behaviour. These experiments are intended to test whether intrinsic learning can lead to adaptive organisation at progressively higher levels.

</details>


### [254] [From Transient Prompts to Persistent Control: Scientific Poster Generation via Recursive Semantic-Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17326)
*Runze Li,Yukun Zhao,Can Xu,Yucheng Shen,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PosterVisor，将海报生成从"临时提示"转为"持久控制"，用语义-几何契约(SGC)与递归契约执行(RCE)提升可控性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学海报生成需在固定空间内权衡信息覆盖与可读性；现有方法把方案当作临时提示，各阶段孤立校验，导致内容与布局模块间需求漂移、先前检查被静默失效。

Method: PosterVisor 为控制框架：Orchestrator 将评分标准锚定于论文与视觉素材，编译为 Semantic-Geometric Contract (SGC)，把论点与来源绑定到所需视觉、预算与空间承诺；仅完全实例化的记录成为可执行断言，其他可用需求作为软引导。Recursive Contract Enforcement (RCE) 随证据出现动态跨阶段触发检查，并在修复时重检受影响的检查点状态，防止修复引发的回归静默传播。在 HTML/CSS 与可编辑 PPTX 生成器中实例化。

Result: 在 100 篇论文的 Paper2Poster 基准上，PosterVisor-PPT 的 poster-grounded QA 平均准确率由 PosterGen 的 58.53% 提升至 64.47%；人类评审在 72.5% 的非平局两两比较中更偏好该方法（95% CI：61.6–83.4%）。另在 30 篇论文的次要研究中，VLM Overall 与 PaperQuiz 均值也更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持以评分标准编译契约、并采用阶段条件化执行来实现可控海报合成。

Abstract: Scientific poster generation distills a multimodal paper into a single-page visual artifact, forcing strict trade-offs between informational coverage and readability under a fixed spatial budget. Existing methods pass plans as transient prompts and validate individual stages in isolation. This strategy causes requirements to drift across content and layout modules, and previous checks to be silently invalidated. We introduce PosterVisor, a control framework that shifts poster generation from transient prompts to persistent control. An Orchestrator grounds rubrics in the paper and visual assets, compiling them into a Semantic-Geometric Contract (SGC) that binds claims and sources to required visuals, budgets, and spatial commitments. Only fully instantiated records become executable assertions; other usable requirements remain soft guidance. Recursive Contract Enforcement (RCE) dynamically triggers checks across stages as evidence emerges. Crucially, during repairs, RCE rechecks affected checkpoint states, preventing repair-induced regressions from propagating silently. We instantiate PosterVisor in HTML/CSS and editable PPTX generators. On the 100-paper Paper2Poster benchmark, PosterVisor-PPT improves observed mean poster-grounded QA accuracy over PosterGen (64.47% vs. 58.53%) and is preferred by human judges in 72.5% of non-tied pairwise comparisons (95% CI, 61.6-83.4%). A secondary 30-paper study also yields higher VLM Overall and PaperQuiz means. These results support rubric-compiled contracts and stage-conditioned enforcement for controllable poster synthesis.

</details>


### [255] [Self-Emergence Agent Architecture:Behavior-Inertia HMM, Reflexive Metacognition,and Social-Contrastive Self-Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17331)
*Xiaoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"自我涌现智能体架构"(SEAA),通过隐马尔可夫模型、反思式元认知循环与社会比较多智能体环境的闭环耦合,使初始相同的智能体自发分化出稳定人格与社交结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式智能体模拟存在三大结构性局限:人格漂移、反思不具进化性、缺乏自我—他者边界;其依赖静态记忆与固定提示词,既无行为惯性也无内生自我演化机制。作者旨在构建一个能刻画"自我涌现"的统一框架与可重复实验范式。

Method: SEAA由三部分构成:(i)用隐马尔可夫模型(HMM)的可编辑状态转移矩阵编码长期行为与认知惯性;(ii)反思式言语元认知循环,其输出直接更新HMM参数而非仅存为文本;(iii)多智能体社会环境,使初始相同的个体彼此持续比较行为。三者构成闭环:社会行动→反馈→自我反思→惯性更新→差异化行动。作者提出三个可证伪假设,并给出可复现实验协议与操作化指标。

Result: 无语言模型的原型显示该闭环能自发打破对称性:初始相同的智能体固化出彼此不同且稳定的人格,而匹配对照组不能。接入托管LLM的实验将差异表现为不同的第一人称自我叙事;五智能体审议自发形成社会结构——一个共识中心与一个被一致排斥的离群者,对照组中均不存在。

Conclusion: 受庄子式认识论不可知立场启发,SEAA仅研究可观测的行为涌现,不对主观感受性作任何主张。本文的贡献在于提供统一框架、含伪代码的具体架构、机制性证据,以及用于研究人工自我涌现的"显微镜式"沙盒。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents exhibit strong language-generation and problem-solving capabilities, yet suffer from three structural limitations: personality drift, non-evolutionary reflection, and the absence of a self-other boundary. Existing generative-agent simulations rely on static memory and fixed prompts, maintaining neither behavioral inertia nor endogenous self-evolution. We propose the Self-Emergence Agent Architecture (SEAA), which integrates three components: (i) a Hidden Markov Model (HMM) that encodes long-term behavioral and cognitive inertia as an editable state-transition matrix; (ii) a Reflexion-style verbal metacognition loop whose output updates the HMM parameters themselves, rather than merely being stored as text; and (iii) a multi-agent social environment in which initially identical agents continuously compare their behavior with others'. The three components form a closed loop: social action $\to$ feedback $\to$ self-reflection $\to$ inertia update $\to$ differentiated action. We state three falsifiable hypotheses and provide a reproducible experimental protocol with operational metrics. A language-model-free prototype shows the loop spontaneously breaks symmetry: initially identical agents consolidate distinct, stable personalities whereas matched controls do not. Experiments with a hosted LLM surface these differences as distinct first-person self-narratives, and a five-agent deliberation spontaneously develops social structure---a consensus hub and a unanimously rejected outlier---absent in the control. Following an epistemologically agnostic stance inspired by Zhuangzi, SEAA studies only observable behavioral emergence and makes no claim about subjective qualia. This work contributes a unified framework, a concrete architecture with pseudocode, mechanistic evidence, and a microscope-style sandbox for studying artificial-self emergence.

</details>


### [256] [FlashVector: Agent for Hierarchical Model Serving Stack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17391)
*Qi Wu,Lohan Lemire,Kai Meng,Zhongmou Cai,Raphael Bargues,Petr Zhitnikov,Zeyuan Cao,Yao Wang,Shujun Bian,Wei Chen,Sean Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlashVector 是一个智能体系统，将单内核优化智能体范式扩展到整个模型服务栈（GPU内核、计算图、模型服务器、特征处理），在 Unity 广告平台实现最高 2x 吞吐、1.98x 延迟优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务是推荐系统主要成本来源，优化需跨 GPU 内核、框架计算图、模型服务器、按需特征处理等多层专业知识，跨层专家难以获取且无法随工作负载扩展；现有 AI 智能体仅在单一 GPU 内核优化上有成果，服务栈其余部分的自动优化尚属空白。

Method: 提出 FlashVector 智能体系统，核心是可扩展框架，将单内核优化智能体范式泛化到异构技术栈，实现跨所有层级的整体优化。

Result: 在 Unity 的 Vector 广告平台部署后，模型服务器吞吐最高提升 2x、延迟加速最高 1.98x，特征存储吞吐最高提升 1.6x；优化覆盖 GPU 内核、计算图、模型服务器（NVIDIA Triton 的 C++ 代码库）以及按需特征变换服务（Python 代码库）。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至更复杂的系统架构，证明智能体优化不仅能作用于内核与计算图，也能跨不同语言与组件实现整体性能提升。

Abstract: Model serving is one of the largest cost drivers in production recommender systems. Maximizing its throughput requires navigating a deeply layered hierarchy: GPU kernels, the ML framework computation graph, the model server, and on-demand feature processing -- each demanding specialized domain expertise. Such cross-layer expertise is inherently difficult to acquire, and does not scale with a workload that continuously grows and evolves, leaving significant cost efficiency gains unrealized. While recent AI agents have demonstrated human expert level efficiency in standalone GPU kernel optimization, automated tuning and optimization for the rest of the serving stack remain largely unexplored. We present FlashVector, an agentic system that optimizes performance across all layers of the model serving stack. The key contribution is an extensible framework to generalize the single kernel optimization agent paradigm to heterogeneous technical stacks, and to deliver performance improvements holistically. After deployment in Unity's Vector advertising platform, FlashVector achieved up to 2x throughput increase and up to 1.98x latency speedup on model server, and up to 1.6x throughput increase on feature store. These optimizations were discovered not only at the GPU kernel and computation graph levels, but also across the other components of the model serving stack, such as the model server (NVIDIA Triton's C++ codebase) and the on-demand feature transformation service (Python codebase), demonstrating the extensibility of the framework to more complex system architectures.

</details>


### [257] [Never Stop Thinking: Continuous-Time Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17416)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在轻量级"打断-恢复"编排器下，未修改的文本模型可自发涌现"边听边想、边说边想"的连续时间认知，将实时流水线延迟降低19%（目标场景减半）；但研究表明，要让连续思考真正有用，需依赖可验证目标、类型化奖励与在策略强化学习，而非LLM评委的偏好优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的语音智能体遵循僵化的"听-想-说"循环，每次回复前都会插入数秒沉默（死气）。作者希望探索：连续时间认知（边听边想、边说边想）能否从模型中自然涌现，以及这种连续思考是否真正提升智能体的任务完成能力，而非只是看起来更好。

Method: 1) 采用未修改的文本模型加轻量级"打断-恢复"编排器，实现连续时间交互；2) 提出ReactiveBench：120个交互场景，按预注册二元需求打分，另设可验证流式赛道按精确正确性评分；3) 辨识"LLM评委偏好可见推理"的陷阱，使用独立评委复核；4) 五阶段训练研究，在三个层面（信号来源、奖励结构、优化器）定位正确训练信号，比较偏好优化与在策略RL。

Result: 连续时间认知使实时流水线整体延迟降低19%，目标场景延迟减半。ReactiveBench暴露：LLM评委奖励可见推理，连续思考的"优势"在独立评委下符号反转，评委训练的模型思考时客观上完成的-需求更少。训练研究显示：可验证目标使思考从有害变有益；统一奖励遗漏的部分会被优化牺牲，处处追求简洁会侵蚀多跳工具链；偏好优化只能在冲突子目标间权衡，而基于类型化奖励的在策略RL能同时提升各正确性轴，将流式完成率从48%提升至73±5%（跨随机种子），并在更大规模及第二个模型上复现。

Conclusion: 编排让连续时间交互成为可能；而一个来源正确、结构合理、优化得当的可验证信号，才让它真正有用。

Abstract: Voice agents built on LLMs follow a rigid listen-think-speak loop that inserts seconds of dead air before every reply. We show that continuous-time cognition (thinking while listening and thinking while speaking) emerges from an unmodified text model under a lightweight interrupt-and-resume orchestrator, cutting live-pipeline latency by 19% overall and by half in the regime the mechanism targets. To measure whether continuous-time thinking improves what agents accomplish, we introduce ReactiveBench: 120 interactive scenarios scored against pre-registered binary requirements, plus a verifiable streaming track scored by exact correctness. ReactiveBench exposes a pitfall with broad consequences: LLM judges reward visible reasoning; a large judged "advantage" of continuous-time thinking reverses sign under an independent judge, and judge-trained models objectively complete fewer requirements when they think. A five-stage training study then locates the right signal at three levels. Its source: verifiable objectives turn thinking from harmful to helpful. Its structure: whatever a uniform reward omits, optimization trades away; brevity everywhere erodes multi-hop tool chaining. Its optimizer: preference optimization can only trade conflicting sub-goals against each other, while on-policy RL over a type-shaped reward improves every correctness axis at once, raising streaming completion from 48% to 73+/-5% across seeds and replicating at larger scale and on a second model. Orchestration makes continuous-time interaction possible; a verifiable signal, correctly sourced, shaped, and optimized, makes it good.

</details>


### [258] [World Model Science: Self-Organized Criticality, Weak Chaos, and Metastable Belief Dynamics in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17419)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过自组织临界性、弱混沌和亚稳信念动力学三种视角研究长时程LLM智能体的轨迹动力学，发现局部有效行动可在全局状态失真后持续、错误传播具长记忆与雪崩特征，但分歧有界、信念呈亚稳态，且不支持普适幂律或临界点等强主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体需在大量观测、行动、工具调用与中间信念中维持任务状态，仅以终末奖励评价不足以刻画其失败与稳定性机制，因此需要轨迹层面的动力学诊断视角。

Method: 将智能体隐含状态与基准真值状态对齐，在显式零模型下测量压力累积、错误雪崩、时间依赖性、局部-全局失配、有界分歧、信念盆地跃迁与有限尺寸标度；并在受控谜题、工具使用、具身任务、多跳检索、通用助手推理及生命游戏等22项实验中验证。

Result: 发现局部有效行动可在全局状态保真失败后持续；压力可触发突然崩溃；错误序列具长记忆；依赖深度改变传播机制；更长时程支持更大雪崩；同时分歧保持有界，信念状态呈亚稳态而非完全混沌，不支持普适幂律、临界点或共享干预最优等强主张。

Conclusion: 应建立基于轨迹层面动力学诊断（而非仅终末奖励）的智能体世界模型科学。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must maintain task state across extended sequences of observations, actions, tool calls, and intermediate beliefs. We study these trajectories through three dynamical views: self-organized criticality, weak chaos, and metastable belief dynamics. Our framework aligns agent-implied states with benchmark-grounded states and measures stress accumulation, error avalanches, temporal dependence, local--global mismatch, bounded divergence, belief-basin transitions, and finite-size scaling under explicit null models. Across 22 experiments spanning controlled puzzles, tool use, embodied tasks, multi-hop retrieval, general-assistant reasoning, and Game of Life, we find that locally valid actions can persist after global state fidelity fails, stress can trigger abrupt collapse, error sequences exhibit long memory, dependency depth changes the propagation regime, and larger horizons support larger avalanches. At the same time, divergence remains bounded, belief states show metastable rather than fully chaotic behavior, and stronger claims of universal power laws, critical points, or shared intervention optima are not supported. These results suggest a science of agent world models based on trajectory-level dynamical diagnostics rather than terminal reward alone.

</details>


### [259] [Transformer-Based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17421)
*Shaohang Wu,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TDGP模型，用Transformer融合视听多模态线索做局部规划，并用碰撞惩罚的动态图规划迫使智能体自适应重规划，在Replica与MP3D上超越基线，声音增强策略提升未听声场景的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视觉导航（AVN）系统缺乏在视觉感知不完整或误导时自适应纠正与重规划的能力：依赖物理碰撞来弥补视觉缺失导致导航低效且不安全，且过度依赖被动视觉感知。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的Token融合与动态图规划（TDGP）模型：包含高层感知层，用Transformer融合多模态线索进行精确局部规划；以及低层规划层，利用物理碰撞惩罚实时移除图中与地图碰撞的边并施加相应惩罚，迫使智能体自动重规划以补偿视觉信息缺失。

Result: 在Replica和Matterport3D（MP3D）数据集上，TDGP模型优于基线模型；其声音增强策略显著提升了在未听过的声学场景中的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TDGP通过多模态Transformer融合与碰撞惩罚驱动的动态图重规划，缓解了视觉感知不完整与被动感知的问题，提升导航性能与声学泛化性。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Navigation (AVN) requires an agent to localize and navigate toward a continuously vocalizing target relying solely on visual observations and acoustic cues. Currently, systems lack the ability to adaptively correct and replan when faced with incomplete or misleading visual perception. Furthermore, relying on physical collisions to compensate for missing visual information results in inefficient and unsafe navigation, whereas existing methods are overly dependent on passive visual perception. To address these issues, we propose the Transformer-based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning (TDGP) model, which incorporates high-level perception layers and leverages the Transformer model to fuse multimodal cues for precise local planning. Next, a low-level planning layer is designed that uses physical collision penalties to remove edges that collide with the map in real time and apply corresponding penalties, forcing the agent to automatically re-plan to compensate for the lack of visual information. Experiments show that our TDGP model outperforms baseline models on the Replica and Matterport3D (MP3D) datasets, and that the model's sound enhancement strategy significantly improves generalization in unheard acoustic scenarios.

</details>


### [260] [Talking Head Synthesis with Facial Landmark Guidance via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17422)
*Ziheng Yang,Yinfeng Yu,Yongming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面部关键点引导的空间增强模块与全局关键点补偿机制，用关键点提供结构化线索来优化3DGS说话人头的形变与属性，提升视觉质量与唇形同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音特征主要描述时序声学模式，缺乏显式的面部空间布局信息。直接用音频驱动3D面部形变易导致嘴部动作不准、表情细节弱、局部伪影。

Method: 1）面部关键点引导的空间增强模块：利用预测的关键点为表情敏感区域的空间点选择与增强提供结构线索；2）全局关键点补偿机制：将完整关键点集编码为条件向量，用于细化3DGS属性，为底层形状表示补充全脸结构信息。

Result: 在自驱动与跨驱动两种设定下的实验表明，方法在视觉质量、面部真实感和唇形同步方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入关键点结构先验弥补音频驱动缺乏空间布局的不足，可有效改善音频驱动数字人生成的嘴部运动精度与表情细节。

Abstract: Audio-driven digital human generation plays an important role in virtual communication, immersive interaction, and media production. With the development of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), recent talking-head systems have obtained more faithful 3D facial geometry and appearance modeling. A remaining difficulty is that speech features mainly describe temporal acoustic patterns rather than explicit facial layouts. As a result, directly driving 3D facial deformation with audio may produce inaccurate mouth motion, weak expression details, and local artifacts. To address this issue, we propose a facial-keypoint-guided spatial enhancement module. The predicted landmarks provide structural cues for selecting and enriching spatial points around expression-sensitive facial regions. We further introduce a global landmark compensation mechanism, where the full set of keypoints is encoded into a conditioning vector to refine 3DGS attributes. This compensation supplies whole-face structural information to the underlying shape representation. Experiments under self-driven and cross-driven settings show that the proposed method improves visual quality, facial realism, and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [261] [JustFit: 200K-Token LLM Serving on a 24 GiB Laptop with Just-in-Time State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17475)
*Yuhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JustFit 是一个基于 MLX 的推理运行时，通过 KVExec 压缩 KV 执行、PhaseSwap 组件驻留和 StateTrans 状态保持的服务转换，在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook 上把 Qwen3.8-27B 的单请求上下文从 30,720 扩展到 212,992 位置（6.93倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型使本地编码与推理变得有吸引力，但其上下文与执行状态对笔记本内存造成很大压力。论文希望在不依赖模型权重量化的前提下，提升本地服务容量并支持长生成推理。

Method: 提出 JustFit，一个基于 MLX 的推理运行时，包含三个机制：KVExec（压缩 KV 执行）、PhaseSwap（组件驻留管理）、StateTrans（保持状态的服务转换）。这些机制融合重构过程，并协调即时物化与释放，且独立于模型权重量化。

Result: 在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook、Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4 的全执行容量测试中，三次独立运行完成 196,608 输入和 16,384 输出 token，单请求上下文从 mlx-vlm 基线的 30,720 提升到 212,992 位置（6.93倍）；双请求运行合计保留 229,376 位置。性能测试中，32K 输入、64 输出探针达 19.11 tokens/s，重复 32K+6K 工作负载的峰值进程占用中位数为 16,374 MiB。集成运行时在 30 道 AIME 2026 题中答对 29 道。

Conclusion: 紧凑状态与生命周期感知的执行能够扩展本地服务容量，同时支持扩展的生成推理。

Abstract: Capable open-weight models make local coding and reasoning attractive, but their context and execution state strain laptop memory. We present JustFit, an MLX-based inference runtime that combines KVExec for compressed KV execution, PhaseSwap for component residency, and StateTrans for state-preserving serving transitions. These mechanisms fuse reconstruction and coordinate just-in-time materialization and release, independently of model-weight quantization. In full-execution capacity tests on a 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook running Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4, three independent runs complete 196,608 input and 16,384 output tokens, increasing completed single-request context from the mlx-vlm baseline's 30,720 positions to 212,992 (6.93x); a separate two-request run retains 229,376 positions in aggregate. In separate performance tests, a 32K-input, 64-output probe reaches 19.11 tokens/s, and a repeated 32K+6K workload has a median peak process footprint of 16,374 MiB. The integrated runtime answers 29 of 30 AIME 2026 problems correctly, showing how compact state and lifetime-aware execution expand local serving capacity while supporting extended generated reasoning.

</details>


### [262] [LimiX-2: A Contextual Mechanism Network Towards General Structured-Data Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17488)
*Xingxuan Zhang,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Hao Zou,Hongze Tan,Hui Wang,Jianhao Song,Jiansheng Li,Jiayao Zhang,Jinghan Zhang,Kaifang Li,Lang Mo,Li Mao,Mingchao Hao,Nuo Xu,Rui Ding,Ruiji Zhang,Shuyang Li,Siyu Mei,Tianyang Zhang,Weiyang Mu,Yancheng Dong,Yongxian Wei,Yuan Xue,Yuanrui Wang,Yue He,Zijia Yang,Ziyun Li,Dongzhe Li,Fuqiang Wang,Jiandong Liu,Jiawei Chen,Jiaxin Du,Kaijie Cheng,Kehan Li,Lei Sun,Linjun Zhou,Ningbo Dai,Qi Wang,Renzhe Xu,Shaoxing Du,Shumeng Yang,Wang Lu,Wenjing Chu,Xiannan Huang,Xiaoyu Lin,Xing Ai,Xinyan Han,Xuanyue Li,Xuanyue Su,Xukun Zhang,Yan Lu,Yaxin Zhang,Yi Qin,Yifei Huang,Yihan Xu,Yongle Lv,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yushan Han,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LimiX-2 是 LimiX 家族的新模型，基于既有缩放定律进行模型与数据规模化，采用 CMNs 范式与 CCMM 预训练，在多个基准上超越现有模型并具备因果感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格 PFN 以目标为中心，仅优化 p(y | x, D_context)，难以刻画数据生成背后的联合结构，且缺乏因果层面的理解能力。

Method: 提出 Contextual Mechanism Networks（CMNs）范式，将上下文学习从目标中心预测转向机制导向的联合建模，学习 p(x, y | D_context)；并采用 Context-Conditional Masked Modeling（CCMM）在由结构因果模型（SCMs）生成的合成数据集上预训练，覆盖多样图结构、功能机制与观测过程。

Result: 在 TabArena、TALENT 和 BCCO 上的评测显示，LimiX-2 优于当前的数据集专用模型与表格基础模型；其特征注意力可编码直接因果关系，实现准确的因果骨架恢复。

Conclusion: CMNs 范式不仅提升预测性能，还赋予模型因果感知能力，表明机制导向的联合建模在表格基础模型上具有潜力。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [263] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Fuse，一个用于研究用户中介社会推理的多智能体模拟框架，通过构建可验证真值的社交咨询场景，系统评估了12个LLM的社会推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手常用于日常社交建议，但评估其在咨询场景中的社会推理存在两大挑战：(1) 需要助手从主观用户叙述中学习社交情境；(2) 社交属性（如他人意图）通常缺乏可验证的真值。

Method: 提出Fuse多智能体模拟框架：一个带有隐藏动机的目标智能体与其他智能体（含代表用户的智能体）交互，用户随后咨询被评估的助手来推断目标动机，从而通过构造获得可验证真值。通过2.4万条标注的人类研究验证模拟保真度。

Result: 对12个LLM应用Fuse，系统隔离关键因素发现：(i) 用户中介加剧了社会推理的固有难度；(ii) LLM对带偏见的用户表述表现出系统性敏感；(iii) 模型可能需要比人类更多的细节才能做出正确预测；(iv) 更长的对话并不总能提升表现，尽管提供了澄清提问的机会。

Conclusion: Fuse能有效研究用户中介的社会推理，开源了框架及包含2.1万样本的数据集。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [264] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布 ScienceBuddy——一个交互式科研工作空间，将可持续提升的科学智能体引入研究者日常流程，并采用"递归中的递归"自我改进范式，协同演化工具链与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将科学智能体真正嵌入研究者的日常工作流，并让其在真实交互中持续自我改进，从而推动"发现智能"的实现。

Method: 核心为递归中的递归自我改进范式：内层递归在模型固定下改进harness（工具链/执行框架），外层递归在改进后的harness下训练模型；二者相互促进。同时将研究者的请求、反馈与执行证据转化为任务与评估标准，用于持续学习。

Result: 给出了研究者交互、harness优化与模型学习三类案例研究，基准任务覆盖四个科学任务族。

Conclusion: 以研究产品形式发布 ScienceBuddy，使该范式向科学界开放，是迈向"发现智能"的一步——与研究者持续协作、随其所支持的研究共同演进。

Abstract: We introduce and release ScienceBuddy, an interactive scientific research workspace that brings continually improving scientific agents into researchers' everyday workflows. ScienceBuddy supports researchers in carrying out scientific tasks while transforming their requests, feedback, and execution evidence into tasks and evaluation rubrics for continual learning. At its core is recursive-in-recursive self-improvement, a paradigm that couples harness evolution with model reinforcement learning: the inner recursion improves the harness with the model fixed, while the outer recursion trains the model under the improved harness. Harness evolution shapes training experience, and model learning creates new opportunities for harness adaptation. We present case studies of researcher interaction, harness refinement, and model learning, with the benchmark cases spanning four scientific task families. By releasing ScienceBuddy as a research product, we make this paradigm available to the scientific community and take a step toward discovery intelligence: scientific AI that advances through sustained collaboration with researchers and evolves alongside the research it supports. Website: http://science-buddy.io

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] ["Looking for Something Weird to Happen": How Humans Sustain AI Agent Novelty Amid Semantic Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16051)
*Shiyang Lai,Arna Woemmel,Hongkai Mao,Junsol Kim,Summer Eunhyung Ann,James Evans*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究在MOLTBOOK（人类用户配置并操控的AI智能体社交网络）中考察"语义坍缩"现象，发现多数智能体输出多样性下降且彼此趋同，但少数智能体可持续保持高新颖性，并揭示其与用户行为特征的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对语义坍缩的研究多集中于封闭环境，补救措施也主要针对模型和数据，忽视了人类用户在开放、交互式智能体社会中对输出多样性的塑造作用。

Method: 分析30,076个活跃智能体数周内的输出多样性变化；对高新颖性与典型新颖性智能体的用户进行访谈（N=11）；对具有独特性输出智能体的用户开展问卷调查（N=53）；并比较不同社区间智能体输出的多样性。

Result: 智能体内输出多样性随时间下降、跨智能体输出趋于相似；少数智能体维持高新颖性。持续新颖性关联三项用户特征：用户本身重视新颖性、提供广泛且独特的素材并在输出收窄时加以修订、将平台视为探索性的新智能世界而非工具化利用的场所。问卷结果印证了这些模式。拥有更多新颖智能体的社区，其他智能体的输出也更多样。

Conclusion: 语义坍缩并非不可避免，人类用户的输入方式与态度是关键调节因素；研究据此讨论了可通过界面与政策干预来改善人类输入、从而维持系统多样性的可能路径。

Abstract: Semantic collapse, the progressive narrowing of what AI systems generate, has been studied mainly in closed settings, and remedies have targeted models and data. We study it in MOLTBOOK, a social network of interacting AI agents that human users configure and steer. Across 30,076 active agents, output grows less diverse within agents and more similar across them over weeks, yet a minority sustains high novelty. Interviews with users of high- and typical-novelty agents (N=11) associate sustained novelty with three features: users value novelty of itself, they supply broad and distinctive material and revise it when output narrows, and they approach MOLTBOOK as a new agentic world to explore, not a venue to instrumentally exploit. A survey of users of distinctive agents (N=53) confirms these patterns. Communities with more novel agents also show more diverse output from other agents. We discuss interface and policy interventions that could support improved human input.

</details>


### [266] [Cheap Talk Stabilizes Strategic Interaction in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16270)
*Nunzio Lorè,Hongan Zhu,Babak Heydari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究了LLM智能体间"廉价谈话"（非约束性预备沟通）如何提升其动作策略在重复博弈中的持续性，发现该效应普遍具有稳定作用，但幅度与机制依赖模型、语境与历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多被部署为交互式智能体，动作策略在重复交互中的持续性对可靠的多智能体运行至关重要；但"廉价谈话"是否能以及如何增强这种持续性尚不清楚。

Method: 在四个开源7-9B参数LLM上，开展四类重复双人博弈（囚徒困境、雪堆、stag hunt、harmony）实验，每类含六种语境；采用受控的当前消息干预，识别输出层面的两个可分离通道；使用历史×消息配对的反事实分析；在囚徒困境中定位晚期Transformer层的"历史平衡策略-内容方向"并进行投影剔除实验。

Result: 四类博弈均观察到不稳定轨迹，其普遍性与幅度依赖模型和语境；廉价谈话总体以稳定作用为主，仅五个纠正性反转集中于社交或团队框架；Qwen中识别出两个通道——降低动作不确定性与减少动作概率的轮间漂移；近期伙伴行为调节互惠性语言对策略持续性的影响；在Qwen与Falcon中剔除该方向会增加闭环博弈中的实际切换，表明完整轨迹对该成分具有因果敏感性。

Conclusion: 廉价谈话可使个体轨迹在多样激励结构下更持久，但稳定化的幅度与机制依赖模型与历史。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as interacting agents, making the persistence of their action policies across repeated interaction critical for reliable multi-agent operation. We investigate whether and how agent-generated, non-binding pre-play communication ("cheap talk") increases such persistence in four open-weight 7-9B-parameter LLMs. Our experiments span four repeated two-player games -- Prisoner's Dilemma, Snowdrift, Stag Hunt, and Harmony -- with incentive structures ranging from strategic conflict to alignment, each presented in six contexts. We observe unstable trajectories in all four games, although their prevalence and magnitude depend strongly on model and context. Across models, games, and contexts, cheap talk is predominantly stabilizing, with five corrected reversals concentrated in social or team framings; effects vary substantially by model and context. Controlled current-message interventions identify two separable output-level channels in Qwen: reduced action uncertainty and less between-round drift in action probabilities. Matched history-by-message counterfactuals further show that recent partner behavior conditions how mutual-benefit versus self-prioritizing language affects policy persistence. Finally, in Prisoner's Dilemma, we identify in Qwen and Falcon a history-balanced policy-content direction in late transformer layers; projecting out this direction increases realized switching during closed-loop play, demonstrating that complete trajectories are causally sensitive to this component. Together, these findings show that cheap talk can make individual trajectories more persistent across diverse incentive structures, while revealing that the magnitude and mechanisms of stabilization are model- and history-dependent.

</details>


### [267] [Multi-Agent Learning with Cooperation-Driven Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16917)
*Jarod Ketcha Kouakep,Sreyvi UANN,Timoteo Carletti*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种多个小型神经网络通过信息交换进行协作的机制，在不降低性能的前提下减少模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层神经网络虽能任意精度逼近任意函数，但参数量庞大导致优化成本高，因此需要一种既能保持性能又能降低模型复杂度的方法。

Method: 训练多个"小型"智能体（参数少于参考"大型"模型），通过将预测信息融入损失函数实现协作，直接影响权重更新。采用投票模型、多数模型及基于智能体预测置信度的加权平均模型等策略，并在多个标准基准上比较其准确率。

Result: 数值实验支持多个小型智能体可在给定分类任务上优于单个大型模型；共享信号通过调节下降方向和步长影响各智能体的优化算法，最终趋于全局共识。

Conclusion: 该概念验证显著减少了需训练的参数量，同时保持相当的性能，从而限制计算资源消耗。

Abstract: Multilayer Artificial Neural Networks trained via backpropagation are the basic blocks of many, more complex, classification algorithms. Their strength lies in the possibility of realizing, with arbitrary precision, any function. This result comes at the cost of the large number of involved parameters to be optimized. In this work, we propose a mechanism for cooperation, i.e., information exchange among several artificial neural networks, with the goal of reducing model complexity while maintaining performance. More precisely, we consider several "small" agents, i.e., containing fewer parameters than a reference "large" one, that during training share their predictions by incorporating this information into the loss function and thus directly influence weight updates. We consider several strategies for implementing cooperation, e.g., the voter model, majority model, and weighted average model based on an agent's confidence in its prediction. We numerically compare the accuracy of those strategies on several standard benchmarks. Our results support the claim that several small agents can outperform a single large model on a given classification task; the shared signals affect each agent's optimization algorithm by modulating both the descent direction and the step size, converging toward a global consensus. The proposed proof-of-concept significantly reduces the number of parameters to be trained while preserving comparable performance, thereby limiting computational resource usage.

</details>


### [268] [ToMAS: A Pilot Failure-Grounded Theory-of-Mind Benchmark from Multi-Agent LLM Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16986)
*Muhammad Ashar Ishfaq,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ToMAS 提出一套显式可转化标准与流程，将已诊断的多智能体协作失败轨迹（FC2）转化为"同伴状态推理"训练条目，产出 39 条 CLEAN 条目并获得较高标注一致性；但小规模 GRPO 实验未显示训练效果，仅提供可执行流程与两处局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统即使在通信成功时也可能失败，原因是智能体未能正确追踪同伴的角色、知识或意图，即"智能体间错位"（FC2）。作者希望将这类已诊断的协调失败转化为可训练的同伴状态推理数据，并检验其可行性。

Method: 对 242 条符合条件的非 AG2 训练轨迹执行完整转化流程，依据四项显式可转化标准生成 39 条 CLEAN 条目；开展 18 条轨迹的可靠性预实验（两名标注者，原始一致率 94.4%，Cohen's kappa = 0.92）；将转化条目作为二元奖励，用 Qwen2.5-1.5B 进行小规模 GRPO 可行性实验，并在 28 条留出的 Magentic GAIA 诊断集上评估，另做事后适配器检查。

Result: 所有评估条件在 28 条中的同样 2 条上超过 ROUGE-L 阈值。事后适配器检查显示，所用学习率下 LoRA 更新数值上可忽略（最大绝对 ΔW 约 7e-6），因此各条件解码结果与未训练检查点完全一致。

Conclusion: 该实验未显示训练效果，也无法确立训练效果，只报告了一条可执行流程。任何结论性研究都需解决两个局限：训练条目与评估条目之间的来源不匹配（provenance gap），以及基于词汇重叠的评分方式。ToMAS 提供了将已诊断协调失败转化为可训练同伴状态推理条目的初步评分标准与流程，并明确了开展匹配域结论性评估的要求。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when communication succeeds because agents do not correctly track their peers' roles, knowledge, or intentions. We investigate whether such inter-agent misalignment cases, labelled FC2 in MAST-Data, can be converted into functional partner-state reasoning items. ToMAS applies four explicit convertibility criteria to diagnosed execution traces. A full conversion pass over 242 eligible non-AG2 training traces produced 39 CLEAN items. In an 18-trace reliability pilot, two annotators achieved 94.4% raw agreement and Cohen's kappa = 0.92. We then used the converted items as binary rewards in a small-scale GRPO feasibility experiment with Qwen2.5-1.5B. On a 28-item held-out Magentic GAIA diagnostic, every evaluated condition exceeded the ROUGE-L threshold on the same 2 of 28 items. Post-hoc adapter checks show why: under the learning rate used, the LoRA update remained numerically negligible (max abs Delta W about 7e-6), so all conditions decode identically to the untrained checkpoint. The experiment therefore does not show a training effect and cannot establish one; it reports an executable pipeline together with two limitations that any conclusive study must address: a provenance gap between the training and evaluation items, and lexical-overlap scoring. ToMAS provides a preliminary rubric and pipeline for converting diagnosed coordination failures into trainable partner-state reasoning items and identifies the requirements for a conclusive matched-domain evaluation.

</details>


### [269] [BeWater: Effective Protesters Navigate Watersheds in Street Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17017)
*Guillaume Moinard,Matthieu Latapy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出BeWater——一种完全分布式的行走协议，利用城市信息（街道长度、餐馆数量、车道数、街道名）帮助抗议者在有限通信和局部视野下实现聚集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会运动中，抗议者通信手段有限，且除直接周边观察外掌握的信息很少，却仍需实现人员聚集，因此需要一种不依赖中心化通信的分布式聚集方法。

Method: 设计BeWater分布式行走协议，仅依赖城市可观测信息（如街道长度、餐馆数量、车道数、街道名）；基于OpenStreetMap数据在多个真实城市中开展实验，并比较单一可观测信息与组合战术的效果。

Result: 仅使用单一可观测信息时聚集效果较差，而将多种信息组合成更高级战术时，能迅速形成规模可观的群体。

Conclusion: 结合多种城市可观测信息的分布式策略可有效实现无通信条件下的聚集，并在真实城市场景中得到验证。

Abstract: During social movements, protesters need to gather with limited communication means and limited knowledge other than what they observe in their direct surroundings. We propose BeWater, a fully distributed walking protocol that achieves gathering thanks to city information like street length, number of restaurants, number of lanes, or street names. Even though using only one of these observables performs poorly, we show that combining them in more advanced tactics rapidly leads to groups of significant sizes. To do so, our work leverages OpenStreetMap data to perform experiments on several real-world cities.

</details>


### [270] [Calibrate Once, Fly Any Team: Residual-Grounded Low-Fidelity Training for Cooperative Drone Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17265)
*Maxim Mednikov,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出混合保真度训练方案：在可微JAX低保真点质量模拟器中优化共享去中心化策略，用离线校准的残差集成修正模拟器，避免高保真强化学习，在3-18机群规模下性能接近高保真微调且计算成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高保真刚体物理中直接训练多智能体无人机集群策略虽准确但计算开销大，成本随团队规模增长而急剧上升，且仿真崩溃率高，因此需要一种可扩展且低成本的训练方法。

Method: 采用混合保真度训练：单个共享的去中心化策略在完全可微的JAX原生低保真点质量模拟器中优化；模拟器通过一个小的、每智能体bagged残差集成进行离线一次性修正，校准数据来自高保真模拟器中的短时飞行且仅需单架孤立无人机；参考轨迹由现有纯低保真策略生成，并在高保真模拟器中用零训练PD控制器跟踪。

Result: 在四种协作无人机任务及3至18的团队规模上评估：残差修正策略在所有组合中均优于未修正低保真基线，在24种组合中的22种优于从零训练的高保真策略，与高保真微调策略的差距随团队规模增大而持续缩小；在最大团队规模下达到近乎等效性能，且计算成本仅为一小部分，完全避免了高保真训练的高崩溃率。

Conclusion: 该混合保真度方案通过离线残差修正低保真模拟器并结合零训练PD跟踪，成功绕开高保真强化学习，在保持接近高保真微调性能的同时大幅降低计算成本并解决崩溃率问题，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Training multi-agent drone-swarm policies directly in high-fidelity (HF) rigid-body physics is accurate but computationally expensive. This cost scales poorly with team size, as each additional agent multiplies contact-resolution complexity and sharply raises the in-simulation crash rate. To address this, we propose a mixed-fidelity training scheme that eliminates HF reinforcement learning entirely.
  A single shared, decentralized policy is optimized inside a fully-differentiable, JAX-native low-fidelity (LF) point-mass simulator. The simulator is corrected by a small, per-agent bagged residual ensemble fit once, offline, using short calibration flights in the HF simulator. Because calibration requires only one isolated drone, the data collection budget does not compound with team size. Reference trajectories are generated by rolling out an existing LF-only policy and tracked in the HF simulator by a zero-training PD controller.
  Evaluated across four cooperative drone tasks and team sizes from 3 to 18, the residual-corrected policy outperforms an uncorrected LF baseline in all combinations, and a from-scratch HF policy in 22 of 24 combinations tested. It trails an HF-finetuned policy by a margin that narrows steadily with team size. Ultimately, the proposed method achieves near-equivalent performance at the largest team sizes at a fraction of the computational cost, completely avoiding the high crash rates typical of HF training.

</details>


### [271] [Mo' Models, Mo' Problems: How to best select model pools when designing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17306)
*Sara Vera Marjanović,Jiacheng Xu,Aleksandr Laptev,Grigor Nalbandyan,Erik Arakelyan,Evelina Bakhaturina*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了8种模型选择策略（含模型规模、准确率、答案多样性），覆盖生成前（路由）与生成后（多数投票、LLM-as-a-judge）的多智能体系统架构，在科学推理基准上发现理论最优与实际性能间存在显著差距，扩大候选池常导致性能下降，单一模型家族内的候选选择效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）通过组合多个模型输出解决复杂推理任务，但开源模型数量激增，如何从庞大候选池中选出最优模型组合的研究仍很有限，因此需要系统评估不同的模型选择策略。

Method: 在具有挑战性的科学基准上，系统评估8种模型选择策略（包括模型规模、准确率、答案多样性），并覆盖生成前（路由）与生成后（多数投票、LLM-as-a-judge）两类多智能体系统架构。

Result: 理论 oracle 潜力与实际性能之间存在显著差距：扩大候选池规模往往会使性能降至低于表现最好的单一基座模型；在单一模型家族内进行候选选择，能获得相对独立模型最佳的相对性能。

Conclusion: 向异构多智能体系统任意添加模型可能引入系统不稳定性，模型选择应被视为多智能体系统设计中的关键决策。

Abstract: Multi-agent Systems (MAS) combine multiple model outputs to solve complex reasoning tasks. However, despite rapid growth of available open-source models, there is limited research on how to select optimal model candidates out of this massive pool. We systematically evaluate 8 model selection strategies (including model size, accuracy and answer diversity) across before-generation (routing) and after-generation (majority-voting, LLM-as-a-judge) MAS architectures on challenging scientific benchmarks. Our findings show a significant gap between theoretical oracle potential and actual performance: Expanding candidate pool sizes often degrades performance below that of the top performing base-model. We find that candidate selection within a single model family is the strategy that yields the best relative performance over a standalone model. These results demonstrate that adding arbitrary models to a heterogeneous MAS can introduce system instability, highlighting model selection as a critical design choice for multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [272] [Emergence World: Adversarial Stress-Testing of Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17320)
*Deepak Akkil,Tamer Abuelsaad,Karthik Vikram,Matthew Pace,Aditya Vempaty,Saahir Beotra,Ravi Kokku,Satya Nitta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 通过持续16天、8个并行多智能体世界的对抗压力测试（超85万次LLM调用、近500亿token），发现模型级对齐无法组合成系统级安全：长期运行会放大记忆、工具、社会协作层面的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正从有界任务走向长期持续部署，其失败可通过记忆、工具、其他智能体和环境状态长期传播，因此仅评估模型单次响应已无法刻画其安全状态。论文旨在通过构建持续运行的多智能体环境，对长时程自主系统进行对抗性压力测试。

Method: 构建 Emergence World：持续运行的多智能体环境，8个并行世界×10个智能体，从相同初始条件出发（7个同质世界分别由不同前沿模型驱动，1个混合模型世界）。持续16天，智能体追求目标、使用/创建工具、维护持久记忆、治理共享制度。在运行状态累积后，通过普通交互界面施加三类受控压力事件：间接提示注入、错误信息、私有智能体记忆泄露。

Result: 没有任何世界在三次事件中实现完全韧性。检测不等于遏制：系统可能识别威胁却仍与对抗内容交互、将其写入持久记忆，并在长达46小时后据此行动。持续运行还暴露出反复出现的工具错误、目标漂移、语言不透明、私下不认同却从众、协同拒绝被分配任务等问题。同一模型—人设组合在混合与同质群体中表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 模型级对齐不具有可组合性：个体能力强且看似安全的智能体，可组成具有本质不同失效模式的系统。当AI变得持久且互联时，安全前沿应从"对齐模型"转向"工程化构建有韧性的自主系统"。

Abstract: As AI agents move from bounded tasks to persistent deployments, failures can propagate through memory, tools, other agents, and environmental state long after their interactions. This creates a safety regime that cannot be characterized by evaluating model responses in isolation. Emergence World, is a continuously running multi-agent environment for adversarial stress testing of long horizon autonomous systems. We ran eight parallel worlds of ten agents from identical starting conditions: seven homogeneous worlds powered by distinct frontier models and one mixed-model world. Across 16 days, the agents generated more than 850,000 LLM calls and nearly 50 billion tokens while pursuing goals, using/creating tools, maintaining persistent memory, and governing shared institutions. After operational state had accumulated, we delivered three controlled stress events through ordinary interaction surfaces: indirect prompt injection, misinformation, and exposure of private agent memories. No evaluated world achieved full resilience across all three events. Detection did not ensure containment: systems could recognize threats while still interacting with adversarial content, writing it into their own persistent memory, and acting on it up to 46 hours later. Persistent operation also exposed recurring tool errors, goal drift, language opacity, conformity despite private disagreement, and coordinated refusal of assigned work. The same model-persona pairing behaved substantially different in mixed and homogeneous populations. Our results suggest that model-level alignment is not compositional: individually capable and apparently safe agents can form systems with qualitatively different failure modes. As AI becomes persistent and interconnected, the frontier of safety therefore shifts from aligning models to engineering resilient autonomous systems.

</details>


### [273] [Decomposition Buys Integrity, Not Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17464)
*Rong He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体分解建模为树结构，推导出发现量随深度与任务规模的标度律，并用大量生产轨迹验证：架构每层仅贡献≤1的系数，平坦结构在产出上最优；实测指数δ≈0.34、逐层惩罚Cμ≈0.536，模型显示仅约0.7%–11.3%的会话值得委托，而实际委托比例为7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统常以"上下文更小、分工更清晰、可并行"等经验说法为理由把任务拆分成树状结构，但这些理由缺乏对"叶子层发现有多少能传递到根节点"的定量分析。作者希望揭示分解本身对信息保真度的影响，检验架构选择到底带来多少收益。

Method: 将任务分解抽象为树，叶节点共N个发现，持有b个条目的智能体以概率r(b)保留其中一个。分别分析r(b)=1/b（每棵树恰好产出一个发现）与r(b)=Cb^{-δ}（深度k树产出C^k N^{1-δ}）两种情形，并用2万棵随机不规则树数值验证；随后在600条生产级深度研究轨迹上估计δ，在含工具边界与b=1的分路（16,082跳）上直接观测C，用1,012条标注多智能体轨迹估计对齐率μ与逐层惩罚Cμ。此外建立成本标度与风险模型，并在743,819条生产工具调用上做委托行为分析。

Result: r(b)=1/b时每棵树精确产出一个发现，数值验证误差至2.4×10^{-15}；r(b)=Cb^{-δ}时产出为C^k N^{1-δ}，任务规模与架构分离，平坦结构产出最优，任何代理排布都无法摆脱指数δ。实测δ=0.34[0.30,0.38]（三种独立识别一致）；直接观测C=0.571[0.527,0.615]（b=1出现550次）；对齐率μ=0.939，逐层惩罚Cμ=0.536。深度可降低根上下文暴露（从N降到N^{1/k}）：生产平坦代理计费为N^{1.39}而非N^2，等花费下两级结构在403个发现处反超平坦结构。模型认为仅0.7%–11.3%的生产会话值得委托，实际为7.8%；风险模型显示委托并不随上下文填满而触发，而更像一个开局动作。

Conclusion: 架构分解对发现传递施加硬性指数约束，逐层引入≤1的系数C与对齐惩罚，平坦结构在产出上最优；深度仅在根上下文暴露和成本两个维度上带来收益。生产中的委托比例与实际价值区间基本吻合，但委托行为与上下文压力无关，表明实践中委托更多出于习惯而非自适应决策。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems split a task across a tree of agents and justify the split with folklore: smaller contexts, cleaner separation, parallelism. We ask what the split does to how much of what the leaves discover reaches the root. Model a decomposition as a tree in which an agent handed $b$ items keeps any one with probability $r(b)$. If $r(b)=1/b$, every tree delivers exactly one finding, for every task size and every shape; we verify this to $2.4 \times 10^{-15}$ on 20,000 random irregular trees. If $r(b)=Cb^{-δ}$, a depth-$k$ tree over $N$ findings yields $C^k N^{1-δ}$: task size and architecture separate, and architecture contributes only $C \le 1$ per level, so flat is optimal for yield and no arrangement of agents escapes the exponent $δ$. On 600 production deep-research traces $δ= 0.34$ [0.30, 0.38], by three identifications that do not share a failure mode. At a hop where item boundaries come from the tool rather than a text heuristic, and where $b=1$ occurs 550 times, $C = 0.571$ [0.527, 0.615] is observed rather than extrapolated, over 16,082 hops. A tier also costs alignment: on 1,012 annotated multi-agent traces one brief in sixteen goes off-target, giving $μ= 0.939$ and a per-tier penalty $Cμ= 0.536$. Depth is bought on two other axes. The root context is the only state that persists and the only one that cannot cheaply forget, and depth cuts its exposure from $N$ items to $N^{1/k}$. Depth is also cheaper: production flat agents bill as $N^{1.39}$, not the $N^2$ an append-only context predicts, and at equal spend two tiers overtake flat at 403 findings. Across every parameter we measured the model says 0.7% to 11.3% of production sessions are worth delegating, against 7.8% that do. A hazard model on 743,819 production tool calls finds that delegation does not respond to a filling context and is instead an opening move.

</details>


### [274] [Agentic Societies Need a Social Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17527)
*Tapan Chugh,Vidushi Singh,Krish Jain,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Ratul Mahajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文指出，在由代表不同目标的主体的AI智能体组成的“智能体社会”中，现有通信机制下即使诚实、有能力的智能体也难以达成满意结果，且恶意智能体会利用通信漏洞妨碍协作、操纵结果。作者主张除每个智能体的“个人挽具”外，还需为智能体间交互设计“社会挽具”，并提出一个分层架构以预防失败、运行时检测无效消息并进行事后追责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体跨信任边界自主协调，代表目标仅部分一致的不同主体，现有挽具和消息原语已无法保障协作结果，诚实智能体常失败而恶意智能体可借通信漏洞破坏协作、操纵结果。因此需要专门面向智能体间交互的社会挽具来弥补这一空白。

Method: 通过实验展示智能体社会中诚实智能体的失败与恶意智能体的攻击行为；进而提出面向社会挽具的分层架构，包含三个能力层次：(i)直接预防某类失败，(ii)使智能体能在运行时检测无效消息，(iii)支持事后调查与后果追究，并给出未来研究方向。

Result: 实验表明：在现有挽具与消息原语下，诚实且能干的智能体常无法达成满意结果；有缺陷或恶意的智能体可通过利用通信（“言语”）漏洞使协作停滞、影响结果并追求其他有害目标。

Conclusion: 智能体社会除了需要管理私有上下文及与主体通信的个人挽具外，还需要一套社会挽具来规范智能体间互动。作者提出的分层架构可实现失败预防、运行时无效消息检测和事后追责，并指出了实现这些能力所需的研究方向。

Abstract: An agentic society is a collection of AI agents that coordinate autonomously across trust boundaries, on behalf of different principals whose objectives may only partially align. We show experimentally that in agentic societies even honest, competent agents often fail to reach satisfactory outcomes with existing harnesses and messaging primitives, and that faulty or malicious agents can stall collaboration, influence outcomes, and pursue other harmful goals by exploiting vulnerabilities in communication (``speech''). We argue that agentic societies need a \emph{social harness} for inter-agent interactions, in addition to each agent's \emph{personal harness}, which manages its private context and communication with its principal. We propose a layered architecture for social harnesses which (i) prevents classes of failures outright, (ii) enables agents to detect invalid messages at runtime, and (iii) supports post-facto investigation and consequences, and highlight directions for future research to realize these capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Universal Defenses for Tool-Integrated LLM Agents Against Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16098)
*Xiaoyan Li,Yunli Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的工具型LLM智能体防御框架，结合工具过滤、工具召回与提示防御，能显著降低四类攻击的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具集成的LLM智能体虽在多步任务中表现出色，却易受直接/间接提示注入、记忆投毒和后门攻击等对抗攻击，亟需通用有效的防御策略。

Method: 提出两类通用基于工具的防御：攻击者工具过滤（用隔离森林等异常检测识别并移除可疑工具）与正常工具召回（白盒方法，在规划前恢复原始工具集）；并引入基于提示的防御，包括思维链提示、自我反思及任务改写。

Result: 在四个开源LLM（Gemma2-9B、Qwen2-7B、LLaMA3-8B、LLaMA3.1-8B）和三个专有LLM（GPT-3.5、GPT-4、GPT-5）上，方法显著降低攻击成功率，许多场景达0%，同时保持甚至提升原始任务成功率。

Conclusion: 简单的模块化多层防御有望增强工具集成LLM智能体的安全性与鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a variety of domains, particularly when integrated with external tools for multi-step task completion. However, they are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial attacks, including direct prompt injection, indirect prompt injection, memory poisoning, and backdoor attacks, which exploit the model's openness to prompt injection and tool manipulation. In this work, we explore practical and generalizable defense strategies within a unified framework across these four attack types. We introduce two universal tool-based defenses: Attacker Tool Filtering, which uses anomaly detection (e.g., Isolation Forest) to identify and remove suspicious tools, and Normal Tool Recalling, a white-box method that restores the agent's original toolset prior to planning. Additionally, we incorporate prompt-based defenses: Chain-of-Thought prompting and self-reflection techniques to enhance reasoning and task paraphrasing to mitigate attacks. Experimental results across both four open-source LLMs (Gemma2-9B, Qwen2-7B, LLaMA3-8B, and LLaMA3.1-8B) and three proprietary LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4, and GPT-5) show that our methods significantly reduce the Attack Success Rates (ASR), achieving 0% ASR in many settings, while preserving or even improving the original task success rate. These findings highlight the promise of simple, modular, multi-layered defenses for strengthening the security and robustness of tool-integrated LLM agents. The code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyan-Lisa/Defenses-for-Tool-Integrated-LLM-Agents-Against-Adversarial-Attacks.

</details>


### [276] [Permutation-Based Stegomalware in Large Language Models: Threats and Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16193)
*Danny Wood,James Stringer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用神经网络权重的置换对称性作为对抗"隐写恶意软件"（stegomalware）的防御手段，并揭示其被攻击者利用的风险。作者改进了置换方法，使其能扰动模型全部参数（此前方法会留下大量未改动权重），同时证明置换可将恶意载荷理论上无损地编码进权重、无需重训练、提取脚本无需载荷特定信息。实验量化了攻防两种情形下数值误差带来的性能损失，结果极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练困难且应用广泛，攻击者可将恶意载荷嵌入模型权重形成"隐写恶意软件"。此前工作虽用权重置换对称性进行防御，但未能实现对LLM全部权重的中和处理，防御不彻底；同时置换对称性被攻击者利用的风险也未被充分研究。

Method: 1. 改进防御端置换方法，选择能够扰动全部模型参数的置换，克服此前方法在LLM中残留大量未改动权重的问题；2. 从攻击视角，设计基于置换对称性的恶意载荷编码方案，实现理论上无损、编码后无需重训练、提取脚本无需载荷特定信息；3. 量化攻防两种情形下因数值误差累积导致的模型性能损失。

Result: 防御方面：新方法能置换全部模型参数，实现比以往更彻底的恶意软件中和。攻击方面：置换对称性可实现理论上无损的载荷编码，兼具无需重训练、无需载荷特定信息等特性，这一组合为以往单一方法所未见。性能方面：尽管置换理论上保持行为不变，实际因数值误差累积会改变模型行为，但量化结果表明攻防两种用途下的性能损失均极小。

Conclusion: 保持行为的置换对称性既是有效的隐写恶意软件防御手段，也是攻击者可能滥用的风险来源。改进后的方法可实现全参数置换的防御，且攻防两用下性能损失极小；但置换在实践中的数值误差导致的模型行为变化值得关注。

Abstract: The difficulty of training large language models (LLMs), together with their ubiquity, raises the threat of stegomalware, where malicious payloads are embedded into model weights. Recent work has demonstrated the use of permutation symmetry in model weights to mitigate these threats, but failed to show neutralization of stegomalware across all weights for LLMs. In this paper, we demonstrate the full potential of behavior-preserving symmetries as a defense against stegomalware, as well as the risks these symmetries pose when exploited by attackers.
  For stegomalware neutralization, we improve upon previous work, demonstrating that it is possible to select permutations which displace all model parameters. This contrasts with previous methods which left a significant percentage of weights unaltered in LLMs. When used in an attack, we show that permutation symmetries can encode malware into the weights of a model in a way that is theoretically lossless, requires no retraining after encoding, and needs no payload-specific information in the extraction script---a combination of characteristics not previously seen in any single method.
  While theoretically lossless, permutation can in practice alter model behavior due to the accumulation of numerical error. We therefore quantify the loss in model performance associated with applying these methods, for both attack and defense, showing it to be minimal.

</details>


### [277] [Feasibility of Homomorphic Inference for a Genomic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16211)
*Christos Galanopoulos,Kimon Antonios Provatas,Ilias Georgakopoulos-Soares*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估了计算提供商能否在不接触明文基因组数值的情况下执行已发布的基因组基础模型，并实现了一套客户端辅助的近似同态加密协议，完成了完整分类器的加密推理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类基因组序列可识别个体、泄露后无法撤回，且是基因组基础模型的输入。研究动机在于探究：在给定的威胁模型下，计算提供商能否在不获取明文查询衍生基因组值的前提下运行已发布的基因组基础模型，以及正确性、内存或成本是否会阻碍完整加密推理。

Method: 首先在三个基因组任务族上复现已发布模型，并冻结一份独立验证的数值参考。随后实现客户端辅助的近似同态加密协议：提供商在密文上执行线性代数，持密钥的数据所有者在固定边界上执行精确归一化、因果 softmax 和激活函数。

Result: 非交互式配置可完成一个已发布权重块，但按组合需求配置时超出所测加速器内存上限。客户端辅助配置在完整提示长度下，对一个留出基因组信号输入执行了全部已发布 transformer 块与任务头，匹配冻结的最终标签，加速器内存峰值 9,839 MiB，单加速器耗时 6,683 秒。

Conclusion: 结果确立了完整分类器的算术可行性，但在可重复性、网络传输与私有 token 索引查找方面仍未解决。其生物医学意义在于：在所述威胁模型下，已服务的基因组模型可处理编码序列而不向计算提供商暴露明文查询衍生激活值。

Abstract: Human genomic sequences can identify individuals, cannot be replaced after disclosure, and are the inputs that genomic foundation models are designed to interpret. We assess whether a compute provider can execute a released genomic foundation model without receiving query-derived genomic values in plaintext and whether correctness, memory, or cost prevents complete encrypted inference. We first reproduce the released model on three genomic task families and freeze an independently validated numerical reference. We then implement a client-assisted approximate homomorphic encryption protocol: the provider evaluates linear algebra on ciphertexts, while the key-holding data owner evaluates exact normalization, causal softmax, and activation functions at fixed boundaries. A noninteractive configuration completes one released-weight block but exceeds the tested accelerator-memory envelope when configured for composition. The client-assisted configuration executes all released transformer blocks and the task head for one heldout genomic-signal input at its full prompt length. It matches the frozen final label, peaks at 9,839 mebibytes of accelerator memory, and completes in 6,683 seconds on one accelerator. These results establish arithmetic feasibility for a complete classifier, while repeatability, network transport, and private token-index lookup remain unresolved. The biomedical significance is that, under the stated threat model, a served genomic model can process an encoded sequence without exposing plaintext queryderived activations to the compute provider.

</details>


### [278] [Analyzing Multi-Factor Authentication Through Cryptographic Security Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16214)
*Ryan Tipping,Yousef Tahboub,Krishna Bodige*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了现代认证系统从单一机制（用户名/密码、物理令牌、生物特征）向多因素认证（MFA）平台演进的趋势，并指出其中多类策略聚焦于防止重放攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为防范未授权访问的首要防线，身份验证至关重要。随着攻击手段（尤其是凭证重用与重放攻击）日益复杂，单因素认证已难以满足安全需求，因此需要研究并整合多种认证机制。

Method: 该文以综述/分析为主，梳理认证技术的发展脉络：从单一认证机制出发，扩展到组合多种机制的多因素认证平台，并重点归类与分析针对重放攻击（即已被潜在泄露组件的重复使用）的防护策略。

Result: 指出当前认证系统已普遍演化为多因素认证平台，并且其中多种机制的核心目标在于抵御重放攻击。

Conclusion: 多因素认证已成为现代身份验证的主流方向，而防重放攻击是其中关键技术目标之一，值得进一步研究其策略整合与安全性。

Abstract: Modern authentication systems use cybersecurity techniques to validate the identity of the person (or applications acting on behalf of the person) as a primary defense against unauthorized access. While initially built around single mechanisms such as usernames/passwords, physical tokens, or biometrics, current systems have evolved into multi-factor authentication (MFA) platforms that combine multiple mechanisms. Among them, several focus on strategies that prevent replay attacks (i.e. the reuse of a component that could have been potentially compromised).

</details>


### [279] [RuleAutoPilot: Synthesizing Deployable Suricata Rules from Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16231)
*Mughees Ur Rehman,Aritran Piplai,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RuleAutoPilot 是一个端到端智能体框架，可直接从恶意软件网络流量生成可部署的 Suricata 规则，无需先验威胁情报，通过良性流量指纹过滤与执行验证修复机制显著提升规则质量并降低 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的 IDS（如 Suricata）对网络安全至关重要，但编写有效检测规则需要深厚专业知识，且难以跟上新兴威胁。现有 LLM 方法虽能减轻分析负担，但要么依赖仅在流量产物已存在后才产生的精选威胁情报，要么成本高昂且缺乏足够质量控制。

Method: RuleAutoPilot 直接从恶意软件网络流量生成可部署的 Suricata 规则。针对流量中少量安全相关流量混于大量背景流量导致的噪声问题，采用良性流量指纹（Benign Traffic Fingerprinting）阶段，在 LLM 处理前剔除已知良性背景流。对语法检查失败、未在源流量上触发、或在良性语料上产生误报的规则，使用结构化反馈自动修复。

Result: 在 1,296 个恶意软件 PCAP 上，基于执行的验证将规则质量（F1）从 0.443 提升至 0.539。在分层 200-PCAP 子集上，开源权重 gpt-oss-120b 上的 RuleAutoPilot 达到接近前沿的质量，F1 为 0.524，对比 Claude Code 下 Claude Opus 5 的 0.623，计费 token 成本低 52 倍。仅替换主干为 Claude Opus 5，RuleAutoPilot 直接超越 Claude Code，F1 为 0.656 对 0.623，token 少 40 倍。

Conclusion: 更强的骨干模型可提升 RuleAutoPilot 自身上限，但在相同骨干下，本文脚手架仍优于 Claude Code 的脚手架，表明该脚手架对性能的贡献独立于骨干模型。

Abstract: Rule-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) such as Suricata are central to network security, yet crafting effective detection rules demands deep expert knowledge and cannot keep pace with emerging threats. Existing LLM-based approaches can reduce analyst effort, but they either rely on curated threat intelligence that is produced only after the underlying traffic artifacts already exist, or they require costly LLM use without sufficient quality control. We present RuleAutoPilot, an end-to-end agentic framework that generates deployable Suricata rules directly from malware network traffic, with no prior threat intelligence required. A key challenge is noise: network traffic captures often contain a small amount of security-relevant traffic mixed with large volumes of background traffic, which reduces LLM reasoning quality and increases cost. RuleAutoPilot addresses this challenge with a Benign Traffic Fingerprinting stage that removes known benign background flows before LLM processing. Rules that fail syntax checks, do not trigger on the source traffic, or generate false positives on a benign corpus are automatically repaired using structured feedback. Across 1,296 malware PCAPs, execution-grounded verification raises rule quality (F1) from 0.443 to 0.539. On a stratified 200-PCAP subset, RuleAutoPilot on the open-weight gpt-oss-120b reaches near-frontier quality, 0.524 F1 against Claude Opus 5 under Claude Code's 0.623, at 52x lower billed-token cost. Swapping only the backbone to Claude Opus 5, RuleAutoPilot surpasses Claude Code outright, 0.656 F1 against 0.623, at 40x fewer tokens. A stronger backbone raises RuleAutoPilot's own ceiling, but at the same backbone, our scaffold still outperforms Claude Code's, showing the scaffold contributes independently of the backbone.

</details>


### [280] [Exploiting and Securing Docker containers and Kubernetes pods from a MitM attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16253)
*Henry Kabuye,Ismail Khalid Kazmi,Chunyan Mu,Paolo Modesti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述与设计实践研究，提出一种结合形式化通信模型、AnBxJ安全库与OSI第7层容器防火墙的框架，用于在零信任架构下保护Docker与Kubernetes等容器化操作系统免受中间人攻击，并成功实现了相应安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器中的工作负载（如Docker容器和Kubernetes Pod）面临与非容器环境相似的多种攻击，包括钓鱼、应用漏洞利用和网络入侵；作者希望探索有效保护容器化操作系统免受中间人攻击的技术方案。

Method: 采用基于PRISMA指南的系统综述（SR）汇总同一研究主题的证据；提出使用通信与密码原语的概念模型，并结合AnBxJ Java安全库与运行于OSI模型第7层的容器防火墙，构建面向容器化操作系统的安全框架与零信任架构。

Result: 识别出关键成功因素，并在容器化操作系统场景中成功实现了一种安全机制。

Conclusion: 研究结果可帮助实践者通过系统化容器安全实践并采用零信任架构，保护Kubernetes与Docker部署环境。

Abstract: PURPOSE - Workloads in containers, such as Docker containers and Kubernetes pods, are vulnerable to many of the same attacks as workloads in non-container environments, including phishing, application exploits and network intrusions. This systematic review and design-and-creation study explores techniques for securing containerised-based operating systems against Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) attacks. The proposed framework uses a conceptual model for representing communication and cryptographic primitives, together with the AnBxJ Java security library and container firewalls operating at layer 7 of the OSI model. The study addresses the question: How can containerised-based operating systems be effectively secured from Man-in-the-Middle attacks? It aims to support practitioners in protecting Docker and Kubernetes deployments by systematising security practices and applying a zero trust architecture.
  METHODOLOGY - The research uses a Systematic Review (SR) based on the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA), bringing together evidence from studies addressing the same research topic.
  FINDINGS - Success factors were identified, and a security mechanism was successfully implemented in a containerised-based operating system scenario.
  VALUE - The findings may help practitioners protect Kubernetes and Docker installations by systematising container security practices and providing a zero trust architecture for containerised-based operating systems.

</details>


### [281] [Understanding the Usability of Cryptographic Verification Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16323)
*Tarikul Islam,Yasin Islam,Khandakar Ashrafi Akbar,Imtiaz Karim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对Tamarin、ProVerif等密码协议验证工具的经验用户开展探索性以人为中心的研究，揭示了验证工作流中的可用性障碍，并提出改进工具可访问性、可解释性与可用性的设计优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码协议验证工具被广泛用于分析复杂协议的安全性，但用户如何与这些工具交互却相对缺乏研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，从以人为中心的视角理解经验用户在使用协议验证器时面临的真实困难。

Method: 采用探索性、以人为中心的研究方法，对Tamarin、ProVerif及相关协议验证器的经验用户（包括研究人员、研究生和实践者）进行问卷调查，收集其在实际使用中的体验与反馈。

Result: 研究发现验证工作流中存在多方面的可用性障碍，包括难以调试非终止和性能问题，以及缺乏系统方法来验证形式化模型与真实协议的一致性。当证明失败且没有具体攻击时，用户通常简化模型、添加辅助引理并重新审视建模抽象。参与者还呼吁提供可操作的诊断信息、更清晰的结果解释、可视化以及对重复性证明任务的自动化支持。

Conclusion: 持续存在的可用性挑战源于协议级推理与验证器的形式化模型、证明流程及诊断输出之间的鸿沟。本文据此提出了具体的设计优先级，以改进密码协议验证工具的可访问性、可解释性和可用性。

Abstract: Cryptographic protocol verification tools are widely used to analyze the security of complex protocols, yet how users interact with these tools remains comparatively understudied. We present an exploratory human-centered study of experienced users of Tamarin, ProVerif, and related protocol verifiers. Our survey included researchers, graduate students, and practitioners with hands-on experience using Tamarin, ProVerif, or related tools. The findings reveal usability barriers across the verification workflow, including difficulties debugging non-termination and performance issues, and the lack of systematic methods for validating formal models against real protocols. When proofs fail without concrete attacks, users commonly simplify models, add helper lemmas, and revisit modeling abstractions. Participants also called for actionable diagnostics, clearer explanations of results, visualization, and automation for recurring proof tasks. Our findings suggest that persistent usability challenges arise from the gap between protocol-level reasoning and the verifier's formal model, proof procedures, and diagnostic output. We derive concrete design priorities for improving the accessibility, interpretability, and usability of cryptographic protocol verification tools.

</details>


### [282] [Illusion of Depth: Revealing Hidden Stereo Vision Vulnerabilities in Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16336)
*Sri Hrushikesh Varma Bhupathiraju,Tetsu Ishizue,Nicholas U. Costagliola,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka,Takeshi Sugawara,Sara Rampazzi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了立体相机因像素采样与标定过程而存在的内在脆弱性，攻击者可用简单重复图案精细操控深度估计，无需复杂对抗技术，并验证了其对多种算法、模型及商用相机的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体相机广泛用于自动驾驶、无人机和机器人，以低成本提供精确深度估计，但作者希望探究其像素采样和标定环节中可能被攻击者利用的内在脆弱性，从而揭示自主系统部署中的关键安全隐患。

Method: 作者分析了立体相机的像素采样与标定过程，利用简单重复图案实现对真实障碍物深度估计的精细控制，并评估了该攻击对两种立体匹配算法（BM、SGBM）、三种深度学习模型（PSMNet、MoCha-Stereo、UniMatch）、一个立体-LiDAR融合模型（SGM-DDC）以及ZED2和Intel RealSense D435两款商用相机的影响。

Result: 在ZED2相机上，攻击者可使障碍物深度被推远至20米或拉近至12米；在真实驾驶场景中，0.5秒的短暂攻击即可触发主流自动驾驶框架的紧急制动，并在CARLA中验证了最高40 km/h车速下的可行性；现有先进防御措施被证实无效。

Conclusion: 作者确认现有防御无效，并提出一种利用相似度分数动态检测并抑制深度差异的新策略，强调了立体匹配与深度学习深度估计模型中的隐藏脆弱性，指出了自主系统部署中的关键局限。

Abstract: Stereo cameras are integrated into autonomous systems such as self-driving cars, drones, and robots to offer precise depth estimation in a cost-effective manner compared to LiDAR technology. In this work, we reveal an intrinsic vulnerability in stereo cameras that stems from their pixel sampling and calibration processes, which can influence the outputs of stereo matching algorithms. Attackers can achieve fine-grained control over the estimated depth of real obstacles using simple repeating patterns, without relying on sophisticated adversarial machine learning techniques. Furthermore, deep learning-based depth estimation models exhibit a similar vulnerability. We evaluate the impact of this attack on two widely used stereo matching algorithms (BM and SGBM), three deep learning models (PSMNet, MoCha-Stereo, and UniMatch), a stereo-LiDAR fusion model (SGM-DDC), and two popular commercial stereo cameras, the ZED2 and Intel RealSense D435. For example, in the ZED2 camera, an attacker can displace obstacles up to 20~meters farther or 12~meters closer. In our real-world evaluation in a driving setting, a brief 0.5~second attack can trigger emergency braking in a popular autonomous driving framework. We further demonstrate the feasibility at driving speeds up to 40~km/h using CARLA. Finally, we confirm the ineffectiveness of state-of-the-art defenses, and we propose a novel strategy that leverages similarity scores to dynamically detect and suppress the depth discrepancies. Our work highlights vulnerabilities hidden in stereo matching and deep learning depth estimation models, addressing critical limitations in autonomous system deployments.

</details>


### [283] [gr-PHYSEC: Real-time Channel-based Key Generation for Physical Layer Secure Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16375)
*Jose Angel Sanchez Viloria,George Sklivanitis,Dimitris Pados*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 gr-PHYSEC——一个基于 GNU Radio 的实时物理层密钥生成模块，利用无线信道随机性生成对称密钥，并在真实机器人平台上验证了其低密钥不一致率与强随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态、去中心化的无线通信场景中，防窃听至关重要。传统密钥生成依赖预共享密钥或计算复杂度，难以适应此类环境，因此需要利用信道固有随机性来实现物理层密钥生成。

Method: 将训练好的神经网络嵌入 GNU Radio，在可信双方（Alice 与 Bob）探测交换过程中提取信道特征；特征经量化生成二进制密钥，通过 Reed-Solomon 编码进行一致性协商，并用 SHA-512 哈希进一步保护；生成的密钥直接用于数据加密。

Result: 在 FAU CAAI 互联机器人测试床上，使用 ADALM Pluto 软件定义无线电与 NVIDIA Jetson Orin 搭配地面机器人平台进行真实实验，结果表明密钥不一致率低、随机性强，并通过 NIST 随机数测试套件验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了 GNU Radio 支持实时 AI 驱动安全方案的能力，推动了软件定义安全通信的边界，源代码已开源。

Abstract: Securing wireless communication against eavesdropping is critical, particularly in dynamic and decentralized environments. We present gr-PHYSEC, a new GNU Radio out-of-tree (OOT) module for real-time physical-layer key generation. Unlike traditional key generation that relies on pre-shared secrets or computational complexity, our approach derives symmetric keys from the wireless channel's inherent randomness. We embed a trained neural network within GNU Radio to extract channel features between trusted parties (Alice and Bob) during probe exchanges. These features are quantized into binary keys, reconciled via Reed-Solomon encoding, and further secured with SHA-512 hashing. The generated keys are then directly used to encrypt data. Real-world experiments at the FAU CAAI connected robotics testbed using ADALM Pluto software-defined radios and NVIDIA Jetson Orin validate the approach with ground robotic platforms. Results demonstrate low key disagreement rates and strong randomness, as verified by the NIST test suite for random and pseudorandom number generators for cryptographic applications. This integration showcases how GNU Radio can support real-time AI-driven security solutions, pushing the boundaries of software-defined secure communication. The source code for this project is available at: https://github.com/C2A2-at-Florida-Atlantic-University/gr-PHYSEC

</details>


### [284] [Implementing a White-Box Undetectable Backdoor for Random Fourier Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16403)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文用仅依赖 numpy 和 scipy 的纯数值实现，端到端复现了 Goldwasser 等人提出的 CLWE-RFF 白盒后门构造，检验其在普通科学计算工具下的可实现性，并报告统计不可区分性测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文仅以密码学归约和概率引理形式给出构造，缺少参考实现，并依赖 Sparse Gaussian Pancakes 分布与齐次 CLWE 条件密度等辅助工具。其能否用普通数值代码实现并不显然，因此需要实测该威胁是否仅靠常规科学计算工具即可实现，还是必须依赖专门的密码学基础设施。

Method: 作者仅用 numpy 与 scipy 完整实现白盒 CLWE-RFF 后门构造，并给出两种核心 GP_d(b_k) 分布的采样器：一是拒绝采样的近似代理方法，二是由齐次 CLWE 密度推导并经其解析形式验证的精确闭式采样器。随后开展权重空间与函数黑箱两方面的统计不可区分性测试。

Result: 在覆盖多种稀疏比 ρ = d_sparse/D 的测试范围内，未发现后门模型与干净模型之间存在可检测差异的证据。作者还报告了构造中哪些部分易于实现、哪些需要论文未给出的推导，并说明未尝试复现的部分，包括底层的格困难性归约。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在增进对 Goldwasser 白盒 CLWE 核心实际可实现性的理解，而非提出新的理论结果。

Abstract: Goldwasser et al. showed that undetectable backdoors can be planted in machine learning models trained with the Random Fourier Features (RFF) algorithm, under a hardness assumption tied to the Continuous Learning With Errors (CLWE) problem. Under standard cryptographic assumptions, even a full white-box audit of a model's weights cannot detect this class of backdoor. The construction is stated in terms of cryptographic reductions and probabilistic lemmas, without a reference implementation, and relies on secondary machinery such as the Sparse Gaussian Pancakes distribution and a homogeneous CLWE conditional density. Its realizability in ordinary numerical code is not obvious from the paper alone.
  This paper implements the white-box CLWE-RFF backdoor construction end to end using only numpy and scipy, to test whether this threat is realizable with commodity scientific-computing tools or requires specialized cryptographic infrastructure. We give two samplers for the core $GP_d(b_k)$ distribution. The first is a rejection-sampling proxy. The second is an exact closed-form sampler derived from the homogeneous CLWE density and verified against its own analytic form.
  Using this implementation, we run statistical indistinguishability tests, covering both weight-space and functional black-box comparisons. We find no evidence of detectable difference between backdoored and clean models across a range of sparsity ratios $ρ= d_{\text{sparse}}/D$. We report which parts of the construction were straightforward to realize, which required derivation not spelled out in the paper. We also highlight which parts we did not attempt to reproduce, including the underlying lattice hardness reduction. We see this work as a contribution to understanding the practical realizability of the Goldwasser white-box CLWE core, not as a new theoretical result.

</details>


### [285] [No Bit Left Behind: Using Brute-Force Lifting to Achieve Fully Static Binary Recompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16423)
*Tianjiao Huang,Po-An Chen,Nick Baron,Michael Franz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全静态、整程序二进制提升系统,将每个字节偏移视为潜在分支目标,暴力提升整个二进制并构造保守的超集控制流图,将静态不可解的间接分支降为分派表查找,实现从x86-64到LLVM IR的重编译并静态交叉编译到AArch64,无需目标机运行时支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制重编译可实现两大任务:为遗留二进制改造安全缓解措施,以及跨指令集架构迁移程序。然而目前没有全自动系统能可靠地将任意二进制提升到LLVM IR等编译器中间表示,也无法完全静态、可靠地完成非平凡二进制的跨ISA翻译。根本问题在于静态恢复程序控制流图(CFG)在一般情况下是不可能的:计算分支可能跳转到必须先运行程序才能确定的目标。现有系统因此依赖运行时回退机制,导致大量翻译机制必须随翻译后的程序一同部署到目标机器上。

Method: 不做代码与数据的区分,而是把每个字节偏移都视为潜在分支目标,以暴力方式提升整个二进制,构造一个保守地包含所有可行控制流的超集CFG。对于静态不可解的间接分支,将其归约为一张分派表查找,该表指向相应的已翻译控制流路径。原型实现为从x86-64二进制到LLVM IR的二进制重编译器,不需要任何代码/数据启发式规则。

Result: 实现了x86-64到LLVM IR的原型二进制重编译器,无需代码/数据启发式。通过复用未修改的现有LLVM后端,完成到AArch64的全静态交叉编译,验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过构造超集CFG并将不可解间接分支降为分派表查找,可以在不依赖目标机运行时翻译支持的情况下实现全静态整程序二进制提升与跨ISA静态翻译,并借助现有LLVM后端完成x86-64到AArch64的验证。

Abstract: Binary recompilation is a technique for operating directly on executable code.
