It promises to automate two important tasks: retrofitting security mitigations onto legacy binaries, and migrating binaries across instruction set architectures (ISAs). Yet today, there is no fully automated system that can reliably lift arbitrary binary executables to a compiler intermediate representation (IR) such as LLVM IR, or that can fully statically and reliably translate non-trivial binary executables from one ISA to another. The main underlying problem is that recovering a program's control flow graph (CFG) statically is impossible in general: computed branches can jump to targets that cannot be determined without actually running the program. Existing systems resort to runtime fallback mechanisms, requiring a significant portion of the binary translation machinery to accompany the translated program on the target machine.
  This article presents a fully static, whole-program binary lifting system requiring no runtime translation support on the target. Rather than attempting to distinguish code from data, we treat every byte offset as a potential branch target and lift the entire binary in a brute-force manner, constructing a superset CFG that conservatively contains all feasible control flows. Statically unresolvable computed branches are thereby reduced to lookups in a dispatch table that points to the corresponding translated control flow path. We have implemented this approach as a prototype binary recompiler from x86-64 binaries to LLVM IR, requiring no code/data heuristics. We validate it with a fully static cross-compilation to AArch64, achieved by reusing existing LLVM backends with no modification.

</details>


### [286] [Evaluating the NIST Bugs Framework Against CWE as a Successor for Automated Vulnerability Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16433)
*Md Nazmul Hoque,Shaswata Mitra,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估NIST提出的Bugs Framework（BF）作为漏洞分类目标及CWE补充框架的可行性，通过专家一致性研究与LLM自动化实验，认为BF比CWE更结构化、更利于自动化分类，但存在属性轴指导不足等缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWE条目存在重叠、非正交结构，导致同一漏洞映射到多个弱点，增加了根因分析与分诊的难度。NIST SP 800-231提出BF，以<原因,操作,后果>三元组及因果链组织漏洞，但BF此前仅被规范定义，缺乏针对自动化分类挑战的实证评估与采用证据。

Method: 基于系统筛选的、与CWE研究相关联的自动化CVE语料库，将BF作为分类目标进行评估。开展两项评估：一是定性评估，由2名匿名领域专家独立将13个CVE映射到BF的四个轴；二是在两种不同预算配置的大语言模型部署上测试其自动化框架的可复现性。

Result: 专家在cause（原因）和operation（操作）轴上表现出高度一致性，attribute（属性）轴一致性一般。LLM部署测试在可复现性方面存在局限，包括证据可获得性不足、闭源软件缺乏可检索的修复提交等。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持BF比CWE更结构化、更利于自动化这一主张，同时揭示了BF的具体不足，尤其是属性轴指导规范不够明确。

Abstract: Vulnerability classification based on root cause weaknesses is essential for numerous cybersecurity activities, where the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) serves as a public repository of such flaws. However, its overlapping entries create a non-orthogonal structure. The result is the same vulnerability being mapped to multiple weaknesses, complicating Root Cause Analysis (RCA) and triage. To address this, NIST Special Publication 800-231 introduces the Bugs Framework (BF), which organizes vulnerabilities into <cause, operation, consequence> triples and links such triples into a causal chain, so that a vulnerability carries its root cause and its sink together instead of a single terminal label. To date, however, BF has been specified but not evaluated regarding its performance against the challenges to automated classification. The evidence required for adoption has not been investigated empirically. We evaluate BF as a classification target and a complement to CWE using a systematically screened corpus of automated Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVEs) linked to CWE research. We assess the reproducibility of CVE-to-BF classification through two evaluations. The first is qualitative: an anonymized inter-rater study in which 2 subject-matter experts (SMEs) independently mapped 13 CVEs onto the four BF axes. Annotators showed strong agreement on the cause and operation axes, while the attribute axis indicated fair agreement. We also tested our automated framework across two large language model (LLM) deployments under different budgets for reproducibility analysis. Despite limitations, such as evidence availability and the absence of retrievable fix commits for closed-source software, our findings support the claim that BF is a more structured and automation-friendly framework than CWE. Our exploration reveals specific gaps in BF, including under-specified guidance on attributes.

</details>


### [287] [Not All Relations Are Equal: Relation-Balanced and Calibrated Graph Learning for Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16462)
*Lijie Zheng,Ji He,Alessandro Brighente,Yulong Shen,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RECAL：一种基于关系均衡掩码图学习的无监督溯源入侵检测框架，通过对齐每条关系的良性误差分布校准重构误差，显著提升APT检测性能并大幅降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溯源入侵检测系统（PIDSs）大多对所有关系一视同仁，忽略了关系的统计异质性；例如在CADETS中不同关系频率差异约140,000倍。这会导致系统偏重高频关系，忽视各关系正常误差水平的差异，从而增加误报和漏报风险。

Method: 提出无监督框架RECAL，采用关系均衡的掩码图学习以更好捕捉稀有交互模式；并针对每条关系自身的良性误差分布对重构误差进行校准，生成可比的异常证据，以区分攻击与良性行为。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上，RECAL的F1分数分别为99.99%、99.93%和99.99%，较各数据集最佳基线分别高出0.88、0.82和0.42个百分点；与报告最低FPR的基线相比，平均FPR分别降低约105倍、4倍和41倍。

Conclusion: RECAL通过关系均衡学习与误差校准，有效缓解关系异质性带来的漏报与误报问题，在APT检测任务上取得领先性能并显著降低误报。

Abstract: Provenance-Based Intrusion Detection Systems (PIDSs) detect Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) by analyzing system interactions. However, existing methods largely treat relations uniformly, overlooking statistical heterogeneity; in CADETS, relation frequencies differ by approximately $140{,}000\times$. This may cause PIDSs to focus more on frequent relations and overlook differences in normal error levels across relations, increasing the risk of false alarms and missed detections. We present RECAL, an unsupervised framework using relation-balanced masked graph learning to better capture rare interaction patterns. It further calibrates reconstruction errors against each relation's benign error distribution to produce comparable anomaly evidence, helping distinguish attacks from benign behavior and reduce false alarms. On three DARPA E3 datasets, RECAL achieves F1 scores of 99.99\%, 99.93\%, and 99.99\%, outperforming the best baseline on each dataset by 0.88, 0.82, and 0.42 percentage points, respectively. Compared with the baseline reporting the lowest FPR, RECAL reduces mean FPR by approximately $105\times$, $4\times$, and $41\times$.

</details>


### [288] [A Cyber Range Evaluation of Autonomous Network Incident Response Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16541)
*Jakob Nyberg,Teodor Sommestad,Andrei Buhaiu,Joakim Loxdal,Pontus Johnson,Mathias Ekstedt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在用于操作员培训的网络靶场中测试自动化网络入侵响应智能体，比较启发式方法与强化学习方法，发现强化学习智能体整体防御效率更高，且性能高度依赖对手策略与模拟用户行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化网络入侵响应需要兼顾防御有效性与可用性代价。作者希望借助面向人类操作员培训的网络靶场环境，评估不同防御智能体的实际表现，并探究强化学习策略相较启发式策略的优劣。

Method: 构建包含可变网络拓扑、红队模拟和模拟用户智能体的仿真网络环境；使用SIEM平台生成告警并映射到智能体所用的数据建模语言；防御目标是阻止红队访问主机并最小化防御措施带来的可用性代价；测试启发式智能体与通过强化学习训练的策略组合，学习策略借助模拟网络攻击的仿真器进行优化。

Result: 强化学习智能体整体上比启发式策略更高效地防御系统；其性能高度依赖于对手策略以及模拟用户的行为组合。

Conclusion: 在网络靶场中，强化学习方法相较启发式方法展现出更优的自动化入侵响应防御效率，但防御效果并非绝对，需结合对手策略与用户行为综合评估。

Abstract: We test the performance of agents for automated network intrusion response in a cyber range intended for human operator training. The range implements an emulated networking environment with a variable network topology, red-team emulation and simulated user agents. The goal of the defensive agents is to prevent hosts in the network from being accessed by the red-team agent, while minimizing the availability costs induced from defensive measures. Alerts are generated using a SIEM platform and mapped to a data modeling language used by the agents. We test a combination of heuristic agents and policies learned using reinforcement learning. The learned policies are optimized to minimize the combined cost using a cyber attack simulator modeling the network. We found that the reinforcement learning agents were overall more efficient at defending the system than the heuristic policy, and that the performance depends highly on the policy of the adversary in combination with the simulated users.

</details>


### [289] [GPUThor: Amplifying Rowhammer Attacks via Non-Uniform Patterns to Exploit ECC-Protected GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16546)
*Chris S. Lin,Joyce Qu,Aditya Rajeev,Gururaj Saileshwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GPUThor 是一种针对 NVIDIA GPU 的高强度 Rowhammer 攻击，利用非均匀敲击模式将位翻转数量提升 500 至 23,500 倍，并首次在 ECC 保护 GPU 上实现可用攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 中的 GDDR 内存易受 Rowhammer 攻击，但已有 GPU 攻击仅能触发数十到数百次位翻转，远少于 CPU 攻击，实际影响受限。其根源在于现有攻击依赖均匀敲击模式，使攻击行与诱饵行被平等激活，导致攻击行敲击强度低。

Method: GPUThor 采用非均匀敲击：逆向 GPU 内存访问合并行为，构造让攻击行比诱饵行更强烈激活的敲击模式；并通过识别 DRAM 内缓解措施生效的刷新时刻，构造跨越刷新间隔的更长攻击模式以规避缓解并进一步提升敲击强度。

Result: 在多种 NVIDIA GPU（A4000、A4500、A5000、A6000）上，位翻转数比既有 GPU Rowhammer 攻击多 500 至 23,500 倍，位翻转率接近最先进的 CPU Rowhammer 攻击；并在 ECC 保护 GPU 上首次实现攻击，可诱发不可纠正的双位与三位翻转，使拒绝服务与权限提升攻击在启用 ECC 的 GPU 上变得可行。

Conclusion: 通过非均匀敲击和刷新间隔规避，GPUThor 显著缩小了 GPU 与 CPU Rowhammer 攻击之间的差距，并将 Rowhammer 的现实威胁扩展至 ECC 保护的 GPU。

Abstract: GDDR memory in GPUs is vulnerable to Rowhammer attacks, where rapid memory accesses induce bit flips in adjacent cells, enabling data tampering and privilege escalation. However, prior GPU Rowhammer attacks trigger only tens to hundreds of bit flips, orders of magnitude fewer than CPU attacks, severely limiting their practical impact. This gap stems from the reliance of existing GPU Rowhammer attacks on uniform hammering patterns that activate aggressor and decoy rows equally, which results in low hammering intensity for aggressor rows.
  We present GPUThor, a high-intensity Rowhammer attack on NVIDIA GPUs leveraging non-uniform hammering. GPUThor reverse engineers GPU memory-access coalescing behavior to enable non-uniform hammering patterns on GPUs, that activate aggressor rows more intensely than decoy rows. Additionally, by identifying refresh instances when in-DRAM mitigations are applied, it constructs longer attack patterns that escape mitigation across refresh intervals, further increasing hammering intensity. Together, these techniques yield 500X to 23,500X more bit flips than prior GPU Rowhammer attacks, across several NVIDIA GPUs (A4000, A4500, A5000, A6000), reaching bit flip rates close to state-of-the-art CPU Rowhammer attacks. GPUThor also enables the first Rowhammer exploits on ECC-protected GPUs, inducing uncorrectable double and triple bit flips, making denial-of-service and privilege-escalation attacks practical even on GPUs with ECC enabled.

</details>


### [290] [The MAL Simulator: Cyber Operations Simulation based on Attack & Defense Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16563)
*Jakob Nyberg,Sandor Berglund,Andrei Buhaiu,Joakim Loxdal,Pontus Johnson,Mathias Ekstedt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 Meta Attack Language (MAL) 的网络攻防仿真器 MAL Simulator，支持进攻与防御智能体的自动化训练，并在模拟网络环境中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持决策驱动的网络攻防仿真、系统分析及自动化智能体开发，需要一个可跨领域适配、无需修改源码的通用仿真平台。

Method: 以攻击建模语言 MAL 构建仿真器，使其可适配不同目标领域；基于 CRATE 网络靶场中采集的数据建模；开展两个案例研究，分别训练防御智能体和进攻智能体。

Result: 训练后的攻击者策略比对比搜索方法更高效地到达目标；在噪声告警条件下，训练后的防御智能体成本低于朴素启发式智能体；将 RL 攻击者与 RL 防御者对抗测试时，防御者性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究表明网络攻击仿真器对于同时训练攻防双方智能体至关重要；MAL Simulator 及工具已公开，并提供与现有机器学习框架兼容的通用接口。

Abstract: We have developed the MAL Simulator, a cyber operation simulator based on the Meta Attack Language (MAL). The MAL Simulator is intended for decision-driven cyber attack and defense simulations, for system analysis and the development of automated agents. By building the simulator around an attack modeling language, it can be adapted to different target domains without modifying the source code. We used the simulator for two case studies where we trained two types of agents for automated cyber operations: a defensive agent and an offensive agent. To ground the experiments, we base the models in data collected from an emulated network implemented in the cyber range CRATE. We found that the trained attacker policy could reach the designated targets more efficiently than the compared search methods, and that the trained defender agent induced lower costs than a naive heuristic agent under noisy alert conditions. When testing the RL attacker against the RL defender, we found that the performance of the defenders dropped significantly. This emphasizes the importance of cyber attack simulators to facilitate training both offensive and defensive agents. The MAL Simulator and associated tooling is publicly available and provides common interfaces for compatibility with existing machine learning frameworks.

</details>


### [291] [From Hypervisor to Container: Cloud Security Vulnerabilities, Defense Mechanisms, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16675)
*Swapnil Vishwas Baviskar,Sanoj R,Hiran V Nath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了2008至2025年间120余篇云计算隔离安全文献，分析虚拟机与容器隔离边界的突破威胁及防御，并提出ADPO量化评分框架与CIA严重度评级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算中多用户共享同一物理硬件，虚拟机和容器虽用于隔离用户数据，但隔离边界存在被突破的严重安全风险，亟需系统梳理相关威胁与防御。

Method: 综述2008至2025年120余篇安全文献，围绕三个核心研究问题评估威胁与防御；提出ADPO量化评分框架（依据准确性、部署难度、性能影响、运维开销，评分0-3），并将攻击影响映射到机密性、完整性、可用性的1-5级严重度量表。

Result: 系统梳理了虚拟机逃逸、虚拟机跳转、CPU缓存侧信道、容器逃逸、脆弱容器镜像、DDoS等威胁及其防御，并通过ADPO与CIA量表实现不同防御系统的横向比较。

Conclusion: 揭示了安全与系统性能之间的权衡关系，并指出低开销入侵检测、构建真实测试数据集等开放挑战。

Abstract: In cloud computing, different users share the same physical hardware, which creates serious security risks. To protect data, cloud systems rely on virtual machines and containers to keep users isolated. This paper reviews over 120 security publications from 2008 to 2025, focusing on how these isolation boundaries can be breached. We examine threats like virtual machine escape, virtual machine hopping, CPU cache side-channels, container breakouts, vulnerable container images, and distributed denial of service (DDoS) attacks. We evaluate these security threats and their defenses using three key research questions. To compare different defense systems, we introduce a quantitative scoring framework called ADPO, which rates defenses from 0 to 3 based on their Accuracy, Deployment ease, Performance impact, and Operational overhead. We also map the impact of these attacks onto a 1-to-5 severity scale for Confidentiality, Integrity, and Availability. Finally, we highlight the trade-offs between security and system performance, and we outline open challenges like building low-overhead intrusion detection and creating realistic test datasets.

</details>


### [292] [MarkSec: Capability-Aware Evaluation of Adversarial Attacks Against LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16681)
*Kairong Li,Zhikun Zhang,Xiao Ren,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MarkSec 框架，统一分析 LLM 水印面临的窃取、抹除与伪造攻击，并在统一评测协议下引入质量约束的攻击成功率指标，揭示攻击效果依赖文本质量约束与攻击通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 水印常用于溯源生成文本，但面临窃取、抹除和伪造三类攻击；这些攻击通常被孤立研究，彼此联系不清。同时评测缺少统一的检测器校准、指标定义与报告协议，且攻击成功率与文本质量分开衡量，难以识别既有效又保持质量的攻击。

Method: 提出 MarkSec 统一框架，将窃取、抹除、伪造三类攻击纳入同一分析体系；在统一报告协议下评测攻击，并提出"质量约束的攻击成功率"指标，联合评估攻击有效性与文本质量。实验覆盖代表性水印家族、攻击方法、LLM 与数据集。

Result: 三项发现：一、仅在"水印移除"上看似最强的攻击，当要求文本质量可接受时可能落后于通用改写；二、通用改写在各水印家族上都是强基线，其相对其他抹除器的优势因家族而异；三、在某一水印家族的案例研究中，要求文本质量时，基于窃取的抹除器常不如最佳通用抹除基线。

Conclusion: 表观上的"攻击赢家"取决于文本质量约束、攻击通用性与能力假设，因此水印安全评测需在统一协议与质量约束下进行。

Abstract: LLM watermarking helps trace the origin of generated text, but faces stealing attacks that recover watermark information, scrubbing attacks that remove watermark signals, and spoofing attacks that forge text accepted as watermarked. These attacks are often studied in isolation, leaving their connections unclear. Evaluations also often lack shared detector calibration, metric definitions, and reporting protocols. Moreover, measuring attack success and text quality separately makes it difficult to identify attacks that are both effective and quality-preserving.
  We propose MarkSec, a general framework that unifies analyses of stealing, scrubbing, and spoofing. We evaluate attacks under a common reporting protocol and introduce a quality-constrained attack success metric to assess effectiveness and text quality jointly. Experiments across representative watermark families, attacks, LLMs, and datasets reveal three findings. First, attacks that appear strongest by watermark removal alone can fall behind general rewriting when success also requires acceptable text quality. Second, general rewriting remains a strong baseline across watermark families, while its advantage over other scrubbers varies by family. Third, in a case study of one watermark family, stealing-based scrubbers often underperform the best general-scrubbing baselines when text quality is required. These results show that apparent attack winners depend on text-quality constraints, attack generality, and capability assumptions.

</details>


### [293] [Toward Secure AI-Powered Penetration Testing Agents: Security Threats, Guardrails, and Architectural Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16694)
*Rahul Dev T Y,Hiran V Nath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对LLM驱动的自主渗透测试智能体进行系统安全分析，梳理其架构、信任边界与攻击面，提出覆盖LLM生命周期、智能体架构与跨领域行为攻击的威胁分类体系，并指出面向该场景的专用防护机制研究空白与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主渗透测试智能体具备持久记忆、真实世界行动能力和长时程推理，其安全风险与传统对话式LLM系统存在本质差异，现有对话AI防护机制不足以保障此类自主攻击型智能体的安全，因此需要专门的安全分析。

Method: 系统分析代表性智能体架构，刻画其信任边界与攻击面，提出与生命周期对齐的威胁分类体系，涵盖LLM生命周期攻击、智能体架构攻击及跨领域行为攻击；并分析现有防护机制的局限。

Result: 建立了针对自主AI渗透测试智能体的威胁分类框架，识别出现有防护机制在语境感知与架构感知方面的不足，并明确了关键研究空白。

Conclusion: 需要发展专门化、语境感知、架构感知的新型防护机制，以保障下一代AI驱动的攻防安全系统。

Abstract: LLM-powered autonomous agents are transforming the penetration testing space with dynamic, multi-step offensive security workflows that require minimal supervision by humans. These agents leverage sophisticated reasoning abilities and external security tools to independently carry out reconnaissance, identify vulnerabilities, devise exploitation plans, and perform post-exploitation operations. But the ability to have persistent memory, to take actions in the real world, and to do long-horizon reasoning raises qualitatively different security concerns than traditional chat-based LLM systems. Existing guardrail mechanisms for conversational AI may not be sufficient to secure autonomous AI pentesting agents accordingly.
  To address these issues, we carry out a comprehensive security analysis on autonomous AI-penetration testing agents. We systematically analyse representative agent architectures, characterise their trust boundaries and attack surfaces and propose a threat taxonomy that is aligned with the lifecycle and covers LLM lifecycle attacks, agent-architecture attacks and cross-cutting behavioural attacks. We analyse the limitations of existing guardrail mechanisms, identify key research gaps, and discuss future research directions for developing specialised, context-aware, and architecture-aware guardrails to secure next-generation AI-driven offensive security systems.

</details>


### [294] [When Agents See Differently: Exposing UI Desynchronization Threats in Mobile Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16732)
*Heng Li,Fulin Zhao,Zhe Geng,Zhiyuan Yao,Wei Yuan,Xiapu Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了"人-代理UI失同步"威胁：攻击者可通过部署前嵌入的扰动使移动代理与人类用户从同一界面观察到不一致的信息，从而将代理引导至攻击者指定的操作，同时保持对人类用户的正常功能与行为一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动代理能自主与应用交互并代表用户执行重要操作，有效的人类监督依赖于"用户与代理从同一界面观察到一致信息"这一基本前提。然而用户通过物理屏幕和人类视觉系统感知界面，受遮挡与亮度对比度限制；而代理消费的数字截图可能保留这些内容，并能访问暴露非视觉组件元数据的无障碍表示。同一UI状态因此可能向两者呈现不同信息，这一前提可被系统性地违反，从而构成新的安全威胁。

Method: 作者研究被重新打包的合法APK克隆能否利用该失同步，在保持对人类用户完全功能性和行为一致性的同时，将代理引导至攻击者指定的操作。为系统性暴露和评估该威胁，作者开发了一个自动化框架，构建与用户运行时指令无关的UI失同步攻击，并将其实现为可部署的APK；扰动在部署前嵌入，无需访问运行时用户指令、代理检测或在线自适应。

Result: 在五个移动代理框架和三个骨干模型上，针对涉及多种应用的546个任务进行静态与动态评估，平均误导率分别达77.9%和66.9%。另有一项186名参与者参与的问卷研究显示，攻击中使用的视觉扰动难以被人类用户察觉。

Conclusion: 该威胁是可行的：部署前嵌入的扰动即可诱导代理偏离，且无需运行时用户指令、检测或在线自适应。研究揭示了人-代理界面感知差异带来的安全风险，并提供了系统的攻击构建与评估框架，同时表明此类攻击对人类用户高度隐蔽。

Abstract: Mobile agents are increasingly capable of autonomously interacting with mobile applications and performing consequential actions on behalf of users. Effective human oversight of such agents relies on a basic premise: users and agents observe consistent information from the same interface. We show that this premise can be systematically violated. Users perceive mobile interfaces through physical displays and the human visual system, making their observations subject to occlusion and luminance contrast limitations. In contrast, agents consume digital screenshots that may retain such content and accessibility representations that expose nonvisual widget metadata. The same UI state can therefore present materially different information to users and agents, a mismatch we term human-agent UI desynchronization. We investigate whether a repackaged clone of a legitimate APK can exploit this desynchronization to steer an agent toward attacker-designated actions, while remaining fully functional and behaviorally consistent with the original application for human users. We demonstrate that this threat is feasible: perturbations embedded before deployment can induce such deviations without access to runtime user instructions, agent detection or online adaptation. To systematically expose and evaluate this threat, we develop an automated framework that constructs user runtime instruction-agnostic UI desynchronization attacks and realizes them in deployable APKs. We conduct static and dynamic evaluations across five mobile-agent frameworks and three backbone models on 546 tasks involving various applications, achieving average misleading rates of 77.9% and 66.9%, respectively. A complementary questionnaire-based study with 186 participants finds that the visual perturbations used in our attacks are difficult for human users to notice.

</details>


### [295] [InceptionRAG: Stealthy Poisoning Attack Against Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16818)
*Jiachang Zhang,Min Chen,Xiao Ren,Zhenyong Zhang,Yuanchao Shu,Yunjun Gao,Zhikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 InceptionRAG，一种通过将恶意载荷碎片化并分散到多个良性文档中、利用 LLM 多跳推理能力诱导错误信息的隐蔽投毒攻击，并给出防御方法 HODOR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 RAG 的投毒攻击多为单点显式注入（恶意载荷完整体现在单个文档内），因此现有防御机制已能有效识别和缓解此类威胁。作者发现这些防御对"间接逻辑诱导"这类新型威胁无效，借此动机提出新的攻击范式。

Method: 提出 InceptionRAG：不注入显式恶意载荷，而是将其拆分为一串"休眠"文档片段，单独看无害可绕过现有防御，但被同时检索时可触发 LLM 通过多跳推理自我推导出目标错误信息。为提升黑盒场景适用性，提出零阶后缀优化（ZOSO）自动生成权威性后缀。防御端提出基于文档隔离的 HODOR，解耦对抗性逻辑依赖。

Result: 在 3 个数据集和 3 个 LLM 上评估，InceptionRAG 在严格对抗约束下攻击成功率超过 80%，且规避能力强，可绕过针对传统单文档注入的现有防御。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个悖论：LLM 推理能力越强，越容易受到基于推理的投毒攻击。作者据此提出 HODOR 防御以缓解潜在滥用。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge but have been demonstrated to be vulnerable to corpus poisoning. Existing poisoning attacks against RAG largely focus on single-point explicit injection, where the malicious payload is fully encapsulated within a single document. Consequently, recent mitigation mechanisms have evolved to identify and diminish these threats effectively. In this paper, we first verify that existing mitigation mechanisms are insufficient for a new class of threats: indirect logic induction. Motivated by this observation, we introduce InceptionRAG, a stealthy attack mechanism that subverts the standard attack paradigm. Instead of injecting explicit malicious payloads, InceptionRAG fragments it into a chain of dormant passages. These passages appear harmless and can bypass existing mitigation mechanisms when examined separately. However, when retrieved together, they trigger LLMs to self-deduce target misinformation via multi-hop reasoning. To further improve the applicability of InceptionRAG in black-box settings, we propose zeroth-order suffix optimization (ZOSO) to automate the generation of authoritative suffixes. Extensive evaluations across three datasets and three LLMs demonstrate that InceptionRAG achieves an attack success rate exceeding 80% even under rigorous adversarial constraints. In particular, InceptionRAG shows superior evasion capabilities, effectively bypassing established defenses that mitigate traditional single-document injections. Our findings expose a concerning paradox: the stronger reasoning capabilities of LLMs increase their vulnerability to reasoning-based poisoning attacks. To mitigate potential misuse, we propose a document isolation-based defense, HODOR, which decouples adversarial logical dependencies.

</details>


### [296] [ROSETTA: Efficient and Accurate Privacy-Preserving LLM Decoding via Hybrid CKKS/TFHE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16915)
*Jiangrui Yu,Baosheng Zhang,Liang Kong,Lin Ding,Yi Chen,Ye Yu,Mingzhe Zhang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 ROSETTA，一个 CKKS/TFHE 混合同态加密框架，通过自适应分段查找表和算子分配策略，加速大模型私有推理中的非线性运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全同态加密（FHE）的大模型私有推理框架在评估非线性运算时效率低下，非线性运算开销巨大并主导了解码阶段，成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 观察到解码阶段非线性运算具有异构工作负载模式，采用混合 CKKS/TFHE 方法应对，具体包括两点贡献：1）基于 TFHE 的自适应分段查找表协议，实现高效且精确的非线性运算评估；2）方案感知的算子选择框架，自动将每个非线性算子分配到 CKKS 或 TFHE，以最小化端到端解码延迟。

Result: 相比 SOTA 框架 CacheMir，ROSETTA 实现最高 4.8 倍的 Softmax 加速，以及 1.5–2.1 倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: ROSETTA 通过混合 CKKS/TFHE 框架有效缓解了 FHE 私有推理中非线性运算的开销瓶颈，显著提升了解码效率。

Abstract: Generative large language models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance on many real-world tasks such as code generation and question answering. These models predominantly rely on an autoregressive decoding strategy that generates output tokens sequentially. However, their pervasive deployment raises serious privacy concerns, motivating private inference frameworks based on fully homomorphic encryption (FHE). A major limitation of existing FHE frameworks is their inefficiency in evaluating nonlinear operations, which incur substantial overhead and dominate the decode stage.
  In this paper, we propose ROSETTA, a hybrid CKKS/TFHE framework that overcomes this limitation. We first observe that nonlinear operations in the decode stage exhibit heterogeneous workload patterns, which can be handled effectively via a hybrid approach. We then realize this with two key contributions: 1) an adaptive segmented lookup-table protocol based on TFHE that enables efficient and accurate evaluation of nonlinear operations; and 2) a scheme-aware operator-selection framework that automatically assigns each nonlinear operator to CKKS or TFHE to minimize end-to-end decoding latency. We demonstrate that ROSETTA achieves up to $4.8\times$ Softmax speedup and $1.5$--$2.1\times$ end-to-end speedup over the SOTA framework CacheMir.

</details>


### [297] [Cybersecurity in Power Grids: Standards and Research Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16928)
*Ferran Bohigas-Daranas,Hamid Latif-Martinez,Nicolas Llorens,David Bru i Bru,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo,Pere Barlet-Ros*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述智能电网网络安全，重点辨析IT与OT环境差异，分析电网架构、变电站威胁及IEC 62351、IEC 62443、ISO 27001等国际标准，并概述以AI驱动威胁检测为代表的最新研究趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网融合信息技术与运营技术，其安全需求与传统IT系统存在本质区别，亟需厘清相关威胁、标准与研究动向。

Method: 采用综述性分析方法：辨析IT与OT环境的关键差异，剖析电网架构与变电站面临的威胁，梳理IEC 62351、IEC 62443、ISO 27001等国际标准。

Result: 明确了IT与OT环境在智能电网安全中的关键差异，归纳了电网架构与变电站的主要威胁类型，并整理了相关国际标准的内容与适用范围。

Conclusion: 智能电网网络安全需兼顾IT与OT双重特性，依托国际标准框架，并结合AI驱动威胁检测等新兴技术提升防护能力。

Abstract: This paper examines Smart Grid cybersecurity, emphasizing the critical distinctions between IT and OT environments. It analyzes grid architecture, substation threats, and key international standards, specifically IEC 62351, IEC 62443, and ISO 27001. Finally, it overviews latest research trends, including AI-driven threat detection.

</details>


### [298] [Observational Indistinguishability and Integrity Blind Regions in Hybrid Quantum-Classical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17150)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"论断相对"的证据/参照框架，用于混合量子-经典工作流的完整性分析，区分观测不可分辨导致的结构盲区与有限批次统计漏检，并通过标签干预实验量化结论完整性与统计响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典工作流的完整性验证面临观测不可分辨性问题，需要区分结构性的"盲区"与有限批次带来的统计性漏检。作者希望建立一个论断相对的框架，明确不同完整性目标（结论完整性、聚合完整性、条目身份）分别需要何种证据（同批次标量R_0、聚合量M_0、条目对齐绑定）。

Method: 构建"论断相对"证据/参照框架，在声明的格（lattice）内定义同批次标量R_0、聚合量M_0与条目对齐绑定三类证据。开展3,600次标签干预实验，比较特征/预测视图与几何对齐聚合盲行；在统计响应层面使用保形（conformal）规则与未校正并集进行对比。另设聚类保持自适应压力测试（Gate A）与165设计单元的有限采样仿真分支。

Result: 特征/预测视图实现精确的标签路径不变性；764条几何对齐聚合盲行均等于配对干净响应，攻击增量增量为零。统计响应上，几何对齐构造用保形规则检出343/2,700结论改变干预，未校正并集检出1,183/2,700；原始冻结同项几何仅11/2,617和43/2,617。保形干净误动作率描述性为0.048–0.059，其有限样本保证要求可交换性，但重叠抽样设计违反该假设。Gate A在40个环境/拆分单元中的25–40个降低响应，同时保留结论改变。165设计单元理想态矢量与有限次仿真分支直接实例化语义、估计与观测核变换。

Conclusion: 该框架为混合量子-经典工作流完整性提供了论断相对的证据分层，能区分结构盲区与统计漏检。固定等权设计无法估计部署流行度，也无法为QPU、供应商或已部署服务提供保证；因此结论仅限定于所声明的格与实验条件内。

Abstract: We present a claim-relative evidence/reference framework for hybrid quantum-classical workflow integrity. Observational indistinguishability yields structural blind regions, distinct from finite-batch statistical misses. Within the declared lattice, a trusted same-batch scalar $R_0$ suffices for conclusion integrity, aggregate $M_0$ for aggregate plus conclusion integrity, and item-aligned binding for item identity. In 3,600 label interventions, feature/prediction views realize exact label-path invariance; all 764 geometry-aligned aggregate-blind rows equal their paired-clean responses, giving zero attack-only increment. For statistical response, the geometry-aligned construction detects 343/2,700 conclusion-changing ($τ\to 0^+$) label interventions with the conformal rule and 1,183/2,700 with the uncorrected union; the original frozen same-item geometry yields 11/2,617 and 43/2,617, respectively. The executed conformal clean false-action rates are 0.048--0.059 descriptively; its finite-sample guarantee requires exchangeability, which the overlapping-draw design violates. The cluster-preserving adaptive stress test (Gate A) reduces response versus matched controls in 25--40 of 40 environment/split cells while retaining conclusion changes. A bounded 165-design-cell ideal-statevector and finite-shot-emulation branch directly instantiates semantic, estimated and observed kernel transitions. The fixed equal-weight design estimates neither deployment prevalence nor QPU, provider or deployed-service assurance.

</details>


### [299] [Plug 'n' Pray: Agentic LLM-based Detection of Potential Log File Exposures in Third-Party Content Management System Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17164)
*Sebastian Neef*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的智能体框架，自动检测最流行CMS（WordPress）插件中的日志文件暴露漏洞，并对300个最常安装的插件进行静态与动态分析，手动复现了79个发现，总结出日志路径与防护模式的分类体系及开发者最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS插件常为调试等目的生成日志文件，若保护不足会泄露凭证、个人数据等敏感信息，且已导致网站被入侵。然而这一高影响弱点研究不足，因此需要自动化的检测手段。

Method: 提出智能体、基于LLM的框架，对每个WordPress插件同时进行静态分析和动态分析，以自动发现潜在的日志文件暴露问题。

Result: 在安装量最高的300个WordPress插件（占活跃安装量75%，超2.5亿次）上评估，手动验证并复现了来自62个插件的81个发现中的79个。发现存在创建控制（如手动激活日志）和访问控制（如.htaccess拒绝规则）等防护措施，但多层防护并非总是具备。

Conclusion: 研究得出日志文件路径与防护模式的分类体系，并提出开发者安全处理日志的最佳实践；同时证实智能体式LLM是安全分析的有用工具。

Abstract: Content Management Systems (CMS), such as WordPress, power a large share of the web (~58%), and their extensibility through third-party plugins is a major source of their popularity as well as of their attack surface. One high-impact weakness that remains understudied is log file exposure by CMS plugins, which create log files for debugging or other purposes. If these files are insufficiently secured, they can disclose sensitive information (e.g. credentials, personal data) which has led to website compromises in the past.
  In this work, we present an agentic, LLM-based framework that automatically detects potential log file exposures in plugins of the most popular CMS (WordPress). Our agent analyzes each plugin by performing static and dynamic analysis.
  We evaluated our approach on the 300 most-installed WordPress plugins (about 0.6% of all), which together account for over 250M active installations, i.e. 75% of all active installations in the official plugin ecosystem. We manually validated each finding, reproducing 79 of 81 findings from 62 plugins. We observed that several protective measures appear to be implemented that we classify as creation-control (e.g. manual log activation) and access-control (e.g. deny rules in .htaccess). However, we find that multi-layered protection is required, but not always present.
  From these results we derive a taxonomy of log file path and protection patterns and deduce a set of best practices for developers to securely handle them. Finally, our study corroborates that agentic LLMs are an useful tool for security analysis.

</details>


### [300] [Cross-Domain Inference for Human Localization: Applying Wi-Fi RSSI Data to CSI-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17204)
*Ariel Duschanek-Myers,Thomas Welsh,Helmut Neukirchen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用RSSI数据替代CSI数据进行人体位置预测的可行性，通过将RSSI数据输入已有的基于CSI的模型进行跨域推理，发现当人体移动时定位置信度约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi信号可用于侵犯个人隐私，但现有方法多依赖CSI数据，而CSI在典型IoT设备上采集需要较高的系统权限和专用驱动。RSSI在低权限设备上也可获取，适用范围更广，因此作者希望探究基于RSSI预测人体位置的可能性。

Method: 为省去训练RSSI模型所需的繁琐数据采集过程，作者借用了一个已有的Wi-Fi姿态预测项目。该项目原本假设输入为CSI数据，因此研究跨域推理的可行性，即将RSSI数据输入该CSI模型。作者采集了RSSI数据集，并与室内人员移动的视频真值同步，以评估模型性能。

Result: 评估结果证实，当存在人体移动时，RSSI数据能以约80%的置信度预测位置。这表明，在CSI数据上训练的模型可用于评估由dBm值组成的低粒度RSSI数据，从而粗略定位采集空间内的人员。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在Wi-Fi密集环境中，大量IoT设备都可能被滥用于隐私侵犯。这提示需关注低权限设备上RSSI数据的隐私风险。

Abstract: Wi-Fi signal data can be used to compromise the privacy of individuals. While many existing approaches rely on Channel State Information (CSI), collecting this data on typical IoT devices often requires elevated operating system permissions and specialized drivers. Consequently, this paper investigates the feasibility of utilizing Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) data to predict human locations. RSSI was selected because it is accessible even on devices with limited user permissions, and therefore is more applicable to a wider array of IoT devices. To bypass the tedious process of obtaining training data needed to train an RSSI-based model, an existing Wi-Fi pose prediction project was used in this research. However, that project assumed CSI data as input. Therefore, we investigate the feasibility of cross-domain inference, i.e., feeding RSSI data into that existing CSI-based model. We collected an RSSI dataset, synchronized with video ground-truth of a person moving within a room, to evaluate the model's performance. This evaluation confirmed that RSSI data can predict locations with approximately 80% confidence when human movement is present. This demonstrates that a model trained on CSI data can be used to evaluate low-granularity RSSI data consisting of decibel-milliwatt (dBm) values to roughly locate people in the collection space. These results imply that a wide range of IoT devices can be used for privacy invasion in Wi-Fi-dense environments.

</details>


### [301] [SEMA-GUARD: Semantic and Graph-Based Vulnerability Detection in Assembly Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17254)
*Halil Dursunoglu,Kaan Sulkalar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SEMA-GUARD框架，结合语义分析与图神经网络检测汇编代码中的漏洞，在Juliet测试集上达到85.1%准确率和0.801的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶意软件分析、固件分析和嵌入式系统分析等源代码不可用的场景中，编译程序的漏洞检测变得愈发重要。然而，现有方法严重依赖语法规律或高层表示，容易受编译器变化影响，且难以直接应用于汇编代码。

Method: 提出SEMA-GUARD框架，采用语义分析与图神经网络。通过对控制流图进行增强表示，加入了更低抽象层次的程序执行信息，包括栈操作、内存访问和数据流。评估数据基于Juliet测试集，将每段源代码先翻译为汇编语言，再切分为函数级代码块。

Result: 仅依赖统计或结构信息的SEMA-GUARD方法取得85.1%的准确率和0.801的F1分数。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将语义信息纳入基于图的模型可能是识别编译代码漏洞的有效方法。

Abstract: In cases where source code is not available, such as malware analysis, firmware analysis, and embedded systems analysis, vulnerability detection in compiled programs has gained importance. Current methods are heavily reliant on syntactical regularities or higher level representations that are vulnerable to changes in the compiler and may not be readily applicable to assembly code.In this article, we present SEMA-GUARD, a framework that uses semantic analysis and graph neural networks to identify flaws in assembly code. The approach improves the representation of control flow graphs by adding information about the program's execution at a lower level of abstraction, including stack manipulations, memory accesses, and data flow. A set based on the Juliet Test Suite was used to evaluate the effectiveness of SEMA-GUARD. In this set, each piece of source code is initially translated into assembly language and then broken down into function-level chunks. The suggested method, which relies only on statistical or structural data, achieves an accuracy of 85.1\% and an F1 score of 0.801, according to the results. Such results imply that including semantic information in graph-based models may be a successful method for identifying vulnerabilities in compiled code.

</details>


### [302] [GAUGE: A Formal Framework for Measuring Cryptographic Security under Heterogeneous Adversary Cost Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17281)
*Bhanwar Gupta,Sanjeev Rana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GAUGE将密码方案的安全性表示为成本模型上的函数（安全画像），形式化价格泛函，证明评级三难困境，并给出多项式时间LP方法判定排名是否稳健、反转或不可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机构以单一比特数报告密码安全性，但该值依赖于对时间、内存和量子资源定价的敌手成本模型；不同约定会产生不同的方案排名。因此需要一个显式、可审计的框架来刻画成本模型依赖下的安全性比较。

Method: 在敌手成本模型的锥上形式化价格泛函；证明安全画像为分段线性且凹；给出关于评级三难困境的证明；设计多项式时间线性规划方法以认证两方案排名的稳健性、反转性或不可比性；并扩展出结合随机密码分析衰减与成本模型不确定性的双层风险度量。

Result: 在NIST后量子标准、经典锚点及25年密码分析突破编年史上评估：认证了ML-KEM-512与AES-128因内存定价4-5%偏移而发生的排名反转；测得八年间格筛成本漂移为每年9.79比特；混合X25519+ML-KEM-768握手以2.3千字节代价将联合破解概率降低二十倍；工件在7秒内复现全部表格与图。

Conclusion: GAUGE为在竞争性成本模型下报告密码安全性提供了显式且可审计的框架。

Abstract: Standards bodies report cryptographic security as a single number of bits, but this value depends on the adversary cost model used to price time, memory, and quantum resources. Different conventions can therefore produce different rankings of cryptographic schemes. GAUGE represents security as a function over admissible cost models, called a security profile. Comparisons then become comparisons between profiles, and ranking reversals become an explicit structural property rather than a measurement error.
  We formalize price functionals over a cone of adversary cost models, show that security profiles are piecewise-linear and concave, and prove a rating trilemma: when two profiles cross, no rating can simultaneously be faithful to underlying costs, total over comparable pairs, and independent of the chosen cost model. We provide a polynomial-time linear-programming procedure that certifies whether the ranking of two schemes is robust, reverses under admissible models, or is genuinely incomparable.
  We extend GAUGE with a two-layer risk measure combining stochastic cryptanalytic decay with uncertainty over the appropriate cost model. We evaluate the framework on NIST post-quantum standards, classical anchors, and a 25-year chronology of cryptanalytic breaks. The analysis certifies a ranking reversal for ML-KEM-512 versus AES-128 from a 4-5% shift in memory pricing, and measures a lattice-sieving cost drift of 9.79 bits per year over eight years. A hybrid X25519 + ML-KEM-768 handshake reduces combined-break probability twenty-fold at a 2.3 kilobyte cost. The artifact reproduces all tables and figures in under seven seconds. GAUGE provides an explicit and auditable framework for reporting cryptographic security under competing cost models.

</details>


### [303] [Can We Stop The Ads? Taxonomy and Characterization of Smartphone Splash Ads and Existing Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17316)
*Shuhao Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Ziyu Shao,Yuqing Yang,Yan Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了108种广告防御实现方案在开屏广告场景下的适用性，发现普遍存在部署门槛，评测11款工具13种配置后仅1款能在10个应用中完全阻止广告跳转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开屏广告作为App启动时的全屏广告，常通过隐蔽的触发机制（如摇一摇）诱导用户跳转至第三方页面，已造成延误紧急响应、分散驾驶员注意力、损害视障用户可访问性等现实危害，因此需要系统评估现有广告防御手段能否应对开屏广告。

Method: 梳理108种有文献记录的广告防御实现，分析其用于开屏广告的适用性及用户部署所需条件；并选取11款工具、13种配置，在10款流行应用中开展实测评估。

Result: 现有防御方案普遍存在显著部署障碍，如需root/越狱、运行时代码注入、修改应用等；无此要求的方案也往往涉及额外权限、规则维护、源码编译或付费。实测中仅1款工具在全部10款应用内阻止了目标广告跳转，且需无障碍权限、广告仍会显示约1秒；其他配置或无法阻止跳转，或导致宿主应用无法启动、卡在广告页。

Conclusion: 现有广告防御工具难以有效且低门槛地应对开屏广告，作者进一步分析未解挑战与未来方向，并呼吁推动智能手机厂商提供更友好、更规范的平台支持。

Abstract: Splash ads are full-screen advertisements that pop up and appear as the first interaction page when users start an app, often tricking users into unknowingly activating certain trigger mechanisms, such as moving the phone to redirect users to other profit-driven third parties. So far, splash ads have already caused significant real-world impacts, ranging from significantly delaying emergency response to distracting drivers, as well as degrading accessibility of apps to vision-impaired users. We analyze 108 documented implementations of advertising defenses to examine their applicability to splash ads and the requirements users face when deploying them.
  Our analysis identifies substantial deployment barriers, including device rooting or jailbreaking, runtime code injection, and application modification. Options without these requirements can still involve additional permissions, rule maintenance, source compilation, or payment. In our evaluation of 13 configurations of 11 tools across 10 popular apps, only one tool prevented the target ad-triggered navigation across all ten apps. It required Accessibility permission, and ads remained visible for approximately one second before dismissal. Other tested configurations failed to prevent navigation or, in some cases, left host apps unable to launch or stuck on the ad page. We further analyze the outstanding challenges and pos- sible future directions, highlighting the urgent need to incentivize smartphone manufacturers to provide more friendly and regulated platforms.

</details>


### [304] [RobResilience: Implementing and Evaluating a Resilience Framework for Cyber-Physical Embodied Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17349)
*Gysella Imrell,Emanuele Miotto,Mahya Mohammadi Kashani,Mauro Conti,Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RobResilience，在Webots仿真环境中基于PR2机器人与ROS2实现面向信息物理系统的形式化韧性框架，通过运行时的三个谓词判断系统是否仍处于可容忍状态并触发缓解策略，八个攻击场景验证了实现行为与理论定义一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身信息物理系统面临主动网络攻击，威胁不仅是数据，还包括物理完整性与人类安全。现有安全方法擅长检测，却缺乏运行时机制来判断扰动是否可容忍、性能退化是否仍在安全运行范围内，导致系统在攻击持续时陷入"优雅失效瘫痪"，无法区分安全的降级状态与灾难性危害。

Method: 提出RobResilience，将形式化韧性框架落地到Webots仿真环境中，使用PR2机器人与ROS2。框架在运行时基于入侵检测系统（IDS）置信度分数导出受损设备集合，并评估三个谓词：可容忍扰动（δ）、可容忍退化（γ）与缓解可行性（μ）。当韧性丧失时，框架触发可用的缓解策略。

Result: 通过八个攻击场景进行评估，这些场景系统性地覆盖了谓词状态空间的所有可能组合，并变化了攻击目标、退化速率与缓解措施可用性。结果确认该实现的运行时行为与理论定义保持一致。

Conclusion: RobResilience在仿真环境中验证了形式化韧性框架的运行时可行性，表明其在攻击进行时能够依据谓词判断区分可容忍降级与灾难性危害，并支持缓解策略的触发，为具身信息物理系统的运行时韧性保障提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In embodied cyber-physical systems, active cyberattacks pose an immediate threat not just to data, but to physical integrity and human safety. While existing security approaches excel at detection, they lack the runtime mechanisms to determine whether a disruption is tolerable or if performance degradation remains within safe operational bounds. This gap leaves autonomous systems vulnerable to graceful failure paralysis, where they cannot distinguish between a safe, degraded state and a catastrophic hazard during an ongoing attack. This paper presents RobResilience, an implementation of a formal resilience framework for embodied cyber-physical systems in a Webots simulation environment, using a PR2 robot and ROS2. The framework evaluates three predicates at runtime: tolerable disruption ($δ$), tolerable degradation ($γ$), and mitigation feasibility ($μ$), over a compromised device set derived from IDS confidence scores. When resilience is lost, the framework triggers available mitigation strategies. We evaluate our implementation through eight attack scenarios that systematically cover all possible combinations of the predicate state space, varying attack targets, degradation rates, and mitigation availability. Results confirm that the runtime behaviour of the implementation is consistent with the theoretical definitions.

</details>


### [305] [Closing the Loop: Bidirectional Fully Encrypted Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17397)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出双向全加密协议（BiFEP）的形式化安全定义，指出两条单向通道的简单组合会被检测，并给出数据流与数据报两种场景下可证明安全的构造，通过Rust实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全加密协议（FEP）使协议产生的所有字节与均匀随机串不可区分。现有工作多研究单向FEP（一方仅发送、另一方仅接收），但多数应用需要双向通信。网络对手可同时观测两个方向的通信及其共享生命周期，因此简单组合两条单向通道可能因方向间依赖（如流量不平衡、通道关闭、故障、连接拆除）而被检测。

Method: 作者为双向FEP引入新的形式化安全定义，涵盖精确整形、交付、协议状态完整性、私有半关闭与跨方向隔离，同时仅暴露可随机化的公开"发送调度"和"关闭纪元"。针对数据流场景，将两条方向分离的FEP与"包装"层结合，防止因单向与双向连接状态不匹配而被检测；针对数据报场景，增加加密的DATA/FIN/ACK及重放保护和容忍丢包的关闭机制。并通过Rust实现进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，简单组合无法满足所定义的安全性质并会导致实际检测攻击；所提出的构造可证明安全。Rust实现验证了设计，且调研的已部署协议均不能提供完整的BiFEP安全属性。

Conclusion: 双向全加密协议需要专门的安全定义与构造，简单组合存在可检测性缺陷；本文提出的BiFEP构造在数据流和数据报场景下均可证明安全，并指出了现有部署协议的不足。

Abstract: Fully encrypted protocols (FEPs) provide encrypted channels that make all protocol-generated bytes computationally indistinguishable from uniform random strings. Several previous works have explored security definitions and constructions of unidirectional FEPs: protocols in which one party acts only as a sender, and the other acts only as a receiver. However, most applications require two-way information exchange, and a network adversary can observe communication in both directions and their shared lifetime. Because the semantics of bidirectional channels involve more complex shared state, it is possible that the ``naïve'' composition of two unidirectional channels can result in a two-way protocol that can be detected based on dependencies between the two directions, such as traffic imbalance, channel closure, failures, or connection tear-down.
  To address this issue, we introduce new formal security definitions for bidirectional FEPs that capture exact shaping, delivery, protocol-state integrity, private half-close, and cross-direction isolation, while revealing a public ``sending schedule'' and ``closing epoch'' that may be randomized. We show that the trivial composition fails to meet these definitions, leading to practical detection attacks. We then construct provably secure bidirectional FEPs (BiFEPs) for both the datastream and datagram settings. For datastream, we combine two direction-separated FEPs with a ``wrapper'' layer that prevents detection based on the mismatch between uni- and bi-directional connection states. For datagram, we add encrypted DATA/FIN/ACK with replay protection and loss-tolerant close. We validate the design through a Rust implementation and show that none of the surveyed deployed protocols provides the full set of BiFEP security properties.

</details>


### [306] [SCHERI: Provably Secure Speculation Under the Constant-Time Policy for CHERI (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17399)
*Shixin Song,Davide Davoli,Elias Storme,Marton Bognar,Dominique Devriese,Frank Piessens,Tamara Rezk*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于能力（capability）架构（如CHERI）结合安全推测执行的问题，指出现有方案无法保持机密性保证，提出形式化框架并设计SCHERI处理器，形式化证明其能为常量时间程序提供端到端的安全推测保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHERI等基于能力的架构为软件组件的架构隔离提供了强支持，而为进一步防御微架构泄漏，软件可采用常量时间（constant-time）写法。但现代处理器高度依赖推测执行，可能破坏常量时间保证并瞬态泄漏被隔离的秘密。因此，需要为CHERI提供安全推测，然而现有提案未能保持机密性保证，这一缺口促成了本文的研究。

Method: 作者首先论证为CHERI提供安全推测并非易事。为此，他们构建了一个形式化框架，用于联合推理能力安全（capability safety）、推测执行与信息流安全，并利用该框架揭示潜在的泄漏。随后在该框架内提出新的处理器设计SCHERI。

Result: 通过形式化框架展示了现有方案中存在的潜在泄漏；对SCHERI进行形式化证明，表明其能为常量时间策略提供端到端的安全推测保证。

Conclusion: 本文为构建未来基于能力的处理器提供了形式化基础与实践指导，使此类处理器对常量时间程序具备抵御Spectre攻击的能力。

Abstract: Capability-based architectures such as CHERI provide strong support for the architectural isolation of software components. To additionally protect against microarchitectural leakage, software can be written in a constant-time fashion. Modern processors, however, rely heavily on speculative execution, which can invalidate the constant-time guarantees and leak isolated secrets transiently.
  In this work, we show that providing secure speculation for CHERI is non-trivial, and that existing proposals fail to preserve the confidentiality guarantees. We develop a formal framework for reasoning jointly about capability safety, speculative execution, and information-flow security, and use it to demonstrate potential leaks. We then present SCHERI, a new processor design within this framework, and formally prove that it provides end-to-end secure speculation guarantees for the constant-time policy.
  Our results provide formal foundations and practical guidance for building future capability-based processors, which are resilient to Spectre attacks for constant-time programs.

</details>


### [307] [You Shall Not Pass into Ring-0! A User Privacy-Friendly Anti-Cheat Architecture for Personal Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17525)
*Santosh Gokul Narayanan,Giovanni Paladino,Chuqi Zhang,Sangho Lee,Zhenkai Liang,Adil Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Tirith是一种反作弊架构，通过在受保护虚拟机中运行游戏并借助虚拟化监控器，在不牺牲检测能力与性能的前提下，消除对不可信内核级反作弊的依赖，从而保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内核级反作弊在竞技游戏中有效，但需要在特权模式（x86的ring-0）安装不可验证组件，带来严重用户隐私顾虑。现有研究多关注反作弊有效性，而用户隐私问题被忽视。本文旨在让用户隐私成为个人计算机的一等公民。

Method: Tirith采用两个核心思路：1）不在玩家（作为root管理员）可控的常规进程中运行游戏，而是在受保护虚拟机（PVM）中执行，天然隔离不可信管理员；2）利用玩家与开发者都信任的虚拟化监控器来监控沙箱外的用户行为（如是否运行恶意驱动）。同时针对现有虚拟机软件栈不适合运行游戏的问题，提出面向安全游戏的Library OS内核和高效图形共享管线。

Result: Tirith消除了运行不受信任内核级反作弊的需求，同时针对广泛常见作弊机制提供与这类方案同等的保护水平，并实现接近原生的渲染与显示性能，在作弊行为检测与性能上均无妥协。

Conclusion: 该工作在不牺牲作弊行为检测或性能的情况下，将用户隐私作为个人计算机中的一等公民，为反作弊与用户隐私的平衡提供了可行架构。

Abstract: Kernel-level anti-cheats are effective against malicious player behavior in competitive video games, but raise significant user privacy concerns regarding installing unverifiable components at privileged modes (i.e., ring-0 in x86). While existing research has focused on improving the effectiveness of anti-cheats, the user privacy concern has been largely ignored. Tirith is an anti-cheat architecture that addresses this problem using two key ideas. First, instead of running video games within regular processes that players (as root admins) have control over, Tirith executes video games in Protected Virtual Machines that naturally sandbox computations from untrusted admins. Second, to monitor user behavior outside the sandbox (e.g., see if they are running malicious drivers), Tirith leverages a virtualization monitor that is trusted by both players and developers. Together, these ideas remove the need to run untrusted kernel-level anti-cheats, while providing the same level of protection compared to such solutions against a wide-range of common cheating mechanisms. The main challenge we face in implementing these ideas, however, is that the existing software stack for virtual machines is not designed to run video games and creates significant security and performance problems. We address these problems by proposing a security-focused Library OS kernel for games and an efficient graphics sharing pipeline for near-native rendering and display performance. In summary, without compromising on cheating behavior detection or performance, this work makes user privacy a first-class citizen in personal computers.

</details>
